Motivation: 现有基准无法充分评估模型在异构知识上下文中执行多跳、链式推理的忠实性与对输入顺序变动的鲁棒性，因而需要一个专门的事实一致性基准与相应的改进方法。

Method: 构建了TKFQA数据集（10,130个QA对），每个样本由表格、文本和知识图谱中的显式反事实推理链构成；评估指标包含答案正确性（Exact Match）、推理链准确性（Reasoning-Chain Accuracy）和对输入顺序的鲁棒性；在14个开源与闭源大模型上进行大规模评测；提出了ORLF训练框架，其通过知识特定的潜向量建模跨上下文拓扑关系，并引入上下文位置编码、latent-bridge注意力掩码与拓扑知识偏置来保持知识特定偏置并编码拓扑语义。

Result: 14个模型的广泛评估显示：SOTA模型在推理链准确性上表现有限，且对异构上下文输入顺序敏感；提出的ORLF在四个不同LLM骨干上优于若干训练免费和LoRA基线，平均Exact Match提升2.15%，Reasoning-Chain Accuracy提升4.29%，并将因输入顺序导致的性能标准差降低0.04%至3.01%。

Conclusion: TKFQA填补了对异构知识来源下链式推理与顺序鲁棒性评估的空白；ORLF通过显式建模跨上下文拓扑关系，在提升推理链忠实性和降低顺序敏感性方面取得可重复的改进，尽管提升幅度有限且需要更多关于可解释性、效率与广泛适配性的分析。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly supported response generation grounded in user-provided knowledge spanning heterogeneous structures. However, existing benchmarks provide limited assessment of whether LLMs can faithfully perform multi-hop reasoning chains across such knowledge contexts while remaining robust to variations in their input order. We introduce TKFQA, a factuality consistency benchmark comprising 10,130 question-answering (QA) pairs grounded in tables, texts, and knowledge graphs (KGs). Each example is constructed from an explicit counterfactual reasoning chain, enabling the joint evaluation of answer correctness, reasoning-chain accuracy, and robustness to different input-order. An extensive evaluation of 14 open- and closed-source LLMs reveals that state-of-the-art models exhibit limited reasoning-chain accuracy and remain sensitive to variations in the input order of heterogeneous knowledge contexts. To address these limitations, we propose ORLF, an LLM-agnostic training framework that models cross-context topological relations through knowledge-specific latent vectors. ORLF integrates context-wise position encoding, a latent-bridge attention mask, and topological knowledge bias to preserve knowledge-specific bias and encode topological semantics. Experiments across four LLM backbones show that ORLF outperforms competitive training-free and LoRA-based baselines, improving average Exact Match and Reasoning-Chain Accuracy by 2.15% and 4.29%, respectively, while reducing order-induced performance standard deviation by 0.04% to 3.01%.

</details>


### [64] [Improving Constraint Models with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08127)
*Florentina Voboril,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者设计了一个LLM驱动的代理，通过生成、验证、诊断和修复CP模型改写，在实测问题上显著改进求解性能，展示了自动化代理可用于优化约束建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CP求解器的运行时间对建模选择高度敏感，而现有自动改写系统依赖于预定义的手工规则库；需要一种能在开放式变换空间中、以经验方式保证正确性的自动化方法，以降低人工建模负担并发现更高效的模型。

Method: 提出一种基于大型语言模型（LLM）的代理框架，从开放式搜索空间生成约束规划（CP）模型变体；代理以输入模型和三个位训练实例为条件，提出候选改写，用CPMpy表示，并在三个更大的测试实例上评估；对每个候选，通过将候选解注入回原始模型来验证正确性，并在失败时进行诊断和修复，迭代改进，最终在中位约十五分钟内返回最佳变体。

Result: 在九个组合优化问题上，对比原始模型，在27个测试实例中有21个被改进，部分问题上的运行时间提升超过两个数量级；与非代理基线比较，表明性能提升源自代理的迭代诊断与修复能力，而非仅仅多样化候选采样。

Conclusion: 代理式、自主的方法能够在无需手工规则的情况下从开放式空间生成并经验性验证更高效的约束模型，证明了该方向在自动化建模和性能优化上的可行性和潜力。

Abstract: The runtime of Constraint Programming (CP) solvers is highly sensitive to modeling choices, such as symmetry breaking, implied constraints, global constraints, constraint reformulation, and variable representation. Improving these constraint models has traditionally required human expertise, and existing automated reformulation systems are restricted to a predefined library of hand-crafted transformation rules. We introduce an agentic framework that instead reformulates a constraint model from an open-ended space and establishes correctness empirically rather than by construction: a Large Language Model (LLM) agent, given a model and three training instances, proposes alternative formulations, validates each by injecting its solution back into the original model, and diagnoses and repairs failures, returning the best variant it finds in a median of about fifteen minutes. The models are expressed in the CPMpy modeling library, and each proposed model is evaluated on three larger test instances. Across nine combinatorial optimization problems, the generated models outperform the originals on 21 of 27 test instances, and on some problems solve more than two orders of magnitude faster. A comparison against non-agentic baselines that reuse the same validation and selection tools indicates that the gains stem from the agent's iterative diagnosis and repair, not merely from sampling several candidates. These results demonstrate that autonomous agentic methods can support the improvement of constraint models.

</details>


### [65] [Back to the Future: A workbook time machine for spread sheet creation benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07873)
*Mansi Uniyal,Agamdeep Singh,Ananya Singha,Priyanshu Gupta,Mukul Singh,Gust Verbruggen,Vu Le,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduces an automatic pipeline to create benchmarks for LLM-driven spreadsheet artifact generation, releases wtmcorpus and a 150-task wtmbench, and demonstrates that query specificity, agent orchestration, and API choice substantially affect LLM performance on Excel tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: There is a need for systematic, scalable benchmarks that measure LLMs’ abilities to create nontrivial, structured spreadsheet artifacts (not just cell values) under realistic instruction ambiguity and varied complexity. Existing evaluations focus on cell-level edits or synthetic tasks and do not capture multi-step artifact generation or the effects of instruction specificity and control interfaces.

Method: We build an automatic pipeline ("workbook time machine") that converts public spreadsheet corpora into benchmark triples: (input workbook, output workbook, query). The pipeline identifies four derived-artifact types—formulas, charts, pivot tables, conditional formatting—generates target workbooks reflecting the desired artifact, and synthesizes queries at three specificity levels. From the generated corpus (wtmcorpus) we curate wtmbench, a 150-task evaluation suite. We run evaluations of existing spreadsheet-manipulation agents and baselines across artifact types, step complexity, and instruction granularity, comparing orchestration strategies and differing spreadsheet control APIs.

Result: We produce wtmcorpus and a curated 150-task wtmbench spanning four artifact types and three query-specificity levels. Empirical results show LLM performance depends strongly on query specificity, the way agents are orchestrated (single-step LLM vs multi-agent/tool chains), and the spreadsheet API used to effect changes. Performance varies by artifact type and step complexity.

Conclusion: wtmcorpus/wtmbench provide a realistic testbed for spreadsheet artifact generation; meaningful evaluation should vary instruction specificity, measure multi-step reasoning, and consider the spreadsheet control interface. Future work should expand task diversity, assess privacy/licensing of source workbooks, and investigate orchestration and API design to improve reliability.

Abstract: We introduce the workbook time machine, a pipeline that automatically creates benchmarks evaluating the ability of language models to create derived objects in spreadsheets (formulas, charts, pivot tables, and conditional formatting). Applied to public workbook corpora, it produces wtmcorpus--a collection of (input workbook, output workbook, query) triples spanning four artifact types and varying complexity. From this corpus we curate wtmbench, a 150-task evaluation benchmark with queries at three levels of specificity. We evaluate existing spreadsheet manipulation agents and baselines on wtmbench across artifact types, step complexity, and instruction granularity. Our evaluations show that query specificity, agent orchestration, and interface API used to control spreadsheets play a big role in LLM performance on Excel tasks.

</details>


### [66] [AI Evaluation Should Measure Verification Cost, Not Correctness Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08709)
*Viviana Crescitelli,Generoso Immediato,Fabio Persia,Stefania Costantini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Verification-Cost Errors (VCEs)这一操作性概念：错误的输入-输出对在给定部署的验证预算下，有一定比例的验证者无法在预算内识别，从而使“正确率”无法反映实际可靠性。作者主张把“相对于部署预算的验证成本”纳入评估维度，并用代码生成和多模态文档理解的实证结果说明高基准准确率可能掩盖高验证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常用的评估指标只关注输出正确性，但实际可靠性还依赖于验证这些输出所需的时间与努力（比如专家人工审查成本）。部分模型输出因表现得可信或权威而难以在有限预算内被发现为错误，形成部署风险。

Method: 提出操作性定义：VCE——在验证预算下被某一声明比例的验证者未能识别的错误输入-输出对；引入“相对于部署预算的验证成本”作为评估维度。通过实验（代码生成与多模态文档理解任务）测量在现实化验证预算下的检测率与传统基准准确率的差异，展示高准确率下的高验证成本现象。

Result: 实证显示：在两个应用场景中，尽管基准准确率较高，但在现实验证预算限制下大量错误难以被发现（即存在明显的VCEs）。论文表明需要在评估中加入验证成本维度，但未提供最终化的通用度量指标。

Conclusion: 仅以正确性作为可靠性度量是不够的。评估应显式考虑在现实资源约束下错误能否被检测到（验证成本），以降低未被发现的错误对部署风险的影响。文章提出了概念框架并通过两个场景的证据支撑，但尚未给出最终化的、可直接使用的度量。

Abstract: The reliability of AI generative models is typically measured by output correctness, yet in practice it depends on the effort required to verify those outputs. We argue that current evaluation metrics overlook a critical failure mode: Verification-Cost Errors (VCEs), defined as incorrect input-output pairs that a declared fraction of the verifier population fails to identify within the verification budget available in a given deployment context. Unlike standard notions of "hallucination", VCEs are defined operationally, by the failure of correct identification within budget rather than by any property of the output itself. Plausibility and authoritative presentation are hypothesised contributors to that failure, not defining conditions. To capture this asymmetry, we introduce the notion of verification cost relative to a deployment budget as an operational dimension that current evaluation does not routinely capture. The quantity is presented as a conceptual instrument rather than a finalized metric. Evidence from code generation and multi-modal document understanding shows that high benchmark accuracy can mask significant verification effort in practice. We therefore take the position that correctness alone is insufficient as a measure of reliability. AI evaluation should explicitly account for verification cost, reflecting whether errors can be detected under realistic resource constraints.

</details>


### [67] [SurgLAT: Surgical Latent Attention Tracking for Depth-Aware Robotic Laparoscope Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07876)
*Rulin Zhou,Qiujie Song,Yujie Ma,An Wang,Wanhao Liu,Guoheng Ma,Yidu Wang,Guankun Wang,Xingrong Diao,Jiankun Wang,Chaowei Zhu,Xianming Liu,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一本体化的因果在线框架（SurgLAT），将记忆驱动的潜在注意力建模与RCM约束的机器人控制结合，实现对动态手术场景中随时间演化的操作区的鲁棒跟踪与自主内镜调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态手术场景中，目标操作区不是稳定的物理对象而是随时间演化的潜在注意力状态；为实现持续、鲁棒的自主腹腔镜控制，需要在线理解外科医生的操作意图并在存在遮挡、快速运动和目标切换时保持视野稳定。

Method: 提出SurgLAT——一个因果的在线框架，将手术潜在注意力状态建模并用于自主腹腔镜视野控制。方法包含：使用冻结的DINOv3编码器提取图像特征；基于状态的空间token混合器与记忆引导的空间先验来汇聚手术证据；一个选择性因果潜在记忆模块通过动态检索当前、近期和历史潜在状态来联合建模短期运动连续性与长期手术意图演化；将学习到的潜在注意力态解码为概率注意力热图与操作区以指导内镜运动；在机器人部署方面，提出基于虚轴(vitual-axis)的显式远心约束（RCM）控制以及冗余感知的零空间初始化以保证机械臂运动的平稳与稳定性。

Result: 在真实腹腔镜视频和物理机器人腹腔镜平台上完成验证。实验显示SurgLAT在遮挡、快速运动及目标转换情形下仍能实现鲁棒的在线操作区域跟踪与稳定的自主内镜调节，证明通过潜在手术意图建模能提升手术自主性的感知与控制性能。

Conclusion: SurgLAT通过联合潜在注意力建模与显式RCM受限控制，展示了在复杂外科场景下实现稳定自主内镜的可行性；该方法在感知—决策—控制的端到端闭环中具有实用价值，但仍需更多关于泛化能力、实时延迟、安全性与临床评估的补充实验来推进临床部署。

Abstract: Autonomous laparoscopic camera control requires continuous understanding of the surgeon's operative intent in dynamic surgical scenes, where the target operative region is not a stable physical object but a latent and temporally evolving attention state. In this work, we present Surgical Latent Attention Tracking (SurgLAT), a causal online framework for latent surgical attention modeling and autonomous laparoscopic view control. SurgLAT uses a frozen DINOv3 encoder and a state-conditioned spatial token mixer to extract operative evidence under a memory-guided spatial prior, while a selective causal latent memory module jointly models short-term motion continuity and long-horizon surgical intent evolution through dynamic retrieval of current, recent, and historical latent states. The learned latent surgical attention state is decoded into a probabilistic attention heatmap and operative region for downstream endoscope guidance. Beyond perception, we further introduce a robotic deployment framework with explicit laparoscopic Remote Center of Motion (RCM) constrained control based on virtual-axis formulation, together with redundancy-aware null-space initialization for stable and smooth manipulator motion. We validate the full system on real laparoscopic surgical videos and a physical robotic laparoscope platform. Experimental results demonstrate robust online operative-region tracking and stable autonomous endoscopy adjustment under occlusion, rapid motion, and target transitions, highlighting the effectiveness of latent surgical intent modeling for surgical autonomy.

</details>


### [68] [Entropy-based Code Adversarial Translation for Real-world Repository Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09273)
*Yushun Tang,Yisen Cao,Zhicheng Chen,Lin Peng,Junkang Mao,Fengyi Song,Yantao Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Proposes ECAT, an adversarial entropy-minimization multi-agent system with a Code Entropy metric and memory tree for Android→HarmonyOS repository migration; yields strong empirical gains on a new large-scale benchmark (74.7%).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs excel at local code tasks but struggle to maintain long-horizon, repository-level objectives when migrating large codebases (Android→HarmonyOS). A unified, optimizable metric and multi-agent optimization loop are needed to drive coherent, runnable migrations.

Method: ECAT: a multi-agent generator--discriminator framework that frames repository migration as adversarial entropy minimization. The discriminator computes a unified metric called Code Entropy and emits text gradients that indicate file-level generation directives and required skills; the generator iteratively edits the repository and only accepts updates that reduce Code Entropy. Low-entropy trajectories are distilled into a self-evolving memory tree for knowledge transfer.

Result: Introduced A2H-RepoBench (real-world repos, tens→hundreds of KLOC). ECAT attains 74.7% overall migration quality per node alignment and agent-based functional judge, outperforming existing agent-based methods across scales. Demonstrated transfer via memory tree.

Conclusion: ECAT provides a practical framework to align long-horizon LLM-driven edits with a repository-level objective via adversarial optimization and knowledge distillation. Promising results, but further ablation, metric transparency, scalability/compute analysis, and broader functional evaluations are needed to validate generality.

Abstract: LLMs have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and automated program repair, but migrating an entire repository rarely produces a runnable application because long-horizon translation challenges LLM-based agents' ability to maintain repository-level migration objectives. In this work, we propose Entropy-based Code Adversarial Translation (ECAT), a multi-agent framework for automated Android-to-HarmonyOS repository migration. ECAT formulates repository migration as adversarial entropy minimization through a generator-discriminator architecture. The discriminator measures migration quality using a unified metric called Code Entropy and produces text gradients that specify both file-level generation directives and the skills needed to execute them. Guided by these optimization signals, the generator iteratively updates the repository, and each update is accepted only if it reduces Code Entropy. Repeated generator--discriminator interactions progressively drive the migration from an initial template toward a functionally complete HarmonyOS repository. Successful low-entropy trajectories are further distilled into a self-evolving memory tree, enabling transferable migration knowledge across repositories. We also introduce A2H-RepoBench, the first real-world benchmark for Android-to-HarmonyOS repository migration, covering applications from tens of thousands to hundreds of thousands of lines of code. Evaluated by node alignment and an agent-based functional judge, ECAT achieves 74.7% overall migration quality and consistently outperforms existing agent-based methods across repositories of different scales.

</details>


### [69] [GRACE: LLM-Grounded Semantic Metric Spaces for Scalable Mixed-Data Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07881)
*Zihua Yang,Zhencheng Xie,Junyang Chen,Liang Xie,Yiqun Zhang,Mengke Li,Yang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将LLM知识一次性映射到属性-值描述并与数据统计交叉验证的框架（GRACE），以可扩展方式改进混合表格数据聚类的语义完整性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥合数值与类别在表格数据上的模态鸿沟：传统方法仅用数据内部统计学习类别关系，无法捕捉未在数据中出现但语义上相关的概念；而将LLM直接嵌入迭代学习代价高昂，限制可扩展性。

Method: 在属性-值层面进行一次性LLM落地（multi-perspective querying），将混合属性映射为知识增强的描述，生成通用语义表示并与数据集内统计证据交叉验证，从而在不将LLM嵌入迭代优化环路的前提下构建统一度量空间，随后将此语义表示用于下游聚类。

Result: 通过一次性语义落地并与内部统计对齐，GRACE在保有传统统计方法可扩展性的同时，在11种竞品上实现了更好的聚类精度与更高的概念可解释性；并提供了源码。

Conclusion: GRACE在成本与语义丰富性之间取得折衷，通过一次性LLM查询生成通用语义表示并与数据统计比对，实现可扩展且更具可解释性的混合数据聚类，但仍需进一步评估对LLM选择、高基数类别、跨域泛化及隐私/成本敏感性的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Clustering mixed tabular data requires a unified metric space to bridge the inherent heterogeneity between continuous numerical measurements and discrete categorical symbols. Traditionally, algorithms rely entirely on dataset-internal statistics to estimate categorical relationships, which confines the learned metric to empirical co-occurrences and ignores conceptually obvious yet statistically unobserved affinities. Although LLMs offer external world knowledge, applying their text-centric reasoning to highly abstract tabular concepts presents significant challenges. Bridging this modality gap to construct a semantically complete metric typically requires embedding LLMs into iterative metric learning loops to dynamically optimize cross-modality representations. This incurs intractable computational overhead, forcing a compromise between semantic enrichment and scalability. Therefore, we propose GRACE, an LLM-grounded framework for scalable mixed-data clustering. GRACE shifts semantic acquisition to the attribute-value level via a multi-perspective LLM querying strategy, mapping heterogeneous values into knowledge-informed descriptions. Crucially, this one-shot grounding extracts general-purpose semantic representations that embed heterogeneous attributes into a unified space, decoupling expensive LLM invocation from iterative optimization. Furthermore, GRACE cross-validates these external semantics against dataset-internal statistical evidence to ensure alignment with the dataset-specific cluster structure. Ultimately, GRACE matches the scalability of conventional statistics-driven baselines while achieving superior clustering accuracy and conceptual interpretability over 11 competing methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/develop-yang/GRACE-GRACE-A

</details>


### [70] [Reason Wide, Not Deep: Amortizing the Reasoning Premium into Distilled Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07885)
*Agamdeep Singh,Srishti Gautam,Priyanshu Gupta,Nikita Mehrotra,Tanmay Bakshi,Sumit Gulwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把跨集共享的程序性知识从轨迹语料中蒸馏为自然语言技能并放入system prompt，可以大幅弥补非推理模型与推理模式之间的性能差距，同时显著降低在线令牌成本；某些域仍需按例深度搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模式在多步代理任务上效果更好但每次部署都要花费大量输出令牌（3–6×），因为它反复推导跨任务共享的程序性知识；希望通过语料蒸馏把这类重复成本摊薄到训练阶段，从而在保持性能的同时大幅降低在线令牌开销。

Method: 在训练语料（已有轨迹）上对常见子程序/技能进行分析与提炼，生成紧凑的自然语言“技能描述”，并把这些技能注入到非推理模式模型的system prompt中；对比有无技能、推理模式与非推理模式在多步代理任务上的表现与输出令牌开销。

Result: 在四个代理基准（ALFWorld、tau^2 telecom与retail、SpreadsheetBench-Verified）上，注入技能能为GPT-5.4-mini恢复55%–100%+的“推理差距”，在两项基准上甚至超过推理模式；同时输出令牌减少2.7–6×，并且产生零推理（思路追踪）令牌。用非推理轨迹蒸馏出的技能与用成对推理/非推理语料蒸馏出的技能性能相近，且在不同领域存在差异。

Conclusion: 测试时推理相当于在单次任务上做“深搜索”，每次部署重复付费；语料蒸馏是跨任务的“宽搜索”，只需一次付费。两者覆盖的程序性知识有重合，且在常见且廉价的轨迹上做宽搜索通常更划算；但对电信与电子表格等域的残余差距表明仍有必要在某些实例上进行按需深搜索。

Abstract: Reasoning modes of language models outperform their non-reasoning counterparts on multi-step agentic tasks, but pay a 3-6x premium in output tokens on every episode -- much of it spent re-deriving procedures that are shared across episodes of the same domain. We show this recurring cost can be amortized: a coding agent analyses a small corpus of existing trajectories from a training split and compiles a compact natural-language skill that is injected into the non-reasoning model's system prompt. Across four agentic benchmarks (ALFWorld, tau$^2$-bench telecom and retail, and SpreadsheetBench-Verified), skills recover 55%-100%+ of the reasoning gap for GPT-5.4-mini on held-out tasks -- exceeding the reasoning mode outright on two of four -- while emitting 2.7-6x fewer output tokens and zero reasoning tokens. Notably, reasoning traces are not a prerequisite: skills distilled from non-reasoning trajectories alone remain competitive with skills distilled from paired reasoning/non-reasoning corpora, with domain-dependent differences between the two sources. We interpret these results through a search lens: test-time reasoning is deep search inside a single episode, re-paid at every deployment, while corpus distillation is wide search across episodes, paid once. The two recover overlapping procedural knowledge, and width over cheap trajectories is often the better buy -- with the residual gap on some domains (telecom, SpreadsheetBench) delineating where genuinely per-instance deep search remains necessary.

</details>


### [71] [TelemetrySuffBench: Is Agent Telemetry Sufficient for Failure-Origin Diagnosis?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07899)
*Yuxuan Zhu,Peng Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 新基准将检测、定位与谨慎回避分离：在完整遥测下模型能检测并定位（Top-1 33.8–97.2%），但在只保留元数据/兼容视图下仍能检测（F1≈99.5–100%）而定位几乎为零（≤0.5%），表明检测—定位差距；去除决策内容令定位准确率归零，溯源移除导致模型依赖性的大幅下降；谨慎回避能力随模型强烈不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实代理系统虽暴露执行轨迹，但可见的遥测经常不足以判定失败的因果起点。需要一个受控基准来量化‘检测’与‘定位’之间的差距，并评估模型在证据不足时是否能安全弃答。

Method: 构造带有延迟绑定故障的多组件、分步骤执行轨迹；将轨迹渲染为成对粗视图、七要素遥测掩码及完全等价的模糊来源对；使用统一评估协议（包含候选集合、无效输出计数、子群分析）评估五种前沿语言模型，并在冻结的生成器族Holdout上复现结果。

Result: TelemetrySuffBench provides a controlled benchmark disentangling failure detection, fault-origin localization, and safe abstention using canonical multi-component traces with delayed-binding faults and multiple telemetry masks. It evaluates five LLMs with unified protocols, candidate sets, invalid-output accounting, subgroup analyses, and a frozen holdout; shows strong detection but poor localization under limited telemetry, identifies key factors (decision content and provenance) affecting localization, and demonstrates model-dependent abstention behavior. Dataset and code are released.

Conclusion: 终端/状态信息足以支持故障检测，但可靠的因果归因（fault-origin localization）需要显式的决策—溯源链接和对证据不足时的有效弃答机制，且这些机制需跨模型稳健。

Abstract: Agent systems increasingly expose execution traces, yet telemetry that reveals a failure may still be inadequate for identifying where that failure originated. We introduce TelemetrySuffBench, a controlled benchmark that separates failure detection, fault-origin localization, and safe abstention under insufficient evidence. The benchmark constructs canonical multi-component traces with delayed-binding faults and renders them as paired coarse views, seven-factor telemetry masks, and exact-equal ambiguous origin pairs. We evaluate five frontier language models using unified protocols, explicit candidate sets, invalid-output accounting, subgroup analyses, and a frozen blind holdout. With full telemetry, origin-step Top-1 accuracy ranges from 33.8% to 97.2% across models. Metadata, OpenTelemetry-compatible, and OpenInference-compatible views retain 99.5% to 100% detection F1 while limiting origin-step accuracy to at most 0.5%, exposing a robust detection-localization gap. Factor ablations further show that removing decision content reduces origin-step accuracy to zero for every model, while provenance removal also causes large model-dependent losses. On rich ambiguous inputs that require abstention, evidence gating reduces unsupported unique-origin answers by 12.5 to 48.6 percentage points for three models, whereas two models still answer every case, revealing strong model dependence in safe abstention. Results on the frozen holdout reproduce the central pattern within the same generator family. These findings show that terminal status can support detection, whereas reliable causal attribution requires explicit decision-to-provenance links and abstention safeguards that remain effective across models. The dataset and benchmark implementation are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TelemetrySuffBench-E635/README.md.

</details>


### [72] [GraphThink: Graph-Enhanced LLM Thinking for Long-Horizon Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07905)
*Chen Li,Sijie Cheng,Yuelin Zhang,Junxi Li,Maozhi Huang,Yang Liu,Wenbing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphThink uses a task graph + scene graph + GRPO reward shaping to improve LLM-based embodied planning, reducing hallucinations and improving long-horizon and OOD generalization; achieves SOTA on ALFRED.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address physical hallucinations, weak long-horizon generalization, and poor environment awareness of LLM-based embodied agents by providing structured task knowledge and persistent scene memory to guide planning and allow corrective replanning.

Method: (1) Task graph provides structured knowledge and contextual prompts, plus iterative refinement for the LLM planner to reduce hallucinations. (2) Integrate task graph into GRPO framework to design rewards that train LLM planner for long-horizon tasks. (3) Scene graph maintains environment memory; an event-driven replanning module uses it to enable closed-loop awareness and error correction. Experiments on ALFRED and held-out tasks demonstrate empirical gains.

Result: GraphThink integrates a task graph for structured planning guidance and a scene graph for environmental memory, combined with an event-driven replanning module and GRPO-based reward shaping to train an LLM planner. It reportedly reduces hallucinations, improves long-horizon planning and generalization, and attains SOTA on ALFRED, outperforming API-based LLM planners on validation and held-out long-horizon tasks.

Conclusion: GraphThink’s combination of structured task graphs, scene-graph-driven replanning, and reward-shaped LLM training is a promising approach that appears to deliver improved robustness and generalization in embodied planning, but the paper needs more detail on graph construction, training procedures, ablations, and real-world constraints to fully validate claims.

Abstract: Embodied agents using LLM-based planners often struggle with physical hallucinations, poor generalization to long-horizon tasks, and lack of environmental awareness. We propose GraphThink, a novel framework that integrates a task graph to provide structured knowledge for robust planning and a scene graph to maintain environmental memory for event-driven replanning. Specifically, the task graph guides LLM thinking through contextual prompting and iterative refinement, effectively mitigating planning hallucinations. Furthermore, within the GRPO framework, the task graph offers delicate reward design to train the LLM planner, enhancing long-horizon planning capabilities and improving generalization. Finally, an event-driven replanning module, powered by the scene graph, enables closed-loop environment awareness and error correction. GraphThink achieves state-of-the-art performance on the ALFRED benchmark. In particular, our high-level planner surpasses leading API-based LLMs on both the validation set and held-out long-horizon tasks, underscoring its robust zero-shot and few-shot capabilities. Additional evaluations further demonstrate strong out-of-distribution generalization to novel tasks and environments.

</details>


### [73] [When Is Benchmark Contamination Detectable? Information Limits and Power-Calibrated Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07914)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjeda Akter,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 建立行为污染检测的统计可检出性：关键量为 α·ρ·√m，效能 ef 可用对照估计；校准效能预测功效但基于高斯预算的样本量反演在小样本时失准。建议用两阶段模拟式规划器和样本分割证书，且将效能、预算和有效性门控与不拒绝结果一并报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为审计返回“无证据”时存在两种含义：样本确实干净，或审计无力（功效不足）。需要形式化这一二义性，为审计提供可估计的效能度量、可证明的下界证书和实践可行的规划器，以便正确解读非拒绝结果并设计有力的审计。

Method: 建立稀疏混合行为通道Q_α=(1-α)P_0+αP_1；用二阶矩精确界定可检出性；把任意标量检测器归结为效能 ef，并从匹配的“干净/见过”对照中估计ef；设计样本分割的分布自由下界证书（无需定向假设）；实证上用冻结校准、精确置换通道的持出功效曲线比较，以及五种注入种子/配对注入实验；提出预宣告两阶段规划器并通过模拟验证其保守性与弃权策略。

Result: Formalized detectability of behavioral contamination under model Q_α=(1-α)P_0+αP_1; detectability governed by α·ρ·√m where ρ^2=χ^2(P_1||P_0). Any scalar detector’s efficacy ef = |E_1 f - E_0 f|/√Var_0(f) ≤ ρ and can be estimated from controls. A sample-split certificate gives distribution-free lower bounds on α without orientation assumptions. Empirically, frozen calibration efficacy predicts held-out power well (R^2=0.83–0.98), but the Gaussian-budget inversion for required sample sizes is miscalibrated at small m, failing in gate-passing channels; problem is inversion, not efficacy transport. A predeclared two-stage planner that simulates the deployed test fixes budgets, is conservative, and abstains when probes don’t transport. The certificate is valid yet often vacuous at audit scale. A five-seed paired injection study orders mechanisms verbatim>paraphrase>surface, and explains apparent answer-only signal by baseline drift. Audit results must report non-rejection alongside efficacy, budget, and validity gates.

Conclusion: 不能单独解读“无证据”——必须同时给出估计的检测效能、用于推断的样本预算及其有效性门。理论上，检测能力由α·ρ·√m控制；实践中，预估效能能很好地迁移，但基于高斯近似反推所需规模在小样本下会导致误判。两阶段预注册的模拟检测器可以修复预算并保证保守性，但证书在审计规模下常显得空洞。

Abstract: Behavioral contamination detectors can return "no evidence" either because a benchmark is clean or because the audit has little power. We formalize this distinction for a benchmark in which an unknown fraction alpha of items was seen during training. With matched clean and seen controls, the behavioral channel is the sparse mixture Q_alpha = (1 - alpha) P_0 + alpha P_1, and an exact second-moment argument shows that detectability is governed by alpha * rho * sqrt(m), where rho^2 = chi^2(P_1 || P_0) measures behavioral separability. Any scalar detector reduces to its efficacy, ef = |E_1 f - E_0 f| / sqrt(Var_0(f)) <= rho, which can be estimated from controls before the audit is run. A separate sample-split certificate lower-bounds alpha distribution-free, without requiring an orientation assumption. Our empirical finding is two-sided. Frozen calibration efficacy predicts held-out power curves, with R^2 = 0.83-0.98 across six exact-permutation channels, but the efficacy-only Gaussian budget is miscalibrated at the small sample sizes it prescribes, failing in 9/9 gate-passing channels even though efficacy itself transports. The failure is in the inversion, not the calibration. A predeclared two-stage planner that simulates the deployed test repairs the budgets, is uniformly conservative, and abstains when its probe does not transport. The certificate is valid but vacuous at audit scale, and a five-seed paired injection study recovers the mechanism ordering verbatim > paraphrase > surface, in which the apparent answer-only signal is explained by baseline drift. We report the audit contract and its failures together: a non-rejection is interpretable only alongside the efficacy, budget, and validity gates that produced it.

</details>


### [74] [TongGuOCR: A Layout-Aware and Token-Augmented OCR Framework for Chinese Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07917)
*Zhongheng Zhou,Yi Sun,Huiguo He,Yuyi Zhang,Peirong Zhang,Yulin Fang,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TongGuOCR结合局部布局重构与令牌级增强（罕见字直接建模+行间跳转令牌），显著改善中国古籍OCR的准确性与复杂版面适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多中国历史文献仅以扫描图像存留，复杂版面、罕见字形与非平凡阅读顺序使常规OCR难以得到准确逐字转写，因此需要同时建模布局信息与对罕见字符与复杂阅读路径的鲁棒解码策略。

Method: 提出两大模块：1) 布局感知预处理（构建并细化局部连贯的识别块，保留局部上下文、减少区间干扰）；2) 令牌增强识别（字符级词表扩展为罕见字提供一对一令牌，缩短解码路径；行间跳转令牌为解码器提供离散的空间位移指导，适应复杂阅读顺序）。

Result: 在两个中国历史文献OCR基准上表现优异。在更具挑战性的M5HisDoc上，达成93.76 AR，相较最佳竞品将NED从10.43降到6.15，将RO-ED从7.53降到3.49，显示明显误差降低与识别率提升。

Conclusion: TongGuOCR通过布局感知预处理与双层次的令牌增强识别，有效提升了中国古籍文献的OCR准确率，在两个基准上显著优于任务特定OCR与通用/面向OCR的多模态大模型。

Abstract: Chinese historical documents preserve valuable cultural heritage, but many collections remain accessible only as scanned page images, preventing full-text retrieval, collation, and computational analysis. Optical character recognition (OCR) can bridge this gap, but accurate transcription remains challenging because historical documents often contain complex layouts, rare characters, and nontrivial reading orders. We propose TongGuOCR, a layout-aware and token-augmented OCR framework for Chinese historical documents. First, a Layout-Aware Preprocessing module constructs and refines locally coherent recognition blocks to preserve local context while reducing interference across regions. Second, a Token-Augmented Recognition module augments the transcription target at two complementary levels: character-level vocabulary expansion gives each rare glyph a direct one-token representation and shortens its decoding path, while line-to-line transition modeling injects discrete spatial displacement tokens that guide the decoder along complex reading paths without requiring precise coordinates. Experiments on two Chinese historical document OCR benchmarks show that TongGuOCR outperforms representative traditional task-specific OCR models, general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs), and OCR-oriented MLLMs. On the more challenging M5HisDoc benchmark, TongGuOCR achieves 93.76 AR and reduces NED from 10.43 to 6.15 and RO-ED from 7.53 to 3.49 relative to the best competing score for each metric. An online demo is available at https://jzzh2004.github.io/TongGuOCR.

</details>


### [75] [ZhuLong: Execution-Grounded LLM Agent for EDA Scripting with Offline API Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07925)
*Yang Liu,Shiwei Hou,Xiyuan Chen,Yu Wang,Sen Yuan,Qirui Gan,Shao You,Feifan Chen,Wencheng Li,Shuyang Hu,Yongzhou Liu,Emma Xia,Xiaojing Lu,Hao Wang,Fan Xu,Yanfeng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于执行验证与沙箱的LLM代理（ZhuLong）显著提升EDA脚本任务通过率，沙箱执行是关键，API自探提高精度并降低调用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EDA脚本依赖大量专有、工具特定且常缺文档的API，纯LLM难以可靠生成可执行脚本。提出结合执行反馈与自动化API探索，以提高脚本生成的可执行性与健壮性。

Method: 系统由：1) API检索与文档检测模块，2) 统一MCP工具接口以执行/检测API调用并在沙箱中运行，3) 离线API自探模块通过构造反事实实验推断未记录API行为。评估使用断言驱动的自动化基准（EDA-Eval-PyAether）和若干交互式任务，并做了消融分析来量化各模块贡献。

Result: ZhuLong is an execution-grounded LLM coding agent for PyAether and SKILL that integrates API retrieval, documentation inspection, and sandbox execution via unified MCP tools, plus an offline API self-exploration mechanism based on counterfactual experiments. On a 158-task benchmark (EDA-Eval-PyAether) it reaches 78.5% Pass@1 in Empyrean Aether vs. 23.6% for a pure LLM baseline. Ablation shows sandbox execution is the dominant factor (−41.2 pp when removed); self-exploration adds +3.2 pp and reduces per-task tool calls by 22.1%. On 20 interactive unsaved-layout tasks it gets 60.0% Pass@1 for PyAether and 50.0% for SKILL.

Conclusion: 执行-落地（execution-grounded）策略通过在安全沙箱中运行与自动探索未记录API行为，显著改进了对工具特定且文档欠缺的EDA API的代码生成与执行成功率。

Abstract: EDA scripting with tool-specific, often undocumented APIs remains a long-tail bottleneck that existing LLMs fail to address. This paper presents ZhuLong, an execution-grounded LLM coding agent for PyAether and SKILL that combines API retrieval, documentation inspection, and sandbox execution via unified MCP tools, augmented by an offline API self-exploration mechanism that infers undocumented API behaviors through counterfactual experimentation.
  We evaluate ZhuLong on EDA-Eval-PyAether, a benchmark of 158 real-world tasks with assertion-based execution, where the complete system achieves 78.5% Pass@1 in the commercial Empyrean Aether environment, substantially outperforming a pure LLM baseline (23.6%). Ablation studies identify sandbox execution as the dominant performance driver (41.2 pp drop when removed), with the self-exploration mechanism contributing an additional 3.2 pp accuracy gain and a 22.1% reduction in per-task tool calls. On 20 interactive tasks involving unsaved layouts and schematics, ZhuLong achieves 60.0% Pass@1 for PyAether and 50.0% for SKILL.

</details>


### [76] [REIN: Bridging the Gap between Reasoning and Reliability via Reflection and Abstention Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07931)
*Zhengze Huang,Luyang Yu,Di Hong,Xinzhe Huang,Wanyu Lin,Zhixuan Chu,Zhan Qin,Tianhang Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REIN trains LRMs to produce a think→reflection→answer chain and rewards abstention when no correct chain exists, yielding large reductions in hallucination and improved selective accuracy while preserving coverage in a single forward pass.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM hallucinations come from two sources: flawed internal inference (reasoning hallucination) and missing factual knowledge (knowledge hallucination). The paper aims to mitigate the first via explicit self-reflection and the second via incentivized abstention to avoid unsupported predictions.

Method: An alignment training framework that forces the model to emit a structured sequence: <think> → <reflection> → <answer>. A reward mechanism is applied to encourage explicit abstention (e.g., "I don't know") when sampled reasoning chains do not produce a correct answer. Training and evaluation are done without process supervision, external search, or multi-round critique; gains are achieved within a single forward pass.

Result: On mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks across multiple model backbones, REIN reduces a hallucination proxy by 58–72% relative to base models, maintains 86–91% average coverage, and improves selective accuracy on attempted questions by 6.6–14.2%. It reduces incorrect-but-self-endorsed responses and retains single-pass inference without extra supervision or external tools.

Conclusion: REIN effectively reduces hallucination in large reasoning models by combining structured self-reflection and an abstention reward, improving selective accuracy while retaining high coverage without external tools or multi-round critique.

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are prone to hallucination, which undermines their reliability and poses challenges for safe deployment. Hallucinations in LRMs arise from two distinct failure sources: reasoning hallucination, where flawed inference steps propagate to an incorrect conclusion, and knowledge hallucination, where the model lacks the requisite factual knowledge to answer the query. To address reasoning hallucination, we propose REIN, an alignment framework that trains LRMs to produce a structured reasoning sequence, $\texttt{<think>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<reflection>} $$\rightarrow$ $\texttt{<answer>}$, enabling explicit self-reflection before committing to a final answer. To address knowledge hallucination, REIN introduces a reward mechanism that encourages explicit abstention (e.g., "I don't know") when none of the sampled reasoning chains yields a correct answer, allowing the model to refrain from unsupported predictions. Extensive evaluations on mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks show that REIN consistently improves selective accuracy, reduces incorrect-but-self-endorsed responses, and maintains high coverage compared with competitive baselines. Notably, REIN achieves these gains within a single forward pass, without requiring process supervision, inference-time controllers, external search, or multi-round critiques. Experiments on multiple backbones show that REIN reduces the hallucination proxy by $58\sim72\%$ relative to the base models while maintaining $86\sim91\%$ average coverage, and improves selective accuracy on attempted questions by $6.6\sim14.2\%$.

</details>


### [77] [Locating Failure in Multi-Page Visually Rich Document Understanding: An Empirical Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07943)
*Lewei Xu,Yihao Ding,Zihan Xu,Daniel Yitian Su,Daochang Liu,Siwen Luo,Yifan Peng,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Multi-page visually-rich document understanding (MP-VRDU) requires managing evidence that is sparse, spread across pages, and often exceeds a model's context window. Prior work has produced competing, largely untested claims about how these systems should be built. We attribute incorrect answers to three failure modes, representation, selection, and reasoning, and isolate each over a multi-page document understanding dataset by intervening on one while holding the others fixed. We find that vision is necessary but does not replace text extraction, that missing pages bound accuracy while distractors cost little, and that reasoners fail to integrate evidence across pages even when it is fully supplied. Prompting can shift reasoning behaviour substantially, improving some outcomes at the expense of others. We translate these findings into guidance for building such systems under a fixed compute budget.

</details>


### [78] [Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution for Verification of Distributed Parallel AI Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07947)
*Gautham Koorma,Vikas Sharma,George Edwards,Mahdi Eslamimehr*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DNSSE通过把大语言模型用于调度引导并与神经-符号求解器和覆盖驱动变异结合，显著提升了分布式AI程序的并发缺陷检测和分支覆盖，同时给出相应的理论保证和成本分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行分布式AI程序具有非确定性执行和高维非线性输入，传统测试（模糊、符号执行）难以覆盖并发缺陷；需要融合语义推理与调度引导的混合测试方法。

Method: 将分布式AI执行建模为非确定性迁移系统，使用线性时序逻辑(LTL)指定正确性；利用LLM预测有前景的调度、符号求解约束以定位输入/调度、以及基于覆盖的随机变异增强探索，组合为DNSSE混合求解器并分析其正确性与复杂度。

Result: 提出DNSSE：将LLM引导的调度预测与符号约束求解和覆盖引导的随机变异结合，理论上证明了求解器的可证性（正确性、有界完备性、概率完备性）并给出LLM调度探索的期望成本分析。实现于PyTorch+Ray，在五个真实分布式AI基准上比最强基线发现2.9%更多并发缺陷，平均分支覆盖率从68.6%提升到91.6%。

Conclusion: 混合使用LLM引导的调度预测、符号约束求解和随机变异，能在工程实践中提高分布式AI程序的测试有效性；理论证明与实验结果共同支持方法的有效性，但需关注可伸缩性、LLM成本与可复现性。

Abstract: Distributed parallel Artificial Intelligence (AI) programs expose reliability gaps that conventional testing cannot close: parallel executions are non-deterministic, and AI workloads bring high-dimensional inputs and non-linear operations that defeat fuzzing and symbolic execution in isolation. We present Directed Neuro-Symbolic Stochastic Execution (DNSSE), a hybrid testing framework that couples schedule prediction guided by a Large Language Model (LLM) with symbolic constraint solving and coverage-guided stochastic mutation. We model distributed AI executions as non-deterministic transition systems, specify correctness in linear temporal logic, and prove soundness, bounded completeness, and probabilistic completeness of the hybrid solver, together with an expected-cost analysis of LLM-guided schedule exploration. A scalable implementation on PyTorch and Ray detects 2.9% more concurrency bugs than the strongest baseline and raises average branch coverage from 68.6 % to 91.6 % across five realistic distributed AI benchmarks.

</details>


### [79] [Guixu: Valuation-Driven Data Discovery for Autonomous AI Agents with On-Chain Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07949)
*Yifan Wu,Yuchen Peng,Jiaqi Chai,Yufei Qian,Xilin Li,Ke Chen,Lidan Shou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guixu moves beyond keyword retrieval by valuing datasets for a given task under budget constraints and enabling verifiable procurement using on-chain attestations and an agentic payment protocol, demonstrated via an interactive system.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autonomous agents increasingly need external data for downstream tasks (training, decision support), but current data discovery systems focus on retrieval and lack task-specific utility estimation, budget-aware selection, and mechanisms to incorporate trustworthy usage feedback.

Method: Three-phase valuation pipeline (including proxy-label propagation), multi-round knapsack optimization for selecting datasets under budget, agentic payment protocol for procurement workflows, and use of on-chain data market plus attestation signals for verifiability.

Result: Guixu: a valuation-driven data discovery system that uses a three-phase valuation pipeline with proxy-label propagation and multi-round knapsack optimization for task-aware data valuation; integrates an agentic payment protocol for budget-constrained procurement; leverages on-chain data markets and attestation signals for verifiable discovery; demonstrated interactive end-to-end workflow from NL task spec to verifiable transactions.

Conclusion: Guixu enables agents to perform task- and budget-aware, trustworthy data discovery and procurement, representing a practical step from retrieval to valuation and verifiable transactions.

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly rely on external data to complete downstream tasks such as model training and decision support. However, existing data discovery systems remain largely retrieval-oriented: they surface candidate datasets from heterogeneous sources, but provide limited support for estimating task-specific utility, selecting cost-effective datasets under budget constraints, or incorporating trustworthy feedback from prior usage. This paper presents Guixu, a valuation-driven data discovery system for autonomous agents. Guixu employs a three-phase valuation pipeline with proxy-label propagation and multi-round knapsack optimization for task-aware data valuation. Guixu integrates agentic payment protocol to enable budget-constrained data procurement workflows. Guixu leverages on-chain data market and attestation signals for verifiable data discovery. Our demonstration highlights how Guixu enables an agent to move beyond keyword-based dataset retrieval toward task- and budget-aware, trustworthy data discovery and procurement. Attendees can interactively explore the full workflow, from NL task specification and multi-source search to data valuation and verifiable transaction feedback.

</details>


### [80] [KGCache: Amortized Subgraph Retrieval for KG Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07954)
*Uros Stanic,Changcheng Yuan,Sabuj Laskar,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KGCache caches one-hop neighborhoods to exploit reuse in KGQA workloads, reducing KG retrieval latency and improving end-to-end throughput under common eviction policies; semantic caching adds modest gains but needs more accuracy validation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reduce redundant KG neighborhood retrievals in KGQA workloads by caching one-hop neighborhoods to speed up grounding of LLMs and KGQA engines, since existing systems repeatedly query same graph neighborhoods.

Method: Design and implement KGCache placed between KGQA engine and KG backend; support ToG and RoG paradigms; cache one-hop neighborhoods keyed by entity (and optionally semantic context); evaluate using traces from WebQSP and CWQ with LRU, LFU and an Oracle policy; measure hit rates, latency and full-system speedup; analyze reuse patterns and explore semantic caching for similar queries.

Result: KGCache, an in-memory cache for one-hop KG neighborhoods compatible with both ToG and RoG paradigms. Placed between KGQA engine and KG backend, evaluated with LRU/LFU/Oracle policies on WebQSP and CWQ. Shows substantial entity reuse, semantic caching gives extra hit-rate on WebQSP. Entity caching speeds KG retrieval up to 1.91x; semantic-context caching yields up to 1.06x full-system speedup and per-hit up to 3.73x faster.

Conclusion: Caching one-hop KG neighborhoods is effective for KGQA workloads with entity reuse; simple eviction policies are useful and semantic-context caching can further improve hit rates, though broader evaluation (accuracy effects, larger KGs, dynamic graphs) is needed.

Abstract: Large language models can answer knowledge-intensive questions more reliably when they are grounded with knowledge graphs, but systems such as Think-on-Graph and Reasoning-on-Graph repeatedly query the same graph neighborhoods across different questions. In this work, we study this repeated retrieval in Knowledge Graph Question Answering~(KGQA) workloads and propose KGCache, an in-memory cache for one-hop knowledge graph neighborhoods. KGCache is designed to be compatible with both iterative traversal (ToG) and one shot planning (RoG) KGQA paradigms. KGCache is placed between the KGQA engine and the backend serving the KG, so repeated entity requests can be served from cache instead of issuing new KG queries. We evaluate KGCache on WebQSP and CWQ using LRU, LFU, and a trace-aware Oracle policy. Our analysis shows that both datasets contain substantial entity reuse among starting entities and entities reached during traversal. We also explore semantic caching for similar queries, which shows additional hit-rate gains on WebQSP and needs further accuracy testing on CWQ. Entity caching accelerates KG retrieval by up to $1.91\times$, while semantic-context caching achieves up to $1.06\times$ full-system speedup in the evaluated WebQSP configurations, with each hit being up to $3.73\times$ faster.

</details>


### [81] [Self-Evolving Neuro-Symbolic Skills for Tool-Augmented Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07955)
*Shi-Yu Tian,Zhuo-Xia Wang,Xuan-Yi Zhu,Zhi Zhou,Xinwei Yang,Kun-Yang Yu,Ming Yang,Yang Chen,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeSy-Spatial is a neuro-symbolic framework that builds and evolves reusable, typed skills (tool-use and geometry) from atomic instructions, retrieving and refining them from trajectories to better solve fine-grained spatial reasoning benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large vision-language models struggle with fine-grained spatial tasks requiring precise spatial perception and geometric computation; existing tool-augmentation approaches either lack dependency constraints or use rigid pipelines that fail to generalize.

Method: Abstract tool interactions and geometric operations into typed executable atomic instructions; compose them into Tool-Use Skills and Geometry Skills; during inference retrieve and execute relevant skills in a closed-loop; during evolution analyze buffered trajectories to refine skill structures and prune unreliable/inactive skills.

Result: On three spatial reasoning benchmarks, NeSy-Spatial consistently improves reasoning accuracy and achieves more precise tool utilization compared to baselines.

Conclusion: NeSy-Spatial proposes a neuro-symbolic, self-evolving framework that improves fine-grained spatial reasoning by composing typed executable atomic instructions into reusable skills and iteratively refining them from successful and failed trajectories.

Abstract: Large vision-language models have achieved strong performance in multimodal reasoning, but they remain unreliable on fine-grained spatial tasks that demand both precise spatial perception and fine-grained geometric computation beyond end-to-end generation. Tool augmentation offers a natural solution, while existing methods either plan tool calls from scratch without explicit dependency constraints or rely on fixed pipelines that are redundant and generalize poorly across spatial tasks. An effective spatial reasoning agent should instead accumulate reusable experience and adaptively compose it for new problems. To this end, we propose NeSy-Spatial, a neuro-symbolic framework for self-evolving spatial skills. NeSy-Spatial abstracts tool interactions and geometric operations into typed executable atomic instructions and composes them into two complementary skill types: Tool-Use Skills for organizing tool execution and Geometry Skills for structured geometric reasoning. During inference, NeSy-Spatial retrieves and executes relevant skills in a closed-loop process. During evolution, it analyzes buffered successful and failed trajectories to refine skill structures and prune unreliable or inactive entries. Experiments on three spatial reasoning benchmarks show that NeSy-Spatial consistently improves reasoning accuracy with more precise tool utilization.

</details>


### [82] [SCOUT: Self-Checking and Recovery-Aware Tool-Thought Agents for Ultra-Long Egocentric Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07959)
*Keyang Zhong,Kuo Wang,Peng Liu,Quanlong Zheng,Junlin Xie,Zhijia Liang,Yanhao Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出具有自检与恢复能力的SCOUT代理与优先不确定性探索的UPS-GRPO训练方法，通过自适应的探索/利用决策和回合级奖励分解，显著提升超长第一视角视频的多跳推理性能，达成SOTA表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长第一视角（egocentric）视频理解需要跨小时或天级别，从时间上稀疏分布的证据中进行多跳推理，现有多模态模型受限于上下文窗口和关键片段定位能力。现有Chain-of-Tool-Thought代理通过迭代检索和检查支持推理，但因僵化的“放大”策略缺少恢复机制而易积累错误。

Method: 提出SCOUT（Self-Checking Chain-Of-Tool-thought）框架：一种具备恢复能力的代理式系统，内含自适应策略，在每个工具调用后的观察上进行评估，根据不确定性动态在“深入（zoom-in）”与“切换区域（exploration）”间权衡，从而在极长时域的视频上实现鲁棒的多跳推理。为训练多回合工具使用代理，提出UPS-GRPO（Uncertainty-Prioritized Policy Optimization）：一种优先对高不确定性后工具状态进行探索的策略优化方法，以保持样本效率；并提出回合级优势分解，将最终结果奖励与基于工具的时间对齐奖励融合以改进长时序决策的信用分配。

Result: 在超长第一视角基准上取得最先进（SOTA）结果，并且在较短时域的长视频任务上保持竞争力。提出的方法在样本效率、长期信用分配和多跳检索-推理的鲁棒性上都有明显提升。

Conclusion: SCOUT+UPS-GRPO为极长时序、多跳工具使用场景提供了一条可行路线，能减少错误传播并改善信用分配；但方法可能面临计算开销、对不确定性估计与时间对齐信号的依赖，以及在不同域间泛化与可重现性方面的挑战，需通过消融、稳健性测试与效率评估进一步验证。

Abstract: Ultra-long egocentric video understanding requires reasoning over temporally sparse evidence distributed across hours or days, challenging current multimodal models with limited context and the grounding of key video segments. While Chain-of-Tool-Thought (CoTT) agent systems enable iterative retrieval and inspection, they suffer from error propagation due to rigid zoom-in strategies that lack recovery mechanisms. In this work, we address these challenges through SCOUT (Self-Checking Chain-Of-Tool-thought), a recovery-aware agentic framework introducing an adaptive policy that evaluates intermediate tool observations and dynamically trades off exploitation (zoom-in) and exploration (region switching), enabling robust multi-hop reasoning over extremely long horizons. However, training such multi-turn tool-using agents remains challenging, as existing RL methods rely on sparse outcome-level rewards and lack supervision over extended decision trajectories, resulting in suboptimal credit assignment for long-horizon reasoning. To address this, we develop UPS-GRPO, an uncertainty-prioritized policy optimization method that concentrates exploration on high-uncertainty post-tool states while preserving sample efficiency. We further introduce a turn-level advantage decomposition that integrates outcome rewards with tool-grounded temporal alignment rewards for improved credit assignment. Experiments show that SCOUT achieves state-of-the-art results on ultra-long egocentric benchmarks, while remaining competitive on shorter-horizon long-video settings.

</details>


### [83] [CyberAGENTS: Structured Autonomy for Agentic Gamified Learning in Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07965)
*Ivan Hornung,Deepthi Marasinghe Arachchige,Tharindu Kumarage,Garima Agrawal,Yuli Deng,Ying-Chih Chen,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个用本体验证与行为模式约束生成式AI代理的游戏化网络安全教学框架，按能力进阶组织内容，四类专职代理配合工作；实证显示这种结构化控制能提升参与、信任与行为稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需要主动问题解决与迭代技能训练的教育场域（如网络安全）中，游戏化能提升动机和实践机会；但将生成式AI用于教学会带来不稳定行为、幻觉与教学目标错配等风险，因此需要将代理系统以学习科学为基础进行约束与验证。

Method: 构建一个以能力进阶为中心的多代理框架（CyberAgents），将学习过程分解为挑战、支持、评估和奖励四类专职代理；每个代理由行为模式（schemas）控制以限定自治范围；使用网络安全本体对所有生成内容进行事前验证以保证领域一致性和安全性；能力进阶模型对主题按难度与先修关系建模，支撑分层化教学与任务递进。

Result: 课堂部署与专家评估显示：启用行为模式与本体验证时，学习者参与度提升、反馈更易解读、对AI生成回复的信任度提高；与未受约束的配置的初步比较表明结构化控制有助于稳定教学行为。

Conclusion: 结构化自治（行为模式+本体验证）可在不完全剥夺生成式灵活性的前提下，稳定并对齐AI代理的教学行为，为设计具教学依据的游戏化代理系统提供可行蓝图；后续需更严格的量化学习效果与可推广性评估。

Abstract: Gamification is especially effective in learning domains requiring active problem-solving and iterative skill-building, such as cybersecurity education. Generative AI agents offer a path to delivering such experiences adaptively at scale, but introduce well-documented risks in educational settings: inconsistent behavior, hallucinated reasoning, and misalignment with pedagogical frameworks. Grounding these systems in learning science is therefore essential. We present \model, an agentic framework for gamified cybersecurity learning that enables structured autonomy through ontology-guided validation, schema-governed behavioral control, and competency-based progression. The system is organized around a competency-based progression model that structures topics by difficulty and prerequisite relationships, reflecting evidence-based principles of scaffolded instruction. The learning loop is decomposed into four specialized agents: challenge, support, evaluation, and reward, each governed by behavioral schemas that encode operational modes and progression logic, bounding agent autonomy without eliminating generative flexibility. A cybersecurity ontology validates all generated content prior to display, enforcing domain-consistent reasoning and safety constraints. We evaluate CyberAgents through classroom deployment with undergraduate students, complemented by expert evaluations from educators and domain specialists. Results indicate improved engagement, clearer feedback interpretation, and greater learner trust in AI-generated responses when behavioral schemas and ontology validation are active. Preliminary comparisons with an unconstrained configuration further support the role of structured control in stabilizing instructional behavior. These findings offer a blueprint for designing pedagogically grounded agentic gamified learning systems.

</details>


### [84] [VDGR-RAG: Vectors, Directories, Graphs, and Reflection Are All You Need for Unified Reasoning over Hierarchical Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07994)
*Wenqi Chen,Haofei Yang,Rui Yang,Fangming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出将目录驱动路由、向量检索、图遍历与迭代反思融合的 VDGR-RAG，通过构建 H^2KG 与原子检索工具链显著提升面向复杂企业文档的检索召回与 QA 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 方法往往未能整体整合向量检索、目录层级信息与图结构推理，导致领域路由不准、对目录/层级文档结构利用不足与企业知识上的推理能力受限。作者旨在把不同检索长处统一到一个 agentic GraphRAG 框架中以提高企业级 QA 的准确性与召回。

Method: VDGR-RAG 架构通过构建层级异构知识图 H^2KG 并组合多种原子检索工具（目录路由、向量检索 + TOC agentic + 图检索的多路检索、目录回溯、动态反思）实现检索增强生成（RAG）。系统先用目录结构对查询进行领域路由，再在 H^2KG 上以可组合工具链执行检索与图遍历，最后通过迭代反思规划下一轮检索以提升定位与推理能力。

Result: 在作者的企业产品文档（覆盖四个无线领域，如节能与故障管理）上，VDGR-RAG 在知识检索召回率和 QA 准确率方面显著优于多种 RAG 基线方法。

Conclusion: 将目录结构与语义向量检索、图遍历及反思机制统一在可组合的检索工具链内，是提升企业级知识问答效果的有效路径。后续需关注可扩展性、构图质量依赖、不同工具贡献的消融研究及公开基准上的可复现性以验证泛化性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is essential for enterprise knowledge question answering (QA), particularly in domains with complex product documentation like telecommunications. However, existing RAG approaches largely overlook the holistic integration of diverse retrieval strengths, leading to inaccurate domain routing, poor utilization of hierarchical document structures, and consequently limited reasoning capabilities over enterprise knowledge. To address these limitations, we present VDGR-RAG, which integrates vector retrieval, directory-driven reasoning, graph traversal, and iterative reflection in a unified framework for accurate enterprise knowledge QA. Specifically, VDGR-RAG is an agentic GraphRAG system that first constructs a Hierarchical Heterogeneous Knowledge Graph ($\text{H}^2$KG) from document chunks to preserve both hierarchical directory structures and semantic relationships, and then employs a set of atomic tools for knowledge retrieval that can be freely composed to navigate the $\text{H}^2$KG: (1) a directory-enhanced routing tool that uses table-of-contents (TOC) structures to route user queries to appropriate domain-specific $\text{H}^2$KGs; (2) a multi-route retrieval tool that combines vector search, TOC-based agentic search, and graph search for comprehensive knowledge retrieval; (3) a directory backtracking tool that corrects knowledge localization biases; and (4) a dynamic reflection tool that iteratively plans the next retrieval phase. We conduct extensive experiments on our enterprise product documents across four wireless domains (e.g., energy saving and fault management). Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms a variety of RAG baselines in terms of both knowledge retrieval recall and QA accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [Thought-Level Beam Search for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08020)
*Lijie Yang,Hongyin Luo,Tri Dao,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gambit 通过思考级束搜索在测试时动态重分配计算，兼顾剪枝与并行化优点，提升准确率与硬件利用率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大推理模型性能越来越依赖测试时计算量，直接扩大量级的计算代价高昂且效率低下，关键问题从“花多少计算”转为“把有限计算放在哪儿”。现有并行采样造成内存瓶颈，而削减式剪枝又不能充分、主动地改变输出分布。

Method: 将测试时推理形式化为对部分轨迹的受限计算分配问题；提出“思考级束搜索”（thought-level beam search），周期性地用轻量评分器探测隐藏状态以剪枝低质量轨迹，并即时从高质量前缀分支，保证持续的硬件利用率以便高效集中计算资源。

Result: 在多个模型与基准上广泛评估：相对剪枝基线，在 HMMT-24 上最高绝对精度提升 6.7%，在 AIME-25 上提升 3.3%；在轨迹完成任务上吞吐率提升超过 2×；相较标准并行采样，令牌总消耗最多减少 68.5%，总体上严格支配现有基线。

Conclusion: Gambit 在相同硬件预算下通过动态将计算集中到最有希望的推理轨迹上，显著超越现有并行采样与削减式剪枝基线，提升精度与吞吐并大幅降低总令牌消耗，从而改进了大推理模型的测试时计算分配策略。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling is a primary driver of performance in large reasoning models (LRMs), but extreme inefficiency bounds current approaches, shifting the critical question from \emph{how much} compute to spend, to \emph{where} to allocate it. We formalize test-time reasoning as a constrained compute allocation problem over partial trajectories. Under a fixed hardware budget, existing paradigms fail to actively allocate the compute to the most promising partial progress: traditional parallel sampling treats traces independently and induces severe memory bottlenecks, while subtractive pruning starves hardware and fails to actively and sufficiently shift the output distribution. To overcome this dichotomy, we introduce Gambit, an inference algorithm that executes \emph{thought-level beam search}. By periodically pruning unpromising trajectories and immediately branching from high-quality prefixes, Gambit dynamically concentrates compute onto the most promising reasoning traces via a light-weight scorer probing hidden states while maintaining continuous high hardware utilization. Extensive evaluations across multiple models and benchmarks demonstrate that Gambit strictly dominates existing baselines. Under identical hardware constraints, our method yields up to a +6.7\% absolute accuracy gain on HMMT-24 and +3.3\% on AIME-25 over pruning baselines, delivers $>2\times$ higher throughput on trace completion, and reduces total token consumption by up to 68.5\% relative to standard parallel sampling.

</details>


### [86] [Legal Responsibilities Using Autonomous Agents For Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08022)
*Mark Burgess*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文讨论AI代理无意逃逸引发的责任归属问题，提出基于Promise Theory与Downstream Principle的系统性追责方法，建议将责任扩展到具独立能力的代理并通过政策限制代理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期AI代理'逃逸'事件造成法律与伦理模糊，促使寻找系统化的责任分配原则与治理工具。

Method: 理论性方法，基于Promise Theory构建责任分配框架，利用Downstream Principle追踪因果影响并提出通过策略限制代理自由的工程与治理建议。

Result: 提出了可操作的概念框架：以因果链为基础判定责任、将代理包含在责任范围内，并建议依靠日志、策略和限制措施实施；同时承认需要进一步实证、法律与技术支持。

Conclusion: 采用Promise Theory与Downstream Principle可用因果影响链条来界定责任，但需技术性可追溯性和法律适配；政策可以限制代理自由以降低风险。

Abstract: Recent incidents involving Artificial Intelligence (AI) agents, which were reported escaping their containment `unintentionally' to gain unauthorized access, pose looming questions about who or what should be held legally responsible for resultant criminal or negligent damage. As the independent capabilities of agents expand, Promise Theory suggests a systematic method to resolve these questions, based on the Downstream Principle for causal influence. Responsibility can easily be expanded to include AI agents where tracing responsibility becomes impactical, and agents' freedoms to act can be limtied by policy choices.

</details>


### [87] [The Authority Expectancy Effect in Multi-User Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08026)
*Eunna Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs reinterpret identical evidentiary content depending on social-authority cues, producing systematic, non-additive shifts in prioritization—termed the Authority Expectancy Effect (AEE).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand how social-authority signals interact with severity-prioritization in LLM decision-making because real-world triage, allocation, and mediation tasks require combining evidentiary severity with who is speaking; miscalibration could produce biased or unsafe outcomes.

Method: Operationalized two model-elicited baselines:
- Triage hierarchy (severity-based prioritization)
- SA hierarchy (model-elicited social authority ordering)
Conducted experiments on four LLMs (Claude, Gemini, GPT, Grok) across three phases: resource allocation, fault attribution, and multi-turn dispute mediation. Manipulated cues: occupational authority, institutional documentation, relational congruence. Measured changes in model judgments and interaction effects; characterized non-additivity and defined AEE with three properties.

Result: Authors identify an Authority Expectancy Effect (AEE): LLMs’ severity-based prioritization is restructured by social authority signals in non-additive ways. Using model-elicited baselines (triage and SA hierarchies) and experiments across four LLMs (Claude, Gemini, GPT, Grok) and three tasks (resource allocation, fault attribution, multi-turn dispute mediation), they show occupational authority, institutional documentation, and relational congruence change model judgments via reference-dependence, evidential reinterpretation, and direction sensitivity.

Conclusion: Social-authority signals reshape LLM inferences about severity and responsibility in ways not explainable by simple additive weighting; AEE is reference-dependent, reinterprets evidence, and is direction-sensitive, implying that authority cues alter probabilistic reasoning and ranking behavior.

Abstract: We investigate how social authority (SA) signals interact with severity-based prioritization in large language models, operationalizing each axis as a model-elicited baseline -- the triage hierarchy and the SA hierarchy. Across four LLMs (Claude, Gemini, GPT, Grok) and three experimental phases -- resource allocation, fault attribution, and multi-turn dispute mediation -- we find that occupational authority, institutional documentation, and relational congruence can restructure model judgments in ways not captured by additive reweighting of authority cues. We formalize this pattern as the Authority Expectancy Effect (AEE) and characterize it through three properties observed across our conditions: it is reference-dependent, defined only relative to a pre-authority baseline; it involves evidential reinterpretation, in which identical content acquires different inferential implications depending on which party bears the SA signal; and it exhibits direction sensitivity, producing opposite outcomes depending on whether authority position and evidentiary cues align.

</details>


### [88] [Decided Upstream, Written Late: Locating and Pricing the Cross-Lingual Refusal Circuit of a Multilingual MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08032)
*Ramakrishna P. Kompella,Aadit Mahajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 安全检测在多语模型中多为语言不变，但拒绝是由后期组装的MoE写入电路决定；可通过廉价地削弱抑制器或昂贵地放大写入器改变行为，带来对多语安全修补的成本-效果地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示并理解多语言模型中安全拒绝行为不均匀的机制：为何模型在英语中拒绝有害请求但在低资源语言中可能同意，评估是否是检测失败或其他内部机制导致不一致。

Method: 在sarvam（一种Indic多语MoE推理模型）上做机械化分析：用表示分析测量语言不变方向；用上游操控（steering）与因果干预验证检测方向控制拒绝倾向；通过梯度方法和局部化诊断识别并验证MoE写入器与attention opposer电路；测试多种干预（抑制、放大、外科编辑）并在第二个MoE模型上复现以检验泛化性。

Result: 发现危险检测在中间层以接近语言不变的方向编码（英语与印地语等Indic余弦相似约0.9），且在上游操控该方向可以因果地控制拒绝行为。但真正把“拒绝”写到输出的是一个晚发、在生成过程中逐步组装的局部可定位电路——一个MoE写入器（writer），被一个attention opposer压制。对该电路的干预成本不一：削弱opposer廉价有效、直接放大writer有高成本、对特定头做外科式编辑无效。该组织和梯度暴露方法在第二个无关MoE模型中复现，但调节杆（lever）强度与架构有关。结果构成了一个带成本评价的多语安全修补可落点图谱。

Conclusion: 该工作把多语安全不均衡从“检测失败”排除，定位到一个局部可控但成本分化的写入电路，并提供了可通用的分析与干预范式，为在不同语言上修补安全行为提供了工程与理论上的路径约束。

Abstract: Safety alignment in multilingual models is uneven: a model that reliably refuses a harmful request in English will often comply with the same request in a lower-resource language. We trace this gap mechanistically in sarvam, an Indic-multilingual mixture-of-experts reasoning model, and find it is not a failure to detect harm. Harm is encoded as an internal direction that is nearly language-invariant in mid-network (English-vs-Indic cosine ${\approx}0.9$ at $L11$), and steering that direction upstream causally controls refusal. But the detection direction is orthogonal to the change that actually writes the refusal, which is late and assembled over the course of generation rather than read off in a single forward pass. We attribute the write to a specific, localizable circuit, a mixture-of-experts writer held in check by an attention opposer and price every way of intervening on it: damping the opposer is cheap and effective, amplifying the writer is a cost wall, and surgical edits to the responsible heads do nothing. The circuit's organization, and the gradient method that exposes it, recur in a second, unrelated MoE model, while the lever's strength is architecture-specific. The result is a cost-measured map of where a multilingual safety repair can land, and what it costs

</details>


### [89] [SkillSmith: Enhancing Locally Deployed Agents via Automatic Skill Construction and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08037)
*Xinle Jiang,Remy Xie,Ming Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过将云端探索自动构造成“Skill”并以非参数方式下发给本地代理，SkillSmith显著弥补了小型开源模型的环境知识缺失，使本地代理在任务效果上接近甚至达成云端水平，同时降低步骤数并具备可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Local-agent frameworks running open-source SLMs are privacy-preserving and cost-saving but underperform cloud-based agents because backbone SLMs lack environment-specific knowledge (rules and procedures) due to limited scale.

Method: 定义Skill作为上下文高效的知识载体；用云端Agent通过任务探索自动生成Skill；将Skill以非参数方式供给并驱动冻结的本地SLM；在本地执行中收集反馈以进化Skill循环迭代；在AppWorld和WorkBench数据集上进行实证评估，与非参数基线和云端代理比较。

Result: SkillSmith—an automated Cloud–Local collaboration that extracts "Skills" from cloud-agent task exploration and iteratively refines them with local execution feedback—enables a frozen local SLM (Qwen3.6-27B) to match cloud-agent effectiveness, outperform non-parametric baselines, cut average actions per task dramatically, and generalize to other SLM backbones without redoing Skill construction.

Conclusion: SkillSmith是一个高效的云—本地协作框架，利用自动化Skill构建与执行反馈进化，在不微调本地模型的前提下显著提升本地代理的多步任务能力，且具备跨模型通用性和操作效率提升。

Abstract: LLM-based agent frameworks now act as personal assistants for multi-step tasks. Existing agent frameworks such as OpenClaw commonly follow the Cloud Agent depolyment mode using closed-source cloud LLMs as backbone model, which may expose private user information and incur repeated LLM-calling costs. Local Agents address these deployment concerns by depolying frontier open-source SLMs on user-controlled devices, but their task effectiveness still lags far behind Cloud Agents. Through diagnostic analysis, we reveal that the limited effectiveness of Local Agents with frontier SLM backbones mainly comes from missing environment knowledge caused by limited backbone model scale including environment rules and operation procedures. To supply such knowledge non-parametrically, context-efficiently, and without expert authoring, we present SkillSmith, a Cloud--Local Agent collaboration framework that uses Skill as a context-efficient knowledge carrier, automatic constructs Skill from Cloud Agent task exploration and evolves Skill using Local Agent execution feedback to enhance a frozen Local Agent. Experiments on daily agent task datasets AppWorld and WorkBench show that the automatically generated Skill enables the Local Agent with Qwen3.6-27B(SLM) to achieve task effectiveness comparable to Cloud Agents with frontier LLMs, outperform the strongest non-parametric baselines, reduce average actions per task from 36.1 to 9.9 on AppWorld-Normal, and generalize to other SLM backbone models without rerunning Skill construction.

</details>


### [90] [Lingjing: A Simulation Testbed for Multi-Agent Embodied Tasks in Open-Ended Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08045)
*Xiaohe Li,Yiru Wang,Junhao Fan,Mingyuan Liu,Jie Huang,Kaixin Zhang,Jiahao Li,Chen Qian,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lingjing是一个面向演化城市的异构多主体仿真平台，支持多引擎同步、自然语言任务、通信机制与可溯源回放。在对若干视觉-语言模型和城市任务的评测中，暴露出感知落地与长程执行的核心瓶颈，以及协调权衡与容量收益递减现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真平台往往将不同形态的机体隔离，并与任务设计和评估解耦，缺乏在演化城市场景中统一、可溯源、端到端评测异构多主体协同智能的环境。Lingjing旨在填补这一空白，为研究者提供可复现的测试床与系统化失败诊断手段。

Method: 提出Lingjing仿真平台：基于地理数据重建并渲染演化城市场景，跨多物理引擎同步仿真，向代理暴露共享的物理与结构化城市状态；提供类似Gym的接口，支持ReAct代理、自然语言单/多代理任务、配置化的星状或广播通信与资源约束；每个episode生成可溯源回放，关联代理轨迹、通信记录、关系图变化、资源消耗与引擎评估；在此平台上，对12个视觉-语言模型在9个城市任务上进行统一引擎入环的评测，并开展控制性实验（通信、可扩展性、鲁棒性、失败溯源）。

Result: 平台实现了城市重建、多引擎同步与可溯源回放；基准实验揭示视觉-语言模型在落地感知与长时程执行方面的持续瓶颈；不同任务呈现协调策略的权衡；增加模型容量带来收益递减；更高工作量降低成功率。平台可用于重复实验与系统化故障分析。

Conclusion: Lingjing为城市级异构多主体智能研究提供了统一且可溯源的测试床，便于端到端评测与失败诊断；但当前结果也表明感知-落地、长期规划与规模化运行仍是主要挑战。建议后续扩展对物理与感知逼真性的验证、公开基线与数据集、以及现实闭环验证以提升迁移价值。

Abstract: Urban embodied intelligence requires coordination among heterogeneous agents (e.g., UAVs, ground robots, and autonomous vehicles) in dynamic cities. Simulators therefore provide a scalable foundation for developing and evaluating such coordination. Existing platforms nevertheless isolate different embodiments and decouple them from task design and evaluation. We present \textbf{Lingjing}, a simulation platform for heterogeneous multi-agent embodied intelligence in open-ended urban environments. Lingjing reconstructs and renders evolving cities from geographic data, synchronizes multiple physics engines, and exposes shared physical and structured urban state to agents. Its Gym-like interface supports user-defined ReAct agents and single- or multi-agent natural-language missions with configurable star or broadcast communication and resource constraints. Each episode becomes an attribution-ready replay that links agent trajectories and communication to relation-graph changes, resource consumption, and engine-based evaluations for systematic diagnosis. We evaluate twelve vision-language models on nine urban tasks under a shared engine-in-the-loop protocol. Controlled studies further examine communication, scalability, robustness, and failure provenance. Results expose persistent bottlenecks in grounding and long-horizon execution. They also show task-dependent coordination trade-offs and diminishing returns from added capacity, while heavier workloads further reduce success. Lingjing provides a unified testbed that enables reproducible end-to-end evaluation and systematic failure diagnosis in urban multi-agent embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [91] [JustLLMGRPO: Radiographic Control for Chest X-Ray Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08046)
*Pengxiang Cai,Xiaohan Li,Anglin Liu,Qingyuan Zeng,Zexun Li,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出“生成器不动、提示可学”是提升 CXR 生成性能的重要方向；提出 JustLLMGRPO，只对 LLM 提示策略做 GRPO 优化并用放射学相关图像反馈，显著降低 FID 并保持与原提示的对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多集中在改进图像生成器，默认提示在域适配后固定；作者认为提示如何表述放射学所见是一个未充分利用的优化空间，优化提示策略可在不改动生成器的前提下带来显著性能提升。

Method: 步骤概述：1) 将 Sana 图像生成器适配至 CXR 域并冻结；2) 使用未修改的 LLM 对放射学文本进行一次性重写，观察到 FID 大幅下降但 BioViL-T 对齐下降；3) 提出 JustLLMGRPO：仅对 LLM 的提示策略应用 Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO），并用分组的、放射学感知的图像反馈作为奖励信号，保持生成器不变；4) 在 CheXGenBench 上用 RadDINO-FID、BioViL-T alignment、分布覆盖和下游分类等指标评估。

Result: 该工作表明：在胸片（CXR）条件生成任务中，通过仅优化用于描述放射学所见的LLM提示策略，而保持已适配的图像生成器（Sana）固定，能显著提升合成影像的视觉质量（RadDINO-FID 从 54.225 降至 ~26.78），同时在保持与源提示对齐（BioViL-T）方面做到不退步。为此，作者提出 JustLLMGRPO：对 LLM 的提示策略应用分组相对策略优化（GRPO），并用放射学感知的图像反馈来指导优化，避免了直接重写提示导致的对齐下降。作者在 CheXGenBench 上展示了方法在图像质量、分布覆盖和下游分类性能上的领先性，且开源代码。

Conclusion: 提示表达方式是条件医学图像合成中被低估的优化维度。通过在不改动已适配生成器的情况下优化提示策略，可以获得接近或超过改进生成器本身的收益。JustLLMGRPO 在保持源提示语义对齐的同时，显著提高了生成影像的质量和实用性，证明了对提示策略的系统化优化具有实际价值。

Abstract: Text-conditioned chest X-ray generation aims to synthesize realistic radiographs that faithfully depict specified findings. Existing work has primarily improved quality by updating image generators, implicitly treating prompts as fixed after CXR-domain adaptation. We show that this generator-centric view leaves a substantial optimization dimension underexplored. With a CXR-adapted Sana generator frozen, one-pass reformulation by an unmodified LLM reduces RadDINO-FID from 54.225 to 27.572. Prompt analysis shows that the LLM suppresses temporal comparisons, uncertainty, and other non-renderable report content while emphasizing visible radiographic findings. However, unconstrained reformulation reduces BioViL-T alignment with source prompts from 0.695 to 0.609. We therefore introduce JustLLMGRPO, which applies standard Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) only to the LLM prompt policy while keeping Sana frozen. Group-relative radiology-aware image feedback retains visual focus while preserving source-prompt alignment. On CheXGenBench, JustLLMGRPO reduces RadDINO-FID to 26.780, a 50.6% improvement over direct prompting, while maintaining alignment (0.696 versus 0.695). It also achieves state-of-the-art distribution coverage and downstream classification utility. These results show that substantial performance can remain latent in how radiographic information is expressed to an adapted generator. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/pxcai/JustLLMGRPO.

</details>


### [92] [SodaMem: Evidence-Grounded Temporal Graph Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08055)
*Fengrong Wan,Chengcan Wu,Ningtao Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SodaMem通過帶來源與時間的事件圖與混合索引，加planner–reader證據搜集，在低成本模型檔位實現高準確度的長期對話記憶與可引用回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents that persist over long conversations need記憶不只是過去被說過的話，還要反映當前真實性、來源與時間順序；現有的平鋪RAG筆記或Markdown日誌在貨幣性、可引用性與時間推理上不足。

Method: 系統化流程：1) 從對話中抽取typed FactEvents並標記provenance spans；2) 在圖結構中保存mention/occurrence/validity三類時間並用SUPERSEDES/CONTRADICTS/UPDATES邊串連事件；3) 採混合lexical+dense向量索引以兼顧精確檢索與語義檢索；4) 在回答階段用planner–reader迴路先檢索並收集可引用證據，再由reader基於證據生成最終回答；5) 對比不同系統的API成本以生成成本-精度地圖。

Result: 提出SodaMem：一個證據驅動的時間圖記憶，抽取typed FactEvents並強制保留provenance spans，記錄mention time/occurrence time/validity，以SUPERSEDES/CONTRADICTS/UPDATES邊維護衝突與更新，採混合lexical-dense索引，並透過planner–reader loop先聚集可引用證據再生成回答。在LongMemEval-S（store-of-record配置）達到92.8%準確率（464/500，best of 3），平均成本約$0.00161/題（約18.3k tokens）並提供成本-準確度比較表，程式碼公開於GitHub。

Conclusion: SodaMem在貨幣性、可追溯性與有序時間推理方面提供實用改進、兼顧成本效益；但評估依賴自我評分與部分成本估算，需補充獨立判分、真實線上延遲/擴展性分析與用戶研究以驗證實際效用與可靠性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents that assist users over weeks of conversation must remember what is currently true, not merely what was once said. Flat RAG diaries and Markdown logs optimize needle retrieval but under-serve currency, provenance, and ordered temporal reasoning (Maharana et al. 2024; Wu et al. 2024; Packer et al. 2023; Chhikara et al. 2025). We present SodaMem, an evidence-grounded temporal graph memory that (i) extracts typed FactEvents with mandatory provenance spans, (ii) persists mention time, occurrence time, and validity with SUPERSEDES/CONTRADICTS/UPDATES edges under hybrid lexical-dense indexing, and (iii) answers via a planner-reader loop that gathers citable evidence before composing a final response.
