Specifically, we craft attacker-submitted queries to lead the agent to perform the target action and explicitly state the corresponding activation condition in the trajectory. We repeat the same condition-action pattern across diverse triggered tasks, while leaving clean queries unchanged, encouraging the evolver to consolidate the pattern as a reusable trigger-dependent rule into the evolved skill. Experiments on three benchmarks across two skill-evolution systems using four open- and closed-source backbone models demonstrate that TBA reliably implants conditional backdoors while preserving clean-task utility, matching or even surpassing direct skill injection. The results reveal a critical vulnerability in trajectory-driven skill evolution.

</details>


### [125] [StructReward: Efficient Structured Process Rewards for Self-Correcting Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08326)
*Yifan Li,Ruxin Sun,Tongzhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StructReward，通过结构化步级对齐用轻量匹配规则生成稠密过程奖励，与最终答案和输出有效性结合，并用GRPO优化，同时回收rollouts并用强LLM重写正确轨迹用于反思训练，省去额外验证器或外部判别器，提升多模态推理的自我改进效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么只用二元最终答案奖励、要么依赖昂贵的过程标注或外部判别器，忽视中间步骤监督。目标是以计算友好的方式提供稠密、细粒度反馈来改进多模态推理。

Method: 将解答表示为步骤序列，使用数值、符号、词汇匹配规则与标注参考步对齐，聚合为过程奖励；与最终一致性和输出有效性奖励经门控Group Relative Policy Optimization(GRPO)目标联合优化；回收策略rollouts用于响应比较与自我修正训练，并用强LLM重写正确轨迹生成反思式训练样本。

Result: 实验表明StructReward在性能与计算效率上优于依赖外部判别器的基线，结构化过程监督与rollout回收推动了自我改进。

Conclusion: 结构化过程监督与rollout回收能在无需额外判别器的前提下，为多模态强化学习提供高效稠密奖励，显著降低计算开销并提升模型推理质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as an effective approach for improving multimodal reasoning. However, most existing methods evaluate an entire response using a binary reward based only on final-answer correctness, thereby discarding the supervision available in intermediate reasoning steps. Process reward models offer finer-grained feedback, but they typically rely on separately trained verifiers, costly chain-of-thought annotations, or online judging by large language models (LLMs). In this work, we introduce StructReward, a compute-efficient framework that provides dense reinforcement signals through structured step-level reward alignment. StructReward represents each generated solution as a sequence of reasoning steps and aligns them with process-labeled reference steps using lightweight numerical, symbolic, and lexical matching rules. The aligned labels are aggregated into a dense process reward and combined with final-answer consistency and output-validity rewards through a gated Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) objective. We further recycle policy rollouts into complementary supervision for response comparison and reflective self-correction, rather than discarding them after policy updates. Separately, we use a strong LLM to rewrite sampled correct trajectories into reflection-oriented training instances, further strengthening the policy's ability to evaluate and refine its reasoning. Since reward computation is performed online without an additional learned verifier or external LLM judge, StructReward substantially reduces the computational overhead of multimodal reinforcement learning. Experimental results show that structured process supervision and rollout recycling provide an efficient path toward self-improving multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [126] [LLMVisor: A Real-Time Latency Attribution Model for Multi-Tenant LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08382)
*Shuowei Jin,Xueshen Liu,Jiaxin Shan,Le Xu,Tieying Zhang,Liguang Xie,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMVisor is a fast, roofline-based piecewise-linear model that decomposes batch latency into per-request latency shares using FLOP and memory-I/O features, yielding much higher attribution accuracy than token-count baselines at microsecond runtime overhead.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multi-tenant GPU inference with co-batching needs accurate, lightweight, real-time per-request attribution to enable fractional sharing and fine-grained control.

Method: Roofline-guided piecewise-linear latency model that attributes batch latency to per-request additive shares using features proportional to FLOPs and memory I/O; runs in microsecond scale inside scheduling loop.

Result: On A100/H100 with Llama 3.1-8B and Qwen 2.5-14B/32B, LLMVisor achieves near-perfect R^2 and reduces relative error versus token-count baseline by up to 2.5x/3.3x (p90/p99 prefill) and 3.5x/4.4x (decode), handling batching variability and sequence divergence.

Conclusion: LLMVisor provides accurate, low-overhead per-request latency attribution suitable for real-time scheduling and fractional sharing in multi-tenant LLM inference clusters, though evaluation details and generalization require further scrutiny.

Abstract: As LLM inference shifts to multi-tenant GPU clusters, co-batching improves throughput but obscures per-tenant usage and limits control. Enabling fractional sharing of the inference engine requires a real-time, per-request attribution primitive that is accurate and light enough to run inside the scheduling loop. We present LLMVisor, a roofline-guided latency attribution model that captures the memory-bound and compute-bound phases via a concise piecewise-linear form over features proportional to FLOPs and memory I/O traffic. LLMVisor decomposes batch latency into additive, per-request shares and runs efficiently at microsecond scale. We evaluate LLMVisor across Llama 3.1-8B and Qwen 2.5-14B/32B on A100/H100 GPUs under varying tensor parallelism and workload mixes. Compared to a token-count baseline, LLMVisor attains near-perfect R-squared and reduces relative error by up to 2.5x and 3.3x at p90 and p99, respectively, for prefill, and by up to 3.5x and 4.4x for decode, despite batching variability and sequence divergence.

</details>


### [127] [Not Worth Another Token: Marginal Value Estimation for Efficient Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08389)
*Harshitha Kolukuluru,Reshma Ashok,Kirat Arora,Evan William Ciccarelli,Nischal Ashok Kumar,Lunyiu Nie,Franck Dernoncourt,Samyadeep Basu,Ryan A. Rossi,Nedim Lipka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对长时程代理的分阶段上下文裁剪进行系统比较：何时裁剪比如何裁剪更重要；轻量启发式效果显著且高效，学习型方法在特定权衡下有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放式、迭代的研究任务中，上下文会迅速增长而边际证据价值递减，导致不必要的token成本、增加延迟与噪声输入；需要一种可行的上下文管理策略来在效率与质量之间取得平衡。

Method: 在长时程研究代理的三条关键管线位置（pre-retrieval、post-retrieval、pre-synthesis）分别施加裁剪策略。比较若干轻量启发式规则（例如基于关键词、相似度、时间戳或冗余度的阈值筛选）和一个学习型价值模型（估计新增证据的边际价值），使用多维度评价指标（token使用量、延迟、最终报告质量与可信度）进行端到端评估。

Result: 本文展示了在长时程研究代理中进行上下文裁剪（pruning）以控制检索上下文增长的系统性研究。作者在检索前、检索后、合成前三个阶段对轻量启发式规则和学习型价值模型进行了比较实验，发现裁剪效果更依赖于应用阶段而非具体得分规则：早期裁剪带来最大的端到端节省，后期裁剪则主要用于精细化合成上下文。轻量级启发式方法可在几乎不降低质量的情况下将token使用量减少最多73％，学习型方法在若干质量-效率权衡点上仍有竞争力，但没有单一方法能在质量、效率和可信度三者上同时占优。

Conclusion: 阶段选择（何时裁剪）对上下文管理的收益影响最大；工程上可以优先采用早期轻量级启发式裁剪以显著降低成本，同时结合后期精细筛选或学习模型以改善最终合成质量与可信性。

Abstract: Long-horizon research agents solve open-ended tasks through iterative retrieval, aggregation, and synthesis, but context grows rapidly while the marginal value of additional evidence often declines. This leads to unnecessary token cost, higher latency, and noisier inputs for final report generation. We study marginal value estimation for context management in deep research agents and present the first systematic stage-aware comparison of pruning strategies across the pipeline. We evaluate lightweight heuristic criteria and a learned value model at pre-retrieval, post-retrieval, and pre-synthesis stages. Our results show that pruning effectiveness depends more on where pruning is applied than on the specific scoring rule: early pruning yields the largest end-to-end savings, while later pruning mainly refines the final synthesis context. Lightweight heuristics reduce token usage by up to 73% with little quality degradation, learned pruning remains competitive on selected trade-offs, and no single method dominates across quality, efficiency, and faithfulness. These findings provide practical guidance for designing efficient long-horizon agentic systems.

</details>


### [128] [CAP: A Scalable Benchmark for Evaluating Cross-Site Browser Agents with Complex Actions and Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08392)
*Zejun Xu,Taiyi Chen,Jin Li,Yongtong Gu,Qi Cheng,Aixuan Lv,Shuai Zhu,Pengfei Zhu,Kaichen Yang,Boyu Sun,Yixian Yang,Mulong Xie,Xin Liu,Dagang Li,Xiaoteng Ma,Hongru Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CAP基准评估跨站点浏览代理，侧重复杂UI操作与视觉感知，构建420任务，实验表明现有代理在感知密集场景性能低下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端任务成功率评测忽视复杂UI与动态渲染视觉感知，尤其是跨站工作流，故需新的基准。

Method: 提出分解-重组流程：为每网站抽象site card（功能、复杂操作、感知要求），再将site card组合成跨站工作流，构建可诊断的任务集并采用可验证的agent-as-a-judge评估。

Result: 构建420个任务、覆盖108网站与24域；评测最先进代理显示低成功率，感知相关操作是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前浏览器代理在跨站、多站点、人类式复杂操作与视觉理解任务上存在显著差距，需加强视觉感知与界面操作能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents that interact with the web through browsers. While recent progress has been driven by benchmarks that evaluate end-to-end task success, these evaluations largely overlook two fundamental sources of difficulty in real web browsing: complex actions over rich user interfaces and visual perception of dynamically rendered content, especially in workflows that span multiple websites. We introduce CAP, a scalable benchmark for evaluating browser agents on cross-site, human-like web tasks that require non-trivial UI interactions and visual understanding. Specifically, we adopt a decomposition-and-recomposition pipeline that first abstracts each website into a structured site card capturing user-facing functions, complex execution operations, and perceptual requirements, and then recomposes these components into realistic cross-site workflows. Each task is therefore grounded in multiple specific operations on each website, enabling fine-grained diagnosis. Built on this framework, we construct 420 tasks across 108 real-world websites and 24 domains under careful quality control. Experiments on state-of-the-art browser agents using our verifiable agent-as-a-judge evaluation framework show low success rates and reveal that perception-heavy interactions remain a major bottleneck, exposing substantial gaps between current agents and real-world web browsing demands.

</details>


### [129] [Estimating Uncertainty in Galaxy Morphology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08398)
*Kai Cheng,Ruoqi Wang,Qiong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UEGMC：基于冻结foundation model特征的后验不确定性估计框架，可无采样地预测多类型不确定性（参数、数据、参考标准、固有模糊性），用于银河形态分类的细粒度不确定性评估，并在实验中表现出有竞争力的量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天文观测数据噪声大且银河形态随时间连续演化导致固有模糊性，现有基于foundation models的GMC通常是确定性点估计，无法量化预测可信度，因此需要高效、可解释的后验不确定性评估方法。

Method: 从已训练（冻结）的foundation model提取表示，构建一个后置不确定性预测器（post-hoc）来回归/分类不确定性分数，框架按来源将不确定性分为模型参数、观测数据、参考标准与物理本质模糊性四类，避免对主干模型做昂贵的采样或多次前向。

Result: UEGMC在所选数据集和对比方法下展示了竞争性的uncertainty quantification性能（具体指标未在摘要给出），且能提供细粒度的不确定性类型输出，同时计算开销小于基于采样的贝叶斯或集成方法。

Conclusion: UEGMC为GMC任务引入了一种高效的后验不确定性估计流程，能基于冻结主干特征直接预测不确定值，兼顾计算效率与细粒度的类型划分，实验表明其在不确定性量化指标上与现有方法可比。

Abstract: Astronomers classify galaxy morphology to investigate cosmic evolution. While deep foundation models are increasingly utilized in Galaxy Morphology Classification (GMC), little work has been done on evaluating the uncertainty of GMC results. Uncertainty evaluation is important because astronomical data are inherently noisy due to instrumental and environmental limitations. Also, the continuous evolution of galaxies creates intrinsic morphological ambiguity. However, current foundation models operate as deterministic point estimators, failing to quantify the uncertainty. To overcome this limitation, we propose UEGMC, a post-hoc framework of Uncertainty Estimation for Galaxy Morphology Classification. It categorizes uncertainty in GMC into distinct types by model parameters, astronomical data, reference standards, or intrinsic physical ambiguities, thereby facilitating better classification. Our framework can directly predict uncertainties from representations extracted from the frozen backbones of foundation models, without computationally expensive sampling, therefore enabling fine-grained uncertainty evaluations. Our experimental results demonstrate that UEGMC provides competitive uncertainty quantification performance compared with previous methods.

</details>


### [130] [Forgotten History or Test-of-Time? Retrospect and Prospect on RAG from an IR Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08445)
*Xiaoyan Zhao,Yujie Cai,Yang Zhang,Grace Hui Yang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张：RAG的核心思想并非起源于LLM，而是来源于早期IR/QA研究；将LLM视为老架构上的新接口，并建议吸收先前成果改进RAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正领域史观与学术归属偏差，减少无谓重复发明，促进IR与NLP/LLM社区间的知识流通，从而更高效地推进检索增强生成系统的设计与评估。

Method: 作者采用文献溯源与概念比对的方法：识别RAG的核心技术要素（检索-生成耦合、知识注入、验证、交互式细化），并将其映射回早期IR/QA研究的对应主题，分析为何此前工作未被充分承认，最后提出以LLM为接口层的再框架及可借鉴的具体研究方向。

Result: 这篇论文的核心论点是：RAG（Retrieval-Augmented Generation）并非完全新发明，其核心思想（检索与生成结合、知识增强、答案验证、迭代查询/提示细化）在早期的信息检索（IR）和问答（QA）研究中已有成熟探讨；当代LLM只是把这一长期存在的QA架构换成了新的接口层。论文通过系统追溯现代RAG与“Agentic RAG”的知识谱系，分析社区割裂、术语变迁与近因偏差导致的低识别度，并主张借鉴早期在用户建模、答案验证和查询细化方面的工作以推进RAG设计。

Conclusion: 论点有说服力且具启发性：通过历史性地将现代RAG归入IR/QA连续谱，论文帮助避免重复发明并促进跨社区整合。不过要增强影响力，应补充更系统的文献对照、细化分类、以及若干实证或案例研究来验证‘借鉴先行工作能提升RAG’的具体效果。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is widely regarded as a novel paradigm born from the limitations of large language models (LLMs)--a mechanism to ground their outputs in external knowledge. This view, however, is incomplete when considered within a broader historical context. In this paper, we argue that the core ideas underlying RAG are not new: foundational concepts such as integrating retrieval and language generation, knowledge augmentation, answer verification, and iterative query (or prompt) refinement had already been studied and instantiated in information retrieval (IR) and question answering (QA) research dating back to the early 2000s, well before the emergence of LLMs.
  We make this case by systematically tracing the intellectual lineage of modern RAG and Agentic RAG back to their classical IR and QA antecedents, and examining why this continuity has gone under-recognized -- a consequence of community fragmentation, shifting terminology, and the recency bias endemic to fast-moving fields. Rather than treating LLMs as the origin point of retrieval-augmented intelligence, we propose viewing them as a new interface layer atop a decades-old QA architecture. This reframing is not merely historical: by situating RAG within the longer trajectory of IR research, we surface underutilized prior work -- on user modeling, answer validation, and query refinement -- that can directly inform next-generation RAG design, reducing unintentional rediscovery and fostering genuine cross-community integration.

</details>


### [131] [TRACE-Memory: Public-Conditioned Retrieval and Utility-Aware Evidence Admission for Personalized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08446)
*Jing Wang,Zhu Wang,Yifan Guo,Yulong Yang,Yunji Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段（Query + Admission）的选择性个性化记忆框架，通过增量效用判断是否引入用户记忆并用渐进式SFT+GRPO训练，在多域任务上显著提升且实现有证据可追溯的紧凑记忆使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人历史往往包含错误偏好方面、公共信息重复或支持不足的条目。默认把记忆注入上下文会浪费上下文预算并可能降低质量，因此应当仅在记忆确实带来超越公共信息的实用价值时才使用。

Method: Stage 1: 生成查询以发现请求和公共上下文中缺失的用户专属信息，进行覆盖导向检索得到候选证据池。Stage 2: 基于响应级增量效用，选择可追溯的紧凑证据单元子集或拒绝全部证据。训练采用：结构化SFT初始化 -> reduced-space stage-wise GRPO暖机 -> 嵌套多样本Joint GRPO联合训练，以稳定策略并优化性能。

Result: TRACE-Memory 引入了一个两阶段的选择性个性化记忆检索与证据筛选框架：阶段一（Query）生成缺失的用户特定信息并检索覆盖导向的候选记忆池；阶段二（Admission）基于响应级的增量效用，在可追溯的证据单元集合与空集合之间选择紧凑子集。训练流程是渐进式的：先结构化的SFT初始化，再经过去空间的分阶段GRPO暖机，最后用嵌套多样本的Joint GRPO联合优化策略。实验覆盖 Goodreads、Amazon Reviews、Reddit 上约4500个控制与自然任务，TRACE-Memory 比随机与词汇基线优越、优于语义检索，并随着本地生成器能力上升与最前沿LLM流水线持平。方法能根据公共上下文充分性来条件化记忆引入，支持选择性而非默认的个性化。

Conclusion: TRACE-Memory 证明在个性化生成中“只在带来增量效用时使用个人记忆”是可行且有效的；其两阶段检索+遴选与分阶段强化学习训练设计能在多个数据集上稳定改进响应质量并节约上下文预算。

Abstract: Personalized generation systems retrieve user history by request--memory relevance and inject it into the model context. Yet relevant history may concern the wrong preference aspect, duplicate public information, or provide insufficient support. We argue that personal memory should be used only when it adds utility beyond a public-only response. We propose TRACE-Memory, a two-stage framework for selective personalization. Stage 1 queries for user-specific information missing from the request and public context, then retrieves a coverage-oriented candidate pool. Stage 2 admits a compact subset of source-traceable evidence units, or the empty set, according to response-level incremental utility. We progressively train the query-generation and evidence-admission policies through structured SFT initialization, reduced-space stage-wise GRPO warm-up, and nested multi-sample Joint GRPO. Across 4,500 Controlled and Natural tasks from Goodreads, Amazon Reviews, and Reddit, TRACE-Memory consistently outperforms random and lexical memory use, improves over semantic retrieval, remains competitive with frontier-LLM memory pipelines as local generator capacity increases, and conditions evidence admission on public-context sufficiency, supporting selective rather than default personalization.

</details>


### [132] [What Keeps Agent Skills from Being Reusable? Evidence from 138K SKILL.md Files](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08453)
*Chi Zhang,Yimin Liu,Xinze Chen,Ping Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对138,133个公开SKILL.md文件的静态与功能分析发现绝大多数（约91.8%）存在至少一项缺陷，主要为包装与组织问题（路由元数据薄弱、主体臃肿不可执行、资源组织差），而非复杂攻击。有效的路由元数据显著提高从启动描述中检索技能的可靠性。遵循规范的技能缺陷更少；AI标记技能在安全与可移植性方面问题更多。基于规范的提示、轻量lint、自动修复与安全门控能显著提升质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SKILL.md被设计为可复用技能描述，许多公开技能仍表现为单任务/单仓库产物，无法在多会话或代理间复用。研究旨在量化这一差距、定位主要失败模式，并探索可行的自动化质量保障手段。

Method: 收集138,133个来自20,556仓库的公开SKILL.md；设计基于官方规范与最佳实践的两层缺陷分类法；对文件执行静态检测（宽松与严格阈值评估）；对20,000个技能进行确定性路由压力测试以评估功能性影响；统计按平台/溯源的缺陷分布；并开展轻量级执行与自动修复实验验证改进流程。

Result: 91.8%技能至少含一项缺陷（在宽松/严格阈值下为88.8%-94.6%）；主要缺陷类别为路由元数据薄弱、主体臃肿/不可操作、资源组织差；路由有效性的有无在确定性检索中显著影响技能被检索的可靠性；规范感知技能缺陷率更低，AI标注技能在安全/可移植性上问题更多；轻量级lint与自动修复实验能降低部分缺陷并提升检索成功率。

Conclusion: 当前SKILL.md生态中可复用性的缺口主要源于工程与规范遵从问题而非攻击性内容。通过推广规范感知的生成、自动化lint与修复，以及安全检查，可在规模上提升技能的可用性与可检索性。

Abstract: Under the current standard, Agent Skills are SKILL.md files that combine instructions with supporting resources, enabling Large Language Model (LLM) agents to reuse procedures beyond a single conversation. Yet many public skills appear to originate from a single task, repository, or conversation, even when they are shared as reusable components. We analyze this gap across 138,133 public SKILL.md files from 20,556 repositories using a two-tier defect taxonomy grounded in the official specification and best-practice guidance. We find that 91.8% of skills contain at least one detected defect, with stable estimates across lenient and strict thresholds (88.8-94.6%). The dominant failures are ordinary packaging problems rather than exotic attacks: weak routing metadata, bloated or non-actionable bodies, and poor resource organization. A deterministic routing stress test over 20,000 skills shows the functional impact: skills with valid routing metadata are retrieved more reliably from startup descriptions than skills with routing defects. Defect rates vary by platform and provenance: specification-aware skills contain fewer defects, while AI-marked skills show more safety and portability problems. Lightweight enforcement and repair experiments support a quality-assured generation workflow combining spec-aware prompting, lightweight linting, automated repair, and safety gating.

</details>


### [133] [Hierarchical Self-Improvement: A Framework for Task-Specific Evolvable Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08466)
*Tailin Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Hierarchical Self-Improvement (HSI)：通过层次化、自我改写的任务 harness 对冻结 LLM 进行持续优化，依赖高质量反馈且受限于骨干模型能力；在多个基准上获得明显提升并能泛化到部分未见任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有改进 LLM agent 的方法多集中在提示语、工具或工作流的手动修改，而将模型周围的可执行框架（harness）视为部署后固定物。作者主张将 harness 视作可针对任务家族持续演化的第一公民，以自动化和周期性地提升冻结模型在特定任务上的表现。

Method: HSI 由三层作用域组成：
- Harness H：执行具体任务的可替换脚本/骨架，通过固定的任务注入接口运行。 
- Evolver：在冻结模型 M 的帮助下改写/优化 H，使其更适合目标任务家族。 
- Meta-evolver：在更外层的固定锚点下改写 evolver 的策略代码，从而调整自我改写的策略。 
设计上使用“thinking-on/off”机制——任务执行时禁用推理以隔离 harness 作用，但在自我改写阶段开启推理以支持设计变更。实验在 BALROG 平台上评估，量化了反馈保真度边界和骨干能力边界。

Result: HSI 在多任务基准上通过演化任务特定的执行脚本（harness）显著提升了一个冻结的大型模型的下游任务表现，同时给出两个实证上的边界（反馈保真度和骨干能力）。在 BALROG 平台上，使用 DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Preview 作为冻结骨干，HSI 在多套中等难度任务上取得了明显的百分比进步（BabyAI +39.3、Crafter +33.0、TextWorld +25.0、MiniHack +15.0），并在 BabaIsAI 的若干子集上展示了强泛化（BreakStop best-test 0.98，GoTo 1.00），但在超出骨干能力的 NLE 任务上无改进。

Conclusion: 任务特定且可演化的 harness 是改进冻结 LLM 行为的有效方向，但其收益受限于（1）用于选择/优化的反馈信号是否富含信息，以及（2）被改写的模型本身是否具备足够能力；因此，HSI 在合适场景下能带来显著增益，但无法突破骨干的根本能力上限。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents are often improved by modifying prompts, tools, or workflows manually, while the executable scaffold surrounding the model---the \emph{harness}---is typically treated as a fixed artifact after deployment. This work studies an alternative where the harness is \emph{task-specific and continuously evolvable}: each task family maintains its own harness, which is hot-swapped across iterations through a fixed task-injection seam and rewritten using environment feedback. We introduce \textbf{Hierarchical Self-Improvement (HSI)}, a framework in which a single frozen LLM $M$ operates across three hierarchical scopes: a task harness $H$ that executes tasks, an evolver that rewrites $H$, and a meta-evolver that rewrites the evolver's strategy code under a frozen outer anchor. A thinking-on/off design isolates the contribution of harness evolution by disabling reasoning during task execution while enabling it during self-modification. HSI is bounded by two factors: a \emph{feedback-fidelity bound}, since evolution requires informative reward signals to guide selection, and a \emph{backbone capability bound}, since harness redesign cannot overcome limitations of the frozen model. On BALROG with DeepSeek-V4-Flash-Preview as the frozen backbone, HSI achieves consistent gains over the initial harness on moderate-difficulty tasks ($+39.3$ on BabyAI, $+33.0$ on Crafter, $+25.0$ on TextWorld, and $+15.0$ on MiniHack, all in raw \% Progress), while obtaining strong held-out generalization on BabaIsAI sub-suites ($0.98$ best-test on BreakStop and $1.00$ on GoTo from a $20\%$ unseen split). On tasks beyond the backbone's capability (NLE), harness evolution provides no improvement. These results demonstrate task-specific harness evolution as a viable axis for improving frozen LLM agents under clear empirical limits. Code is available at https://github.com/TailinZhou/hsi.

</details>


### [134] [LLM within MCP Matters: Measuring Inefficient Resource Utilization Driven by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08467)
*Minhan Cho,Soyoung Park,Kihyeon Jeong,Byeongkyu Jeon,Daejin Choi,Jinyoung Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在有可用搜尋工具時，LLM 常放棄系統提示中嵌入的伺服器資料；組合式指令工程可緩解但不能根本解決，應在 MCP 層面引入優先指令於工具選擇之前的機制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP 要標準化伺服器如何向 LLM 暴露資料與工具。伺服器常把常用參考資料（例如識別碼查表）直接嵌入系統提示，以便模型能立即使用而非重複透過工具搜尋。問題是：客戶端 LLM 是否會實際採納這類指令內嵌資料？

Method: 實驗性大規模評估：在一個生產等級的法律資訊 MCP 伺服器上，對 24 種 LLM（9 Claude、6 Gemini、9 GPT）進行共 54,000 次試驗。設計了一個移除搜尋工具的診斷條件以區分能力缺失與行為偏好；並針對三個指令層級的干預做 2^3 因子分析，測量模型對嵌入表格條目之「命中率（hit ratio）」。

Result: 關鍵發現包括：在無搜尋工具時，23/24 模型對嵌入資料的命中率至少 98%；當搜尋工具存在但未被移除時，有 9 種模型的命中率降至 15% 以下。三項干預的組合能把 20/24 模型的命中率恢復到至少 86%，但個別干預對不同模型族群存在顯著交互作用且可能反效果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，當客戶端 LLM 同時面對伺服器指令裡嵌入的參考資料與可用的搜尋工具時，模型往往偏好使用工具而非直接採納指令內的資料；這一現象主要是行為偏好而非能力缺失。透過三項指令級干預的組合可以在大多數模型上顯著恢復對嵌入資料的使用，但單一干預對不同模型家族有反效果。作者因此主張 MCP 主機應提供一種顯式機制，在模型做工具選擇之前先把伺服器指令置於優先考量。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes how servers expose data and tools to Large Language Models (LLMs). A common server design embeds frequently used reference data, such as identifier lookup tables, directly in the server instructions: the system-prompt text a server hands to the host application. When a query concerns an entry of the embedded table, the model can act on it immediately instead of re-discovering the same information through a search tool. We test whether client LLMs actually consume such instruction-embedded data, reporting a 54,000-trial study across 24 LLMs (9 Claude, 6 Gemini, 9 GPT) on a production legal-information MCP server. A diagnostic condition that removes the competing search tool shows that failures are dominated by behavioral preference rather than missing capability. With search unavailable, 23 of 24 models read the embedded data reliably (hit ratio at least 98%); with a search tool merely present, 9 models drop below 15%. A 2^3 factorial analysis of three instruction-level interventions reveals strong interaction effects: combining all three restores at least 86% for 20 of 24 models, but individual interventions can backfire for specific model families. Per-server prompt engineering is therefore a workaround rather than a fix; we argue that MCP host applications should provide an explicit mechanism that places server instructions ahead of tool selection in the client LLM's deliberation.

</details>


### [135] [Aero Realtime: Fully Aligned Input-Output Streams for Low-Latency Streaming Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08469)
*Kaichen Zhang,Wei Huang,Keming Wu,Bo Li,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Aero Realtime是一个4B参数的双工（duplex）流式多模态模型，通过将视频、音频、文本对齐到共享时间网格（约80 ms的音频slot）实现输入与输出同步，使用单一自回归目标同时学习‘何时响应’和‘生成何物’，并在推理时只追加最新slot、沿用先前状态及KV缓存以实现高效增量计算，在4块NVIDIA A6000上保持低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式多模态模型通常采用先填充(prefill)再解码(decode)的回合式流程，无法在主动生成时自然引入新观测；已有的‘微回合轮询’或外部响应门会破坏连续性并使KV-cache友好性受损，需一种能同步输入输出且便于高效服务的新方法。

Method: 将视频、音频、文本映射到共享时序格（每个音频slot约80 ms），每个slot预测一个词元或静音；使用单一自回归目标同时训练响应时机与生成内容；推理时仅追加最新slot、传递先前输出状态并复用KV缓存；并配套实时QA构造、slot对齐监督、硬件感知分布式训练与可恢复推理方案。

Result: 在4块NVIDIA A6000工作站GPU上，对连续流视频进行20分钟测试时，Aero Realtime实现了84 ms的中位处理延迟和173 ms的P95延迟，总体延迟保持在源时间线200 ms以内，验证了模型的实时性与效率。

Conclusion: 完全对齐的输入-输出建模能够实现双工、主动且硬件友好的实时多模态交互；Aero Realtime在资源可得的工作站级GPU上展示了可行且低延迟的端到端流式生成能力。

Abstract: Existing streaming multimodal models process observations incrementally but still follow a turn-based prefill-then-decode pattern, making them non-duplex: new observations cannot naturally enter an active generation stream. Proactive alternatives use micro-turn polling or external response gates, which fragment continuous interaction, decouple response timing from language generation, and complicate KV-cache-friendly serving. We introduce Aero Realtime, a 4B streaming multimodal model with a duplex architecture for realtime generation. Aero Realtime aligns video, audio, and textual output on a shared temporal grid, where each approximately 80-ms audio slot predicts either a lexical token or a silence token. This allows input and output to advance together, enabling one autoregressive objective to learn both when to respond and what to generate. During inference, Aero Realtime appends only the newest multimodal slot, carries forward the previous output state, and reuses the KV cache for efficient incremental execution. We further provide a complete training and serving recipe, including realtime QA construction, slot-aligned supervision, hardware-aware distributed training, and resumable inference. On four NVIDIA A6000 workstation GPUs, Aero Realtime maintains 84-ms median and 173-ms P95 processing lag over 20 minutes of a continuously streamed video, remaining within 200~ms of the source timeline. These results demonstrate the feasibility of fully aligned input-output modeling for duplex, proactive, and hardware-aligned multimodal interaction.

</details>


### [136] [Yesterday's Shield, Today's Spear: A Self-Evolving Safety Guardrail in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08471)
*Cong Ming,Jingyi Chen,Bin Liu,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu,Yingfei Xiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SESG is a production multi-agent self-evolving safety pipeline that detects novel jailbreaks and harmful categories in live traffic, synthesizes and validates targeted training data, and rapidly deploys updates—cutting response time and human effort while improving performance versus static and adaptive baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deployed safety guardrails are static and quickly outpaced by new jailbreak techniques and emergent harmful content categories; an automated, self-evolving pipeline is needed to keep defenses current with low human overhead.

Method: A multi-agent system monitors live traffic and flags two failure classes: newly-formed jailbreaks and novel harmful-content categories. Upon human confirmation, a generation agent synthesizes paired training examples targeted at the failure; a validation agent rebalances and filters the batch toward the directions the deployed model fails on; a routing agent selects the appropriate training action and returns the updated guardrail model. The pipeline iterates (V0→V6) with short cycles (16–24 hours per new threat) and limited human effort (~2 hours per cycle).

Result: Over six live evolution rounds, a 1.7B-parameter guardrail adapted to a new threat within 16–24 hours using ~2 hours human time, outperforming static guardrails (0.6B–9B) and an adaptive baseline on six emergent threats while retaining general screening capability. In production since April 2026, SESG autonomously closed 14/15 new threats in two months. Nine test sets for six threats were released.

Conclusion: SESG demonstrates that a production multi-agent pipeline can autonomously detect novel jailbreaks and harmful categories in live traffic, generate targeted synthetic training data, validate and rebalance that data toward model errors, and deploy updated guardrails rapidly—substantially reducing human time and improving robustness compared to static guardrails.

Abstract: Deployed LLM safety guardrails are predominantly static: trained once and frozen at release, while new jailbreak techniques and previously un-addressed harmful categories emerge within days, leaving the defense perpetually a step behind. We present SESG (Self-Evolving Safety Guardrails), a multi-agent system running in production. SESG monitors the live traffic behind a deployed guardrail and surfaces two classes of failure: jailbreaks novel in form and harmful categories novel in content. Once a failure is confirmed, a generation agent synthesizes paired training data targeted at it; a validation agent rebalances the batch toward the direction in which the deployed model errs, so that the model's own mistakes steer its training set; and a routing agent matches the training action to the diagnosed gap and returns the next version to production. Over six rounds of live evolution (V0 to V6), a 1.7B guardrail adapts to a new threat in 16-24 hours, with about 2 hours of human effort, versus the 40-90 hours of the manual process it replaces. On six emerging threats, it outperforms static guardrails from 0.6B to 9B and an adaptive baseline while preserving its general screening competence. Since April 2026, SESG has been the primary update pipeline of Sangfor's guardrail, autonomously closing 14 of 15 new threat scenarios in two months. We release 9 test sets for the 6 new threats at https://github.com/Trams1017/SESG. Warning: This paper contains examples that may be harmful or offensive.

</details>


### [137] [HoloAegis: Frozen Representation, Topological Inference: Minimally Parametric Safety Manifolds for Zero-Shot LLM Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08485)
*Tak Ho Alex Li,Kaijie Liu,Lik-Hang Lee,Kin Chung Ho,Ping Shum,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HoloAegis用冻结编码器+稀疏锚点与自由能评估实现零微调、低延迟的安全判定，声称在多项基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全防护方法要么通过微调破坏预训练表示，要么依赖生成式评判器造成高推理成本。目标是保留预训练语义并用几何/拓扑不变性实现高效鲁棒的安全判定。

Method: 1) 使用未微调的编码器将文本归一化到单位球；2) 预先构建锚点库（锚点数量K为唯一可调参数之一）；3) 对输入与锚点集计算基于Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能的得分；4) 使用双时尺度指数移动平均（EMA）监测多轮对话中的语义漂移；5) 根据自由能阈值或边界稳定判据输出安全/风险判定。无梯度训练，推理复杂度主要为O(K)相似性计算。

Result: 论文提出HoloAegis，一种基于冻结语义表示的最小参数拓扑推理框架。核心思想是在不微调编码器的前提下，将文本映射到单位球面，并通过预先构建的锚点簇（System Topology Anchor Bank）与基于Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能的判定函数进行纯几何决策；引入双时尺度指数移动平均用于检测多轮语义漂移；提出并实证支持“拓扑边界稳定性猜想”，认为稀疏锚点中心对抗高频词汇扰动更稳定。实验在8个基准上取得优异AUC成绩并具备低延迟与跨语种能力。

Conclusion: 该方法若能经他人复现，将为LLM安全提供一种计算开销低且对词表扰动鲁棒的新范式；但关键细节（锚点构建、参数选择、理论证明和数据/基准描述）需要进一步澄清与验证。

Abstract: Current LLM safety guardrails face a fundamental tension: fine-tuning distorts pre-trained representations while generative judges incur prohibitive inference costs. We challenge the prevailing paradigm by asking: can safety be achieved through pure geometric reasoning over frozen semantic representations? We present HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that decouples representation from reasoning. We term our approach minimally parametric because the only free parameters are the anchor count K and the temperature tau, both fixed after construction and requiring no gradient-based training. An un-fine-tuned encoder maps text to a unit sphere, after which all decisions are purely geometric. We formalize safety evaluation as a Gibbs-Boltzmann Free Energy computation over a pre-computed System Topology Anchor Bank, and we introduce Dual Time-Scale Exponential Moving Averages to detect progressive multi-turn semantic drift. Our key theoretical insight is a Topological Boundary Stability Conjecture: we provide theoretical motivation and strong empirical evidence that sparse anchor centroids stabilize the decision boundary against high-frequency lexical perturbations far better than full vector space methods. Evaluated across 8 benchmarks, HoloAegis achieves state-of-the-art accuracy (1.0000 AUC on AuthenHallu, 0.9802 on HarmBench) with sub-millisecond latency, zero cold-start data, and cross-lingual transfer (0.9758 AUC on Chinese CHIFRAUD).

</details>


### [138] [TrustRoboReward: Preference-Ordered Isotonic Score Editing for Multi-Paradigm Robot Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08491)
*Yidong Wang,Yan Zhan,Ziteng Feng,Zhenyu Cui,Ziyi Zhou,Renzhao Liang,Jiaxuan Zhu,Zilei Yang,Yiran Zhao,Zhongkuan Mao,Bo Jia,Hanchu Ni,Chenggang Xie,Biao Liu,Yi Zhang,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在多范式数据上用有序各向同性编辑校准点式分数，TrustRoboReward显著减少评分与偏好冲突，从而提升视觉奖励模型的一致性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）奖励判别器（如RoboReward）依赖粗粒度点式分数且缺乏成对偏好，导致奖励模型在长时序机器人操控任务中表现受限与视频场景理解不足；成对标注更贴近人类判断但与现有点式标签不一致。

Method: 构建四范式数据集（Score-A/Score-B/Pair-A/Pair-B），提出Preference-Ordered Isotonic Score Editing (POISE)对点式分数进行有序性校准以消除与成对偏好的分数-对冲突，并在训练/推理中结合TrustJudge聚合进行对比评估。

Result: 理论上POISE将分数-对冲突率从20.15%降至0%（TrustJudge仍为20.46%）；实证上，Qwen3-VL-4B+POISE在基准上总体得分77.96%接近GPT-5-mini(78.09%)并超越RoboReward-4B基线10.13%，测试时分数-对一致性提升至71.90%，使用TrustJudge推理聚合后总体得分升至78.57%。

Conclusion: 本文提出TrustRoboReward与POISE，通过对点对齐与偏好约束的联合编辑，解决点式评分与成对偏好冲突，显著提高机器人长期操作任务的奖励判别一致性和评估性能。

Abstract: Reward models are a bottleneck for reinforcement learning in embodied AI. Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires scalable vision feedback beyond handcrafted rewards or task-specific annotations. Existing open-source VLM reward judges like RoboReward adopt simple 1--5 trajectory progress scoring, lacking pairwise preferences for RLHF, DPO and Bradley-Terry frameworks, while failing to optimize video scene understanding. Augmenting RoboReward with pairwise comparison and video-QA supervision causes inconsistency between pairwise preferences and pointwise scores, introducing training noise and hurting downstream performance---an issue aggregation methods such as TrustJudge cannot resolve. To address this, we propose TrustRoboReward, a multi-paradigm reward modeling framework equipped with Preference-Ordered Isotonic Score Editing (POISE). We construct a unified four-paradigm dataset with trajectory progress scoring (Score-A), video-QA answer quality scoring (Score-B), and their pairwise counterparts (Pair-A, Pair-B). Pairwise labels align better with human judgment than pointwise scores, inspiring us to calibrate pointwise scores to avoid score-pair reversals against pairwise preferences. POISE rectifies pointwise scores and eliminates cross-paradigm reversal conflicts unresolved by TrustJudge. Theoretically, POISE reduces score-pair reversal conflicts from 20.15% to 0%, whereas TrustJudge retains 20.46% conflicts on the same corpus. Evaluated on our benchmark, Qwen3-VL-4B trained with POISE achieves an overall reward score of 77.96%, nearly matching GPT-5-mini (78.09%, gap 0.13%) and outperforming the strongest RoboReward-4B baseline by 10.13%. It also lifts test-time score-pair consistency to 71.90%, exceeding RoboReward-4B (57.26%) and GPT-5-mini (68.09%). Integrating TrustJudge aggregation during inference boosts the overall score to 78.57%, surpassing the GPT-5-mini teacher model.

</details>


### [139] [MathShikkha: A Controlled Study of Answer-Only and Chain-of-Thought Supervision for Bangla Mathematical Reasoning in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08503)
*Rahma Simin Ali,Jawad Hossain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在孟加拉语数学推理上，教师生成的 CoT 对小模型与外域/更大基准显著有利，但对较强模型的原域准确率无明显提升；主要收益是语言一致性、可审计推理与外域鲁棒性，而非普遍提高原域推理质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在低资源语言（孟加拉语）中，教师生成的 Chain-of-Thought 理由监督是否能超越传统答案-only 微调，提高模型的数学推理性能与可解释性。

Method: 用 GPT-5.4 生成推理理由构建 MathShikkha 数据集；在严格匹配的训练/评估协议下微调四个 4B–7B 模型，答案-only 与 CoT 条件共享数据拆分、响应掩码、解码与评分，仅训练目标不同。统计检验包括配对自助法置信区间、McNemar 检验与人工标注（两位作者 + 外部专家，Cohen's κ=0.76–1.00）。对外域基准 BanglaMATH 进行了污染审计。

Result: 在低资源语言（孟加拉语）上比较教师生成的 Chain-of-Thought（CoT）监督与答案-only 微调的效果：构建 MathShikkha（含 GPT-5.4 生成的推理理由），在受控匹配协议下对四个 4B–7B 学生模型进行微调。发现：在原始（in-domain）数据上，CoT 对三款较强模型未显著优于答案-only（置信区间和 McNemar 测试均包括零/不显著），但显著提升最弱的 4B 模型（+18.56 点，p<0.0001）。在更大且经过污染审计的 BanglaMATH 基准上，CoT 对所有四个模型均显著优于答案-only（+20.1–28.1 点，均 p<0.0001）。答案-only 微调在外域任务上使三模型表现低于基线，而 CoT 则保持或提升所有四模型的外域准确率。人工判定显示 CoT 并未在推理内容质量上明显优于基线，其主要可量化作用是提升目标语言遵从性和生成可审计的推理。总体结论：理由监督的价值取决于骨干模型能力和分布转移；其主要收益体现在语言一致性、可审计性和外域鲁棒性，而非必然提升原域推理有效性。

Conclusion: 理由（rationale/CoT）监督的效用依赖于模型规模与分布差异：小/能力有限模型与面对分布转移时能显著获益；对于能力较强模型，在原域数据上可能无增益但可带来目标语言遵从与可审计性。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning remains challenging in low-resource languages such as Bangla. We study whether teacher-generated Bangla Chain-of-Thought (CoT) supervision provides benefits beyond ordinary supervised fine-tuning. We construct \textsc{MathShikkha}, a Bangla mathematical reasoning dataset with GPT-5.4-generated rationales, and fine-tune four 4B--7B student models under a matched protocol in which answer-only and CoT conditions share data splits, response-only loss masking, decoding, and scoring, differing only in the training target. In-domain, CoT provides no significant improvement over answer-only fine-tuning for three stronger backbones (paired bootstrap 95\% CIs include zero; exact McNemar $p \geq 0.17$), despite generating 15--52$\times$ more tokens, but significantly improves the weaker 4B model by 18.56 points ($p < 0.0001$). On the larger, contamination-audited BanglaMATH benchmark, this pattern reverses: CoT significantly outperforms answer-only supervision for all four models by 20.1--28.1 points (all $p < 0.0001$). Answer-only fine-tuning also reduces out-of-domain accuracy below the base model for three models, whereas CoT preserves or improves it for all four. A human study with two co-author annotators, external-expert adjudication, and Cohen's $κ= 0.76$--$1.00$ finds no significant CoT improvement over the base model on reasoning-content criteria; instead, its measurable effect is target-language adherence and producing inspectable reasoning. Overall, rationale supervision's value depends on backbone capability and distribution shift: in this setting, its main benefits are Bangla adherence, auditable reasoning, and out-of-domain robustness rather than improved in-domain reasoning validity.

</details>


### [140] [Understanding Calibration and Truncation Error Propagation in Training-Free Low-Rank Compression for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08506)
*Mohanad Odema,Gabrielle De Micheli,Dayin Gou,Nilesh Malpeddi,Prathamesh Vaste,Jacob Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种训练-free的低秩压缩改进方法，通过分层压缩与校准修正以及迭代秩分配修正，减少压缩时激活误差累积与层重要性分布变化带来的错配，从而提升压缩后LLM的零样本任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练-free低秩压缩存在两类关键问题：压缩过程使用的校准激活残差会在层间累积导致推理时表示偏移；以及压缩改变了层重要性分布，破坏了原先按重要性分配秩的假设。两者共同导致压缩时与部署模型之间的错配，影响下游性能。

Method: 在现有SOTA矩阵分解框架上加入两步：1) 分层压缩（Layer-by-Layer Compression）配合校准修正：每层压缩时用校准数据修正激活残差以减少后续层的误配；2) 迭代压缩与秩分配修正（Iterative Compression with Rank Allocation Correction）：循环地调整各层分解秩以反映压缩后层重要性的变化，直至收敛。该方法无需权重微调。

Result: 在Llama与Qwen3不同规模和多个基准上的实验显示：在各种压缩率下，相较于按权重分解与联合分解基线，本文方法在零样本任务上可获得约0.5–2.5个百分点的准确率提升，表明校准与秩修正能有效缓解表示错配。

Conclusion: 在不额外训练的前提下，通过校准修正与迭代秩分配两项简单机制，可以显著缓解压缩过程中的表示错配，取得比按权重或联合分解基线高约1–2.5个准确率点的提升，适用于Llama与Qwen3等模型。

Abstract: Training-free low-rank compression frameworks have been gaining prominence for LLM compression given their effectiveness in reducing model parameter count while maintaining task-level accuracy. However, existing SOTA frameworks share two key limitations: (1) residual errors in calibration data activations accumulate across layers during compression, causing misalignment between representations simulated at compression time and those experienced at inference; (2) the assumption that layer importance distribution is preserved post-compression does not hold. Together, these two effects introduce misalignment in the compression process in relation to the deployed model. We study these effects and propose a simple, training-free methodology compatible with existing frameworks to mitigate them, comprising: (1) Layer-by-Layer Compression with Calibration Correction; (2) Iterative Compression with Rank Allocation Correction. Implemented atop an existing SOTA decomposition framework, and evaluated on Llama and Qwen3 models across various benchmarks and compression rates, our approach demonstrates up to ~1-2.5 accuracy point improvements over per-weight and joint decomposition baselines on zero-shot tasks.

</details>


### [141] [Time Present and Time Past: Benchmarking Large Language Models on Temporally Evolving Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08512)
*Mahbub E Sobhani,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Fahmid Hasan Chowdhury,Md Adnan Arefeen,Farig Sadeque,Md. Faizul Bari,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TIDE is a new benchmark testing version-resolution in temporal QA on real evolving legal documents; current LLMs perform poorly, especially at rejecting incorrect versions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This paper addresses temporal ambiguity in QA over evolving legal and official documents, where different answers are correct at different dates and amendments explicitly replace earlier text; existing datasets don't test version resolution.

Method: Created expert-verified dataset from Bangladeshi customs instruments (1969–2025), annotated date-dependent QA pairs across eight task types; evaluated nine LLMs under standardized protocol with three-judge LLM council and a hard date gate separating correctness of meaning vs time.

Result: They release TIDE: a benchmark of 3,050 QA pairs over 644 Bangladeshi customs instruments (1969–2025), expert-verified, code-mixed, with dates in two calendars. They evaluate nine LLMs across parametric, gold-context, and retrieval settings; best macro accuracy 68.5%; resolving implicit dates 59.7%; detecting supplied wrong-version 26.7%. Models favor parametric answers and often fail to reject incorrect supplied versions. Data and code released.

Conclusion: Version resolution remains unsolved: models often choose wrong temporal versions, rely on parametric knowledge over authoritative context, and struggle with implicit dates and detecting non-governing versions.

Abstract: Evolving documents, such as laws, tax codes, and software documentation, are amended, replaced, and sometimes reverted over time, so a question has different correct answers at different dates. In contrast to encyclopedic knowledge, where an old fact is simply overwritten, an amendment is itself an official text that states what it replaces and when it takes effect, and the earlier version stays correct for its validity period. The central challenge is therefore version resolution, that is, identifying the version in force on the queried date. Existing temporal QA datasets treat time only as an annotation, so version resolution stays untested. We present TIDE, an expert-verified benchmark of 3,050 QA pairs over 644 official customs instruments issued between 1969 and 2025 by the Government of Bangladesh, covering eight task types over deeply code-mixed documents that are heterogeneous in layout and dated in two calendars. In addition, we evaluate nine recent LLMs under a single protocol across parametric, gold-context, and retrieval access, scored by a three-judge LLM council with a hard date gate separating correct meaning from correct time. The best macro-averaged accuracy is only 68.5%. Resolving a version from an implicit date reaches 59.7%, and detecting that the supplied version does not govern the query reaches only 26.7%. Models are more likely to find correct versions than to reject incorrect ones, and they tend to follow a confident parametric answer over the supplied authoritative text. All code and data are available at https://github.com/icsetepa44/TIDE

</details>


### [142] [Reproducing and Stress-Testing Two Approaches to LLM Reasoning Reliability: Test-Time Probability Aggregation and Logic-Representation Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08514)
*Minhan Cho,Jimin Kweon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Reproduced two LLM reliability methods (RPC and LCF). RPC’s reported gains over self-consistency do not generalize across four new domains and lose significance with larger sampling; LCF’s ‘logic’ direction exists but is weak and its positive effects are inconsistent across models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Original claims were tested only by each method’s authors, with no independent reproduction, limited domains/models, and missing LCF code; independent stress-testing is necessary to validate reliability claims before adoption.

Method: Re-implemented RPC’s published-path aggregation and re-created LCF’s projector/contrastive/intervention pipeline; evaluated RPC across four new task domains with Qwen3-8B and LCF across four 7–8B models; probed representations for separability and measured statistical significance of effects.

Result: RPC reproduces original grid on authors’ released paths but shows no significant advantage over self-consistency on new domains (paired p ≥ 0.28); on BIRD RPC’s advantage grows with K but reverses when sample size increases (Δ +2.5 at K=32, p=0.16; becomes –0.25 at n=200). LCF’s best sub-layer separability is 0.82 vs 0.95 for a semantic control; only one non-significant positive effect (Qwen3 ΔProb, p=0.56) while significantly reducing ΔProb on two other models.

Conclusion: Neither method shows robust, generalizable improvements across models and domains: RPC’s edge is sample-size- and domain-dependent and not reliably significant; LCF identifies a weak separable ‘logic’ subspace but yields inconsistent and often negative effects when intervened on.

Abstract: We independently reproduce two recent methods for making large language model (LLM) reasoning more reliable, and stress-test them across domains and models (RPC across four new task domains with Qwen3-8B, LCF across four 7-8B models). The first, RPC, aggregates token probabilities and self-consistency at inference; the second, LCF, trains projectors that split hidden states into "content" and "logic" and edits the logic part toward a valid region. Validating such reliability claims matters because the original evaluations are run by each method's own authors and were never independently reproduced or stress-tested across models and domains, and LCF shipped no public code. We re-run RPC's published-path aggregation and re-implement LCF's projector, contrastive, and intervention pipeline, then extend both to text-to-SQL, legal extraction, fallacy identification, and precedent grading, and probe LCF's representation directly. RPC reproduces the original grid exactly on the authors' released reasoning paths; on four new domains its edge over self-consistency is never significant (ties or small mixed differences, paired p >= 0.28), and on BIRD, the one domain where we vary the budget, the edge grows with K as predicted but its largest gap (+2.5 accuracy at K=32, p=0.16) reverses to -0.25 when we enlarge the sample to n=200. LCF's logic-validity direction is real but weak (0.82 separability at the single best sub-layer versus 0.95 for a semantic-attribute control); its one positive effect (Qwen3 $Δ$Prob) is not significant (p=0.56), while it significantly reduces $Δ$Prob on two of the other three models.

</details>


### [143] [Discovering Diverse Planning Policies for Multimodal Embodied Agents with Quality-Diversity Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08523)
*Pengfei Xu,Yong Liu,Xiaoya Nan,Qiang Yang,Peilan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Proposes a QD-based method that evolves diverse multimodal planning policies, organizes them in a behavior-indexed archive, and switches policies online upon stalls, improving task success and efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multimodal embodied agents struggle with long-horizon tasks because they often use a single planning style; when that style fails the agent stalls. The paper aims to mitigate this by discovering diverse planning policies to avoid stall and improve adaptability.

Method: Treat planning-policy templates as evolvable individuals; summarize rollouts into structured success/failure experiences; guide recombination and experience-guided mutation; map policies into behavior space (interaction intensity and goal-directedness); retain highest-quality policy per niche; online execution with monitoring and rollback-switch upon stall.

Result: A Quality-Diversity framework that evolves planning-policy templates, summarizes rollouts into success/failure experiences for guided variation, maps policies into a behavior space of interaction intensity and goal-directedness, and retains high-quality policies per niche. Online, it detects stalls and switches to behaviorally distinct policies after rollback. Experiments on ThreeDWorld transport benchmark show improved success and interaction efficiency over baselines.

Conclusion: Discovering diverse policy repertoires via a QD framework supports adaptive multimodal planning and online failure recovery, outperforming representative baseline planners on a benchmark.

Abstract: Multimodal embodied agents are increasingly required to solve long-horizon tasks by integrating visual observations, textual goals, and interaction history into closed-loop decision making. However, state-of-the-art large-model-based planners often rely on a single dominant planning style during execution. Once this execution mode becomes ineffective, the agent may remain stalled for many steps, repeatedly interacting with the environment without making meaningful progress. We address this limitation by proposing a Quality-Diversity (QD) framework for discovering diverse planning policies for multimodal embodied agents. The proposed method treats planning-policy templates as evolvable individuals and organizes them into a behavior-indexed archive rather than collapsing search to a single prompt style. In the offline stage, rollout trajectories are summarized into structured success and failure experiences, which guide policy variation through recombination and experience-guided mutation. The resulting policies are mapped into a behavior space defined by interaction intensity and goal-directedness, and the highest-quality policy in each niche is retained in the archive. In the online stage, the agent executes one policy at a time while monitoring task progress. When persistent stall is detected, the system rolls back to the latest checkpoint and switches to a behaviorally distinct archive policy to resume execution. Experiments on the ThreeDWorld transport benchmark show that the proposed framework improves both task success and interaction efficiency over representative baseline planners. These results suggest that discovering diverse policy repertoires is an effective way to support adaptive multimodal planning and online failure recovery.

</details>


### [144] [Deep probabilistic logic programming for diagnostic reasoning from incomplete information: A case study in stroke detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08561)
*Felix Weitkämper,Monchito Avila,Elizabeth Nanjala,Siska,Grace Zawadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并验证了一个用最大熵补全文献统计、借助ProbLog 2和DeepProbLog构建可解释神经符号诊断系统的工作流，并分析了信息不完整和模型压缩的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始医疗数据隐私问题限制数据直接共享，文献中的汇总统计更易获得；因此需设计能基于汇总统计训练且保持可解释性的诊断方法；DeepProbLog提供神经网络与概率逻辑融合的框架，适用于透明且有因果语义的诊断推理。

Method: 采用最大熵技术在不完整统计信息下构造概率性因果模型；在ProbLog 2下形式化该模型并将其映射为可在DeepProbLog中表达的判别性神经符号模型（集成神经网络处理视觉/听觉传感器数据）；对来自不同完整度数据的模型进行比较实验；探索ProbFOIL 2对大型判别模型的压缩效果。

Result: 证明了从汇总统计出发构建可工作的神经符号诊断模型是可行的：最大熵补全和ProbLog 2能生成合理的因果模型，转换到DeepProbLog后可处理多模态输入；信息缺失会降低性能但仍可得到可用模型；ProbFOIL 2在压缩方面展示潜力；并讨论了方法在可解释性、可验证性和临床部署方面的利弊。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一条从文献汇总统计到基于DeepProbLog的诊断系统的可行路径，通过最大熵方法补全概率信息、用ProbLog 2建立熵最大化的因果模型并转换成判别性神经符号模型，评估了信息不完全时模型性能并探讨了用ProbFOIL 2压缩判别模型的潜力，最后讨论了将DeepProbLog用于诊断推理的观点与影响。

Abstract: In medical applications, raw data is frequently associated with significant privacy concerns, lending particular importance to the encoding of summary statistics from the literature. On the other hand, deep learning has become an invaluable tool for assessing symptoms based on visual or auditory sensor data. DeepProbLog allows for an extensible neuro-symbolic approach that accommodates connectionist components to analyse patient images within a transparent and rigorous probabilistic framework, namely probabilistic logic programming under the distribution semantics. Framed as a case study in stroke detection from multimodal data, this contribution explores the pathway from summary statistics available in the literature to a DeepProbLog-based diagnostic system. It suggests a workflow using established maximum entropy techniques to complete available probabilistic information and the probabilistic logic programming system ProbLog 2 to move from the entropy-maximising causal model to a discriminative neuro-symbolic model expressible within DeepProbLog. The relative performance of models derived from less complete data is analysed alongside the potential of the probabilistic inductive logic programming system ProbFOIL 2 for compressing large discriminative models, and the perspectives and implications of using DeepProbLog for diagnostic reasoning are discussed.

</details>


### [145] [VoxZip: Semantic-Anchored Temporal KV Cache Compression for Long-Context Audio Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08569)
*Wenxu Jia,Dongjie Fu,Xize Cheng,Fangming Feng,Linjun Li,Wenshi Chen,Yingming Li,Zhou Zhao,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VoxZip，一种免训练的两阶段语义锚定KV缓存压缩框架：第一阶段用ASR转录作为语义锚点进行时间对齐、压缩和融合音频token；第二阶段用基于时间衰减累积注意力的动态过滤逐出非必要token以缓解早期token偏置。在Qwen3-Omni与六个音频基准上的评测显示，在长上下文场景下20x压缩仍保留90%以上性能；4x压缩可实现1.9x推理吞吐提升与3.3x峰值内存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Speech LLM在长上下文推理时因KV缓存内存占用过大导致的性能与效率瓶颈，同时保留语音连续性与语义信息，避免传统文本压缩方法带来的语音信息损失。

Method: 两阶段：1）ASR语义锚点驱动的时间对齐、压缩与融合音频tokens以提升信息密度；2）基于时间衰减的累积注意力动态过滤，逐出低重要性token并减少早期token偏置。该方法无需训练，可与现有模型（如Qwen3-Omni）结合使用。

Result: 在Qwen3-Omni与六个音频基准上，VoxZip在20x KV缓存压缩下仍保留>90%未压缩基线性能；4x压缩时带来1.9x推理吞吐率提升与3.3x峰值内存降低，且在短音频任务中维持高保真度。

Conclusion: VoxZip在长音频推理场景下能大幅压缩KV缓存同时保持高性能与高保真短音频感知，兼具训练自由和显著的吞吐/内存优势。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Speech Large Language Models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in understanding complex audio tasks. Despite this progress, their long-context inference remains severely bottlenecked by prohibitive KV cache memory demands. Existing text-centric compression methods struggle here, often disrupting speech continuity or discarding crucial semantic cues. To address this, we propose VoxZip, a train-free, two-stage semantic-anchored KV cache compression framework. The first stage uses automatic speech recognition (ASR) transcriptions as explicit semantic anchors to temporally align, compress, and fuse audio tokens, significantly reducing the initial KV cache while elevating token information density. To further improve the compression ratio, the second stage employs a dynamic filtering strategy based on temporally decayed accumulated attention to evict non-essential tokens while mitigating early-token bias. Comprehensive evaluations on Qwen3-Omni across six diverse audio benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of our approach. VoxZip excels in long-audio reasoning and consistently maintains high-fidelity perception on short-form tasks. Notably, it sustains over 90\% of the uncompressed baseline performance even under an aggressive 20x KV cache compression in long-context scenarios. Furthermore, at a 4x compression ratio, VoxZip yields a 1.9x increase in inference throughput alongside a 3.3x reduction in peak memory overhead. Code and models will be available at https://github.com/MM-Speech/VoxZip.

</details>


### [146] [FailForge: Distilling Procedural Competence from Persistent Failures into Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08570)
*Dongyi Lv,Fushun E,Aichen Cai,Liang Huang,Ya Zhang,Qiuyu Ding,Canhui Wu,Zhi Wang,Yuesong Zhang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用失败样例自动诊断并蒸馏为可注入的技能以引导重试，将成功路径并入训练语料，从而把丢弃的高价值样本变为可学习信号，显著提升了最难可验证任务的解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的拒绝采样微调（RFT）丢弃失败样例，而这些样例通常是最难且信息量最大的；通过回收这些失败并将其转化为可学习的信号，可以提升模型在最难可验证软件工程任务上的表现。

Method: 提出了FailForge——一个将失败回滚转换为训练信号的智能体框架。对于每个失败样例，智能体根据错误反馈和执行轨迹诊断失败原因，将诊断结果蒸馏为简洁且可操作的“技能”，将该技能注入模型上下文以引导第二次尝试；成功的受引导轨迹被纳入RFT语料库，但在训练时移除技能提示，使模型内化行为而非依赖外部提示。

Result: FailForge在边际额外成本下恢复了超过26%的先前失败实例；使用扩充语料训练Qwen3.5-4B，在SWE-bench的Verified解决率上比强RFT基线提升了6.6个百分点，改进主要集中在最难的问题上。

Conclusion: FailForge能有效将失败转化为训练增益，提升模型在困难可验证任务上的能力；通过在训练时移除技能提示，方法促使模型内化 recovered 行为而非依赖提示。

Abstract: Rejection sampling fine-tuning (RFT) is widely used to train code agents by generating trajectories on verifiable software engineering tasks, retaining those that pass the tests, and fine-tuning on the successful rollouts. However, even strong code agents repeatedly fail on a substantial fraction of such tasks, and standard RFT simply discards these failures. The discarded samples are precisely the hardest and most informative ones, drawn from verifiable instances that are costly to curate. Stronger base models may reduce the number of failures, but the remaining hard cases still define the frontier for further improvement. We propose FailForge, an agentic framework that converts failed rollouts into training signal. For each failed instance, an agent diagnoses the failure from error feedback and execution traces, distills the diagnosis into a concise and actionable skill, and injects the skill into the agent context for a guided second attempt. Trajectories that succeed under skill guidance are folded back into the RFT corpus. Crucially, the skill is removed at training time, so the model internalizes the recovered behavior rather than relying on external hints at inference. FailForge recovers over 26% of previously failed instances at marginal additional cost, and training Qwen3.5-4B on the augmented corpus improves the SWE-bench Verified resolve rate by 6.6 points over a strong RFT baseline, with gains concentrated on the hardest problems.

</details>


### [147] [SDDBMs: Soft Denoising Diffusion Bridge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08594)
*Shiyi Qi,Kun He,Mingmou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SDDBMs soften endpoint conditioning by prescribing a Gaussian terminal marginal, yielding closed-form soft h-transforms and stable bridge dynamics; this unifies prior bridge methods and empirically improves stability and image restoration quality.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hard endpoint conditioning in diffusion bridges forces the terminal law to a Dirac measure, causing terminal-boundary singularities and ill-conditioned drift coefficients that harm numerical stability and sampling quality. Regularizing the terminal constraint should mitigate these issues.

Method: Introduce Soft Denoising Diffusion Bridge Models (SDDBMs): replace hard endpoint conditioning by prescribing a non-degenerate Gaussian terminal marginal under the transformed path measure; derive closed-form expressions for the Gaussian terminal reweighting and soft h-function, forward marginals, and x_0-free dynamics; provide sampling and modelling recipes based on these closed forms.

Result: A generalized, closed-form framework (SDDBMs) that subsumes existing bridge models (DDBMs, GOUB, UniDB) as special cases, offers improved numerical stability and better generation quality on image restoration tasks, and allows flexible terminal center and variance choices.

Conclusion: Soft terminal constraints provide a principled, analytically tractable way to avoid terminal-boundary singularities in diffusion bridges; SDDBMs form a flexible family that both explains earlier models and improves practical performance, while opening directions for adaptive terminal priors and broader non-Gaussian generalizations.

Abstract: Diffusion bridge models leverage Doob's \(h\)-transform to construct stochastic transports between arbitrary endpoint distributions, and have shown strong potential in image-to-image translation and restoration. However, most existing bridge models rely on hard endpoint conditioning, which forces the terminal state to match a prescribed target exactly. This hard constraint induces terminal-boundary singularities: the terminal law collapses to a Dirac measure, and the resulting drift coefficients become ill-conditioned near the endpoint. In this paper, we propose Soft Denoising Diffusion Bridge Models (SDDBMs), a generalized framework that regularizes diffusion bridges directly at the level of their terminal constraints. Instead of imposing an exact endpoint, SDDBMs prescribe a non-degenerate Gaussian terminal marginal under the transformed path measure, with a flexible terminal center and variance. Starting from this prescribed marginal, we develop a complete closed-form construction of the soft bridge, including the Gaussian terminal reweighting and soft \(h\)-function, the induced Gaussian forward marginals and \(\mathbf{x}_0\)-free dynamics. Theoretically, SDDBMs provide a unified probabilistic perspective that encompasses existing diffusion bridge models, including DDBMs, GOUB, and UniDB, as special cases under specific parameter choices. Extensive experiments on image restoration tasks demonstrate that SDDBMs achieve improved numerical stability and superior generation quality over existing bridge-based methods.

</details>


### [148] [Unaccountable Delegation, Fading Skills: Mapping the Risks of Workplace AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08601)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Lakmal Meegahapola,Edyta Bogucka,Jie M. Zhang,Michael Luck,Elizabeth Black,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者提出基于agent—goal—environment的框架与结构化提示，在O*NET任务上自动生成并验证大量岗位级AI代理风险场景，进而形成15类工作场所AI代理风险分类法，揭示了增强/自动化在风险类型上的差异及人—代理界面风险的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI风险分类侧重广泛风险，缺乏对岗位/任务层面由AI代理引入的社会-技术风险的细粒度刻画，需构建能捕捉人—代理—环境交互所致风险的分类工具。

Method: 构建多层框架（agent—goal—environment），将框架嵌入结构化提示并对O*NET的2,078项任务生成风险场景，场景标注严重度与部署模式；用45名工人和独立LLM判定器验证；在既有分类基础上扩展为15类工作场所AI代理风险分类法，并做可用性比较。

Result: 生成8,356条与岗位任务对齐的风险场景（含严重度与自动化/增强部署标签）；提出覆盖所有场景的15类风险分类法；发现：增强并非天然安全、错误代理行为是最多且最严重的风险、自动化偏组织风险而增强偏劳工风险、工人更偏好本文分类法。

Conclusion: 工作场所AI风险既来自代理本身，也源于人与代理的协作方式与部署策略；降低风险需同时改进代理与人—代理协作设计，并在组织层面制定配套治理措施。

Abstract: To anticipate socio-technical risks from AI agents, organizations need taxonomies to classify them. However, existing AI risk taxonomies focus on broad risks and do not capture job-specific risks introduced by agents. To address this gap, we make three main contributions. First, we developed a multi-layer framework from a literature review of AI agents. The framework models three core components and their interactions: agents, goals, and environment. Second, we embedded this framework in a structured prompt and applied it to descriptions of 2,078 job tasks from the O*NET database, producing 8,356 risk scenarios labeled by severity and deployment mode (automation or augmentation). We validated these scenarios with 45 workers across 10 job roles and an independent LLM judge, confirming their plausibility and alignment with job tasks. Finally, we extended an existing taxonomy to create a 15-category taxonomy of workplace AI agent risks that covers all our risk scenarios. Our analysis highlights four findings. First, augmentation is not inherently safe because overreliance on agents can gradually erode workers' skills and oversight. Second, Erroneous Agent Actions accounts for the largest share of risk scenarios and has the highest concentration of severe risks. Many arise at the human-agent boundary. Third, automation is associated mainly with organizational risks, while augmentation is associated mainly with risks to workers. Fourth, workers found our taxonomy easier to use for a risk classification task than two other taxonomies and preferred it in 64% of non-tied comparisons with a recent generative AI risk taxonomy. These findings show that workplace AI agent risks do not arise from agents alone; they also depend on how people work with agents and how agents are deployed. Safer workplaces require not only safer agents but also carefully designed human-AI agent collaboration.

</details>


### [149] [ForestBench: A Unified Graph Framework for Evaluating Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08605)
*Guo Chen,Ziwen Li,Reed Li,Yu Lu,Haibo Shi,Bingbing Xu,Junjie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ForestBench：把异构MAS执行轨迹统一成协作图并用‘参考森林’作为多样化成功示例集合进行快速、可复用、结构化评价，避免仅凭结果或额外LLM判分的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方式要么只看结果抛弃协作过程（outcome-only），要么依赖额外LLM作为裁判并受模型/量表影响（LLM-as-Judge），缺乏对异构MAS执行轨迹的通用、可重复、无额外推理的评价基线；因此需要一种统一的结构表示与基于多样化成功示例的评估框架。

Method: （1）定义协作图表示以统一异构轨迹，（2）为每个查询构建参考森林——由多种已验证成功的协作图组成，（3）将候选轨迹映射为协作图并在图空间内与参考森林比较以得分，（4）实现ForestBench：筛选需求协作的查询、为每个查询预计算十个参考图、在六个MAS框架上评测，并通过控制实验验证稳定性与鲁棒性。

Result: 提出了一个通用评估框架，将不同多智能体系统（MAS）的本地执行轨迹映射为统一的协作图（collaboration graphs），并引入以查询为条件的参考森林（reference forest）——每个森林包含多种已验证成功的协作图作为参考集合。用该表示来比较候选图与参考森林，构建了具体实现ForestBench：从七个公开数据集中筛选出844个需要协作的查询、为每个查询预构建十个成功目标条件参考图，并在六个代表性MAS框架上进行评估。通过骨干模型、参考构建与扰动控制实验检验评估稳定性。ForestBench在基准森林构建完毕后对轨迹的评分是毫秒级且不需进一步调用LLM推理。

Conclusion: ForestBench提供了一种可重用、快速且通用的结构化评估手段，通过将轨迹映射到统一的协作图空间并与查询特定的参考森林进行比较，实现不同MAS方法在相同表示、参考集和指标面板下的公平比较，但依赖于参考森林的构建质量与覆盖度。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) are proliferating rapidly, but their heterogeneous execution traces provide no common basis for evaluation across methods. Outcome-only benchmarks discard collaborations, whereas LLM-as-Judge evaluation requires additional, model-dependent inference and can vary with the LLM and rubric. We introduce a generalizable evaluation framework that maps native MAS traces into a shared space of unified collaboration graphs, enabling different methods to be evaluated under the same representation, reference set, and metric panel. Candidate graphs are compared with a query-specific reference forest. Each forest is a benchmark-provided collection of verified-success graphs: it records diverse ways in which representative MAS methods can complete the task, rather than prescribing a unique optimal process. Instantiating the framework as ForestBench, we filter $844$ collaboration-necessary queries from seven public datasets, precompute ten successful target-conditioned reference graphs per query, and evaluate six representative MAS frameworks. Controlled backbone, reference-construction, and perturbation studies test the stability and scope of evaluation. Once the benchmark forests are built, ForestBench scores a trace in milliseconds without further LLM inference, providing a reusable structural basis for comparing diverse MAS collaboration traces.

</details>


### [150] [Walking through Discussions: A Mobile Visual Analytics System for In-Situ Group Discussion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08617)
*Yiping Sun,Ziyao Kang,Wei Zeng,Minli Wu,Jiazhi Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileGroupVis通过流式语音分析与小屏可视化，为走动式课堂提供多组监控与单组诊断支持，初步验证了其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理课堂中教师需边走动边同时监控多组讨论，现有工具多为桌面端的事后分析，难以支持现场快速诊断与干预，因而需要专门为小屏与走动式教学场景设计的可视分析工具。

Method: 采用轻量级流式分析管道：音频→结构化讨论数据；对话分析模块提取交互强度、话题进展与偏离等特征；在移动端设计六个协调视图（概览glyph、多种详细视图）并优化触控交互；通过两个案例研究与专家访谈进行使用验证。

Result: 提出了MobileGroupVis，一款用于课堂现场走动教学的移动可视分析系统。该系统通过轻量级流式分析管道将小组音频转成结构化讨论数据，并基于对话分析模块提取交互模式、话题推进和话题偏离；在小屏触控上设计了六个协同视图（含一个压缩型glyph编码：词数、交互强度、话题偏离）以同时支持多组监控与单组诊断，并通过两个案例研究与专家访谈验证了系统在帮助教师了解讨论过程、识别需关注小组及支持课堂干预方面的初步有效性。

Conclusion: 系统为教师在现场同时监控多个讨论小组并快速定位异常提供了实用工具，能辅助课堂干预；但对ASR鲁棒性、隐私、评估广泛性和定量效果验证等方面仍需进一步工作。

Abstract: Group discussion-based teaching is widely used to foster collaborative learning, yet teachers in physical classrooms often struggle to simultaneously monitor multiple groups and quickly diagnose a target group before intervening. Existing visual analytics tools primarily support post-hoc analysis on desktop, providing limited support for in-situ walk-around teaching. To address this gap, we present MobileGroupVis, a mobile visual analytics system for in-situ analysis of classroom group discussions. MobileGroupVis integrates multi-group monitoring, single-group diagnosis, and instructional intervention into a concise analytical workflow tailored for small-screen touch interaction. The system is powered by a lightweight streaming analysis pipeline that converts group audio into structured discussion data and further extracts interaction patterns, topic progression, and topic deviation through a dialogue analysis module. To enable both glanceable overview and traceable diagnosis, we design six coordinated views, including a compact glyph that visually encodes word count, interaction intensity, and topic deviation for efficient cross-group comparison and anomaly localization, along with detailed views for opinion evolution, interaction dynamics, topic coverage, and dialogue records. We evaluate MobileGroupVis through two case studies and expert interviews. The results provide preliminary evidence that MobileGroupVis supports teachers in understanding discussion processes, identifying groups in need of attention, and facilitating in-class intervention.

</details>


### [151] [Business Arena: Benchmarking LLM Agents in a Realistic Marketplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08621)
*Yijun Pan,Yukun Lian,Kunyu Shi,Junbo Li,Hongwei Xue,Sicong Xie,Guannan Zhang,Xiaoying Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Business Arena 提供基于真实数据的长期跨境电商仿真，用利润与细粒度指标评估 LLM 代理，显示现有模型在商业操作上仍落后于人工策略并能揭示具体决策成败原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理基准很少评估商业运营这种有延迟因果、跨期风险和法律/合规约束的复杂工作流，因而需要一个现实、可控且可解释的测试床来衡量 LLM 基础代理的商业能力。

Method: 设计了 Business Arena 仿真平台，让代理在跨境商店中进行长期买卖决策，基于阿里巴巴真实采购数据并用权威来源校准市场条件；对 15 个前沿模型进行端到端评估，并与人工策略比较，使用技能级别指标与动作级归因解析表现，并做机制消融以检验结果可靠性。

Result: 不同模型间最终净资产平均值相差约九倍；最佳模型仍落后于人工设计策略；技能分析显示多样运营风格（高毛利/高周转/客服导向）；动作级归因识别出进货、定价与回收决策为主要价值驱动；机制消融表明优异表现反映真实商业推理而非仿真技巧。

Conclusion: Business Arena 是面向端到端商业代理评估的首个现实且可信的测试床，为量化、可解释的商业智能评估奠定基础，但仍需扩展基线、增强统计检验与开源复现以提升广泛接受度。

Abstract: Running a business is a challenging form of intelligent work. Operators must infer opportunities from partial signals, commit capital under uncertainty, adapt to delayed outcomes in a changing market, and satisfy regulatory obligations before trading legally. Frontier LLM agents can increasingly complete complex workflows, yet business-related capabilities are rarely evaluated in existing agent benchmarks. We introduce \textbf{Business Arena}, a controlled environment where an AI agent runs a cross-border shop, buying from suppliers and selling to buyers over a long horizon. We ground the arena in real Alibaba.com sourcing data and market conditions calibrated from authoritative sources. Delayed and coupled consequences make individual business decisions difficult to judge, but their combined outcome is measurable through profit. Because profit alone cannot explain why an agent succeeds or fails, we compare agents with human-designed strategies to estimate available opportunity, use skill-level metrics to reveal underlying strengths and weaknesses, and trace realized gains and losses to the actions that produced them. We use mechanism ablations to establish that strong results reflect genuine business intelligence rather than neglect or simulator-specific shortcuts. We evaluate 15 frontier models and find a ninefold difference in mean final net worth. Even the best model falls behind human-designed strategies, indicating that business operation remains challenging for LLM agents. Skill-level analysis reveals operating styles, from margin-focused premium sellers to high-turnover wholesalers and customer-service specialists, while action-level attribution identifies the sourcing, pricing, and recovery decisions that create or destroy value. Together, Business Arena takes a first step toward a realistic and trustworthy testbed for evaluating end-to-end business agents.

</details>


### [152] [MedCalc-R1: Knowledge-Guided Reward Framework for Medical Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08623)
*Haotian Wang,Lian Yan,Xingzhi Yao,Fanshu Meng,Ye He,Jingchi Jiang,Yi Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过要求生成可验证的计算公式并结合硬安全阈值与精度敏感的软奖励，MedCalc-R1提高了临床数值推理的可解释性、可靠性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于容差的浮点数奖励在门限选择、训练稳定性和临床场景精度上存在不足；因此引入基于知识的可验证机制与兼顾安全与精度的混合奖励，以提升可靠性与可解释性，满足安全关键域的需求。

Method: 在RL训练中对生成的结果同时施加两类奖励：1) 知识验证奖励——要求模型输出显式计算公式，随后由外部符号/数值验证器检查公式与答案一致性；2) 混合软硬奖励——硬奖励为基于临床安全阈值的指示函数（满足/不满足），软奖励在满足安全阈值的范围内按精度差异给出连续回报，以引导精细化学习。训练可与常用策略梯度或PPO等算法结合。

Result: 提出了MedCalc-R1框架：在RLVR（强化学习带可验证奖励）上下文中，为数学/临床计算任务引入知识验证奖励（要求显式生成计算公式并用外部验证器验证）和混合软硬奖励（硬阈值保证临床安全，软奖励对精度敏感并在可接受范围内提供渐进引导）。在推断精度和泛化性上对比基线方法取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 知识验证奖励+软-硬混合奖励能缓解单纯容差奖励的阈值调参、训练不稳和可解释性差的问题，适用于对安全敏感的临床计算任务，但需要关注外部验证器可靠性、计算开销和奖励设计细节。

Abstract: In Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) frameworks for mathematical reasoning tasks, floating-point results are typically evaluated using a tolerance-based reward. However, this strategy suffers from challenges such as difficulty in threshold calibration, unstable training dynamics, and limited accuracy, especially in clinical scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose a knowledge-guided hybrid reward framework (\textsc{MedCalc-R1}). Specifically, we introduce a knowledge verification reward mechanism that enforces explicit generation of computational formulas, which are further validated by an external verifier to enhance interpretability and reasoning reliability. Furthermore, we design a hybrid soft-hard reward scheme combining a hard constraint based on clinical safety thresholds with a soft, precision-sensitive reward that progressively guides learning within the acceptable range. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms existing baselines in both reasoning accuracy and generalization capability, validating the effectiveness and applicability in safety-critical domains.

</details>


### [153] [UniMoMo: Expert Merging-Based MoE Acceleration for Large Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08627)
*Lei Xin,Bin Gu,Peize Li,Zitong Wang,Jianbo Zhao,Changjiang Jiang,Yanyue Xie,Chao Huang,Xuyang Zhao,Zunhai Su,Fanhu Zeng,Zhenglun Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniMoMo: post-training, calibration-driven functional clustering plus adaptive protection to compress MoE checkpoints under expert budgets, retaining ranking quality while improving serving speed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sparse MoE layers increase model capacity via many experts, but deployed checkpoints still store and route over the full expert bank. There is a need to export trained MoE checkpoints to smaller, budgeted models without adding online compression modules.

Method: UniMoMo formulates post-training MoE compression as constrained graph coarsening: experts are grouped by functional similarity measured on an unlabeled calibration set (response similarity to shared recommendation states), and a layer-adaptive protection mechanism prevents merging high-traffic experts based on routing exposure.

Result: On Amazon Beauty, KuaiRec, and TenRec with 2–6 MoE blocks, converted four-expert checkpoints achieve source-relative mean NDCG@10 ratios of 99.92%–102.30% and A100 speedups 1.28×–1.63×; aggressive two-expert, top-1 points yield NDCG@10 ratios 98.36%–104.24% and speedups 1.47×–2.21×. The full conversion-and-adaptation workflow is evaluated over five runs.

Conclusion: This paper demonstrates that a trained sparse recommendation MoE can be post-hoc compressed into a smaller standard MoE under an explicit expert budget with minimal or no loss in ranking quality, enabling practical serving speedups.

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) layers expand recommendation capacity through conditional computation, yet a trained checkpoint still stores and routes over its full expert bank. We study a deployment problem: convert that checkpoint to a smaller standard MoE under an explicit expert budget, without adding a compression-specific online module. To address this, we introduce UniMoMo, a post-training compression framework formulated as a constrained graph coarsening problem. Rather than relying on parameter distance, UniMoMo groups experts based on their functional similarity, using an unlabeled calibration set to measure how similarly experts respond to shared recommendation states. To prevent performance degradation, we introduce a layer-adaptive protection mechanism that restricts the merging of high-traffic experts based on their routing exposure. Across Amazon Beauty, KuaiRec, and TenRec with 2, 4, and 6 MoE blocks, the final four-expert checkpoints obtain source-relative five-run mean NDCG@10 ratios of 99.92%--102.30% and measured A100 speedups of 1.28$\times$--1.63$\times$. An aggressive two-expert, top-1 operating point obtains ratios of 98.36%--104.24% and speedups of 1.47$\times$--2.21$\times$. These endpoint results evaluate the complete conversion-and-adaptation workflow and show that a trained recommendation MoE can be exported at multiple serving budgets.

</details>


### [154] [A QUBO-Inspired Computational Framework for Airport Landside Bottleneck Diagnosis and Dynamic Dispatch Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08632)
*Wuming Lei,Xiaobin Li,Mingyan Sun,Jianing Long,Yulin Tong,Yanbin Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种受QUBO启发的计算框架，用于机场场外交通的瓶颈诊断与动态调度，基于五分钟状态模型，在浦东和萧山两机场案例中证明能显著降低乘客排队长度并在扰动下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机场场外在高峰到达时会引起乘客与车辆队列、上车位与道路之间的耦合拥堵，传统调度方法难以同时诊断多源瓶颈并给出实时可执行的调度策略，需新的计算框架提升效率与鲁棒性。

Method: 构建五分钟时间步的多要素状态模型（乘客到达、车辆供给、上车位服务、车辆存储、道路容量），用服务强度、道路需求饱和度、瓶颈频率、队列严重度、影子价杠杆和复合拥堵严重性指数进行瓶颈诊断；比较两种调度算法：有限动作模型预测控制（MPC）与受QUBO启发的模拟退火调度。

Result: 在强峰基线情形下，QUBO启发方法比基线或MPC使最终乘客队列分别在浦东与萧山显著减少；诊断揭示不同主导瓶颈；在需求、供给、服务率、道路容量、模式分担与随机扰动下仍保持队列降低效果。

Conclusion: QUBO启发方法在强峰值场景中能显著减少乘客等待队列（浦东由3445降至2477，萧山由2053降至1482），不同机场的主要瓶颈类型不同（浦东以道路饱和为主，萧山以上车位服务为主），且在所测不确定性水平下保持队列减少效益。
