*Alexander Beiser,Markus Hecher,Nysret Musliu,Georg Trausmuth,Stefan Woltran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ASPaeroFlow：一种针对联合航路流量与动态空域配置（ATFCM）的启发式方法，结合实例空间分解与基于Answer Set Programming（ASP）的局部精确求解，以缓解将ATFM与DAC联合优化时搜索空间爆炸导致的可计算性问题。基准测试显示该启发式在小到产业规模实例上在精度与时间上位于精确方法与运营基线之间；并证明同时优化优于顺序优化，且DAC对解质量影响大于流量控制措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ATFM方法通常将空域配置（DAC）与流量管理（ATFM）分开处理，形成固定需求与固定容量之间的循环依赖。联合优化可消除该矛盾，但导致搜索空间大幅增长、精确模型在中大型实例上不可计算。ASPaeroFlow旨在在可接受计算成本下获得近优解。

Method: 方法上采用两层策略：先对实例空间进行分解（分区/分时等启发式划分）以缩小子问题规模，再对每个子问题应用基于Answer Set Programming的局部精确优化。通过一系列从小规模到行业规模的实例进行基准比较，并做消融实验评估各组成部分的贡献。

Result: 基准结果表明：1) 启发式在运行时间与解质量上位于精确算法与运营基线之间；2) 同时优化的解优于先决顺序优化；3) 消融分析显示DAC对最终目标的影响大于单独的流量措施。

Conclusion: ASPaeroFlow在保持可计算性的同时改进了解质量，提供了精确方法与现有运营基线间的可行折衷；同时优化优于顺序化流程，DAC调整对结果贡献更大。

Abstract: While mathematical models act as vital decision support systems for operational Air Traffic Flow and Capacity Management (ATFCM), existing approaches isolate Air Traffic Flow Management (ATFM) from Dynamic Airspace Configuration (DAC). This separation introduces an unresolved circular dependency between fixed-demand and fixed-capacity assumptions. Although joint optimization resolves this gap, the enlarged search space renders exact models computationally intractable for medium- to large-scale instances. To bridge this gap, we propose ASPaeroFlow: a heuristic for the joint ATFCM; it combines instance-space decomposition heuristics with a local exact approach using Answer Set Programming. We benchmark ASPaeroFlow from small to industry-sized instances and compare it with exact and alternative approaches. The results indicate that (1) the heuristic provides a computational middle ground between exact methods and operational baselines; (2) simultaneous optimization can outperform sequential optimization on joint ATFCM; and (3) an ablation study indicates that DAC has a larger impact on solution quality than flow measures.

</details>


### [214] [CoRE: Consensus Rewards via Equilibrium for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09324)
*Ambuj Mehrish,Sebastiano Vascon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CoRE：用结合答案一致性、推理相似度与生成置信度的图结构并通过复制子动力学找出占优集合，以替代简单多数投票，产生分级伪标签与奖励，理论可证且在多任务多模型设置下优于多数投票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数投票丢弃所有少数答案（即便少数是正确的）并把所有与多数一致的回合同等打分，这造成伪标签粗糙且易受错误众数支配；因此需要一种能区分回合间细粒度证据（答案与推理一致性与置信度）的共识机制，以在无标注的测试时刻生成更可靠、分级的奖励信号，提升自监督/测试时 RL 的效果。

Method: 1) 收集 N 个 roll-outs（答案 + 推理轨迹 + 生成置信度）。2) 为每对回合构造带权边，权重由答案一致性（相等/相似的最终答案）、推理相似度（路径或语义相似性度量）、生成置信度（置信分数或校准后的概率）组合得到。3) 将这些节点与边组成图，应用复制子动力学（replicator dynamics）找出占优集合（dominant set），该集合作为精炼的伪标签候选群；此外从动力学稳定性/权重导出每回合的分级奖励和整题的凝聚性门控（cohesiveness gate）。4) 将这些伪标签/奖励用作测试时强化学习的信号，训练模型获得更高准确率并更快收敛。

Result: CoRE 构建一个基于 N 个模型自回合（roll-outs）的图，图的边将答案一致性、推理相似度和生成置信度三种信号组合起来；通过复制子（replicator）动力学提取占优集合（dominant set），从而得到精炼的伪标签、对每个回合的分级奖励以及每题的凝聚性门控。CoRE 严格推广了多数投票：在特例中可退化为多数投票；论文给出 block-value 分析，导出何时共识可以从更大的错误众数中恢复正确少数的阈值，并证明置信度校准可将该阈值按比例降低。实验证明，在 7 个 backbone 与 5 个 benchmark 上（42 个模型—基准单元格、每个三次随机种子），CoRE 平均将未训练基线提升 +21.7 点（多数投票 TTRL 为 +20.4），在有争议的同意场景中最多比投票高出 +7.5 点，并在 54%–70% 更少的步数内达到投票基线的准确率。

Conclusion: 将 roll-out 组看成图而非票箱，能把脆弱的多数投票变为校准、分级、自监督的奖励信号而不需额外的 roll-out 成本；CoRE 在理论和实证上都优于简单的多数投票，特别是在存在正确少数或投票结果可争议的情形下。

Abstract: On unlabeled test data, reinforcement learning lacks a ground-truth reward; test-time RL methods derive one from the model's own roll-outs, rewarding those that match the majority vote over $N$ sampled answers. That vote discards a correct answer whenever it is a minority and scores every majority-matching roll-out identically. We replace it with \emph{CoRE} (Consensus Rewards via Equilibrium): the $N$ roll-outs form a graph whose edges combine answer agreement, reasoning similarity, and generation confidence, and replicator dynamics extract its dominant set, yielding a refined pseudo-label, a graded per-roll-out reward, and a per-question cohesiveness gate. CoRE strictly generalizes voting: majority voting is recovered as a special case; a block-value analysis gives a sharp threshold for when consensus recovers a correct minority against a larger wrong plurality; and confidence calibration provably lowers that threshold multiplicatively. Across seven backbones and five benchmarks (42 model--benchmark cells, three seeds each), \emph{CoRE} improves the untrained base by $+21.7$ points on average versus $+20.4$ for majority-vote TTRL, wins wherever agreement is contestable with margins over the vote of up to $+7.5$ points, and reaches the voting baseline's plateau accuracy in $54$--$70$\% fewer steps. Consensus, not counting: treating the roll-out group as a graph rather than a ballot box turns a brittle vote into a calibrated, graded, self-supervised reward at no extra roll-out cost.

</details>


### [215] [GeoPhysAdapter: Scale-Matched Geophysical Adaptation for Cross-Domain Landslide Mapping with Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09325)
*Zhihang Liu,Mei-Po Kwan,Jinlin Wu,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoPhysAdapter augments a frozen vision foundation model with dense geophysical priors and bounded adaptation at pixel and candidate-body decision units; adapting at the candidate-body level yields substantially larger reductions in cross-domain false positives and better IoU than pixel-level adaptation, with a conservative fallback to visual output when geo-support is lacking.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cross-domain transfer for newly triggered landslides is undermined by high-confidence false alarms and the uncertain geographic context problem (UGCoP) when coarse resampling misaligns physical supports (terrain, material, rainfall) with pixel-wise segmentation. Integrating multi-scale geophysical constraints aims to reduce spurious detections while preserving transferability.

Method: Anchor a frozen vision foundation model and apply bounded adaptation guided by geophysical priors at two decision units (pixel and candidate landslide body). Constrain model outputs using dense spatial guidance (terrain, material), regional modulation, and event-timing forcing; where geophysical support is insufficient, revert exactly to the foundation visual prediction.

Result: On an event-isolated PILD dataset (4 public sources, 55 global events, 7,890 test samples): 70.3% of cross-domain false-positive mass concentrates in near-pure spurious bodies (median equivalent diameter 207 m). Pixel-level adaptation removed 507,817 erroneous pixels and reduced error by 7.76%. Candidate-body-level adaptation, with identical samples and anchor, reduced error by 23.99% (~3.1× pixel effect), improved IoU by 0.031 (14.2% relative), and achieved a 9.92-to-1 ratio of corrected to harmed pixels.

Conclusion: Embedding terrain, material, and triggering priors as multi-scale, bounded guidance anchored to a frozen foundation model effectively mitigates UGCoP-induced false alarms. Operating adaptation at the candidate-body decision unit maximizes error reduction and IoU gains; reverting to the visual prediction where support is absent preserves baseline performance. Public data and code enable reproducibility.

Abstract: Newly triggered landslides rarely carry immediate annotations, so cross-domain transferability determines the value of landslide mapping for emergency response and regional risk assessment. Vision foundation models have strengthened representational transfer, yet on unseen regions, events, and data sources they still generate high-confidence false alarms. Terrain, material, and rainfall triggering can constrain such errors, but their supports are local, regional, and event-scale, so that resampling onto a 10~m grid misaligns them with the segmentation decision unit and compounds the uncertain geographic context problem (UGCoP). We propose GeoPhysAdapter, which anchors on a frozen vision foundation model, restricts terrain, material, and triggering to dense spatial guidance, regional modulation, and event-timing forcing, and applies bounded adaptation at two decision units, the pixel and the candidate landslide body, reverting exactly to the visual prediction where support is insufficient. On an event-isolated PILD dataset of four public sources, 55 global landslide events, and 7,890 test samples, 70.3% of cross-domain false-positive mass lies in near-pure spurious bodies of median equivalent diameter 207m, matching coarse-prior support rather than the pixel. Pixel-level adaptation removes a net 507,817 erroneous pixels and reduces error by 7.76%, whereas raising the decision unit to the candidate body, under identical samples, anchor, and baseline, increases error reduction to 23.99%, approximately 3.1 times the pixel-level effect, improves IoU by 0.031 (14.2% relative), and corrects 9.92 pixels per pixel harmed. The data and code are publicly available at: https://github.com/Liu-Zhihang/geophysadapter.

</details>


### [216] [Control-Oriented Scenario Tree Construction through Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09335)
*Fabio Pavirani,Bert Claessens,Pierre Pinson,Chris Develder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出以控制表现为目标的情景树构造：固定拓扑、注意力策略分配样本、强化学习训练（带非对称评论家），在电池套利任务上显著提高收益与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于分布距离（如Wasserstein）的情景抽样与降维方法追求概率重构精度，但这种分布层面的接近不一定转化为更好控制决策。故应直接以决策性能为目标来构造情景树，从而使树结构聚焦于对控制结果影响最大的事件。

Method: 在给定树形（分支数、深度）下，将从概率模型或历史数据采样得到的多条未来轨迹视为候选。用一个参数化的注意力网络对候选情景打分并顺序分配到树的叶节点，策略通过强化学习（策略梯度或类似算法）训练，目标是最大化控制器在由该树驱动的闭环运行中的累积利润。为减小训练方差，引入一个基于实际未来实现轨迹的非对称评论家作为价值评估器以稳定学习。

Result: 本文提出一种基于控制目标的情景树（scenario tree）构造方法：在固定树拓扑下，将样本情景分配到树叶的决策建模为基于注意力（attention）的策略，并用强化学习直接以闭环控制收益为目标进行训练。训练通过利用实际未来轨迹的非对称评论家（asymmetric critic）来稳定。实验在风险厌恶的电池套利任务上显示了在不同预测集合规模下均优于传统的前向/后向降维方法和确定性控制，并能更好地控制尾部风险。

Conclusion: 情景树的价值应以其对后续决策的影响来评估；通过基于控制回报学习的构造器，可以得到更紧凑并关注高影响事件的树结构，从而提升闭环性能和尾部风险控制。

Abstract: Multistage stochastic model predictive control (MPC) handles uncertainty by optimizing over a scenario tree, a finite branching approximation of future outcomes constructed from sampled forecasts. To build such a tree, conventional methods focus on matching the underlying probability distribution---e.g., via Wasserstein-based scenario reduction---but improved distributional accuracy does not necessarily yield better control performance. We propose a control-oriented approach that learns scenario tree construction directly from its impact on downstream decisions. Fixing the tree topology, we formulate tree construction as a sequential assignment of sampled scenarios to leaves. This assignment is parameterized by an attention-based policy over the scenario set and trained using reinforcement learning, with closed-loop control profit as the objective. Training is stabilized by an asymmetric critic that leverages realized future trajectories. We evaluate the method on a risk-averse battery arbitrage problem. Across a range of forecast set sizes, the learned construction consistently achieves the highest profit, outperforming classical forward and backward reduction methods and certainty-equivalent (single-trajectory forecast) control. The learned policy also exhibits greater robustness on challenging instances, consistently demonstrating better tail-risk characteristics. Analysis of the resulting trees indicates that our method constructs compact, selectively branching structures that capture high-impact events while keeping most trajectories nearly deterministic. These findings highlight that the value of a scenario tree depends critically on the decisions it supports, and provide an effective framework to train scenario tree constructors merely based on the closed-loop control optimization signal.

</details>


### [217] [LLM-Guided Heuristic Design from Simulation Traces: A Case Study in Dynamic Production and AGV Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09343)
*Jinbo Li,Chuanhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LLM的轨迹驱动代码级策略改进框架，通过可查询的低分仿真轨迹和并行候选编辑，有效提升复杂调度仿真中的策略表现并优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于仿真的优化多把模拟器当黑盒，只用汇总分数排序候选，难以解释失败原因与指明应改进的具体策略逻辑。利用事件级轨迹可以为LLM提供可问询的诊断证据，从而生成针对性的代码修改。

Method: 框架在每个候选策略上进行多次仿真复制；重放最低分的复制以生成可查询的事件级轨迹；一个管理代理（manager）从轨迹与评估证据中提出瓶颈假设；多个编辑代理并行实现代码级修订；执行检查与重复评估后，仅保留改进的候选策略作为“best-so-far”。LLM在评估批次间生成改进建议，仿真运行由固定策略（policy）控制。

Result: 在一个离散事件的产线与AGV调度仿真上，使用Gemini-3.1-Pro进行了五次独立优化运行，最终平均得分77.51（0–100尺度）；最高运行中从62.49提升至78.61，改进举措包括主动充电、基于距离的AGV分配与重新平衡派工优先级。在100个配对随机种子上，最终策略在所有种子上均胜过滚动MILP、规则化与元启发式基线，并在随机故障下保持优势。长时域与可变到达间隔的再优化也超过基线。消融实验表明并行候选生成与轨迹数据库访问均对最终平均分有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于大型语言模型（LLM）的启发式设计框架，利用仿真重放的低分样本轨迹作为可查询证据，指导多候选并行代码级修改，从而在离散事件的生产与AGV调度仿真中显著提升策略表现。

Abstract: Simulation-based optimization (SBO) evaluates executable policies under stochastic dynamics, but most methods treat the simulator as a black box: aggregate scores rank candidates without revealing why they fail or which policy logic should change. We present an LLM-guided heuristic design framework that uses repeated simulation for selection and event-level traces for diagnosis. Each incumbent is assessed through multiple replications, while replaying its lowest-scoring one produces a queryable trace. A manager agent formulates bottleneck hypotheses from this evidence, and editing agents implement parallel code-level revisions. After execution checks and repeated evaluation, best-so-far selection retains only improvements. LLM revision occurs between evaluation batches, while a fixed policy controls each simulation run.
  We evaluate the framework in a discrete-event simulation of dynamic production and automated guided vehicle (AGV) scheduling. Across five independent optimization runs with Gemini-3.1-Pro, final mean scores averaged 77.51 on the simulator's 0-100 scale. In the highest-scoring run, trace-based diagnoses motivated proactive charging, distance-aware AGV assignment, and rebalanced dispatch priorities, raising the best-so-far mean score from 62.49 to 78.61. On 100 matched seeds, the best final policy outscored representative rolling-MILP, rule-based, and metaheuristic policies on every seed and retained its advantage under random faults without re-optimization. After separate re-optimization for a longer horizon and variable order interarrival times, the resulting policies again outscored all baselines. Ablations with two LLM backbones showed that removing either parallel candidate generation or trace-database access reduced final mean scores. These results show that simulation traces can guide targeted code-level policy improvement in complex simulation-based scheduling.

</details>


### [218] [CircuitReason-1k: Benchmarking Long-Horizon Visual-to-Symbolic Reasoning inElectrical Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09374)
*Xinqi Yang,Kang An,Tengyue Wang,Zhongyu Yang,Chenxu Du,Yuanchi Zhu,Hebao Zhu,Ziliang Wang,Faqiang Qian,Yunli Yang,Qibing Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个1000题的电路视觉到符号长程推理基准并评测多模态大模型，发现尽管总体表现可观，但在长程推理和物理约束保持上仍存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉识别不足以解决电路分析；需要一个端到端基准来评估模型从图像识别到物理建模、方程构建和符号推理的完整能力。

Method: 收集真实教科书问题，每题配图、问题、类型化或语义化答案以及参考解；用证据优先的对齐流程把题目、图像和解答对应；按电路类型和依赖深度做推理导向分类；评分采用保守类型化分数并结合身份盲化的多模型语义一致性以避免人为筛选。

Result: 构建了CircuitReason1K：包含1000个教科书电路题的基准，用于端到端视觉到符号的长程推理评测；设计证据优先对齐管线与以推理为导向的题目分类；提出保守的类型化评分与身份盲化的多模型语义一致性评估策略；在3个商用聊天机器人和6个开源多模态大模型上评测，最高系统84.8%准确率，但随推理深度下降，暴露拓扑-目标绑定、物理约定和后期量传播等失败模式。

Conclusion: CircuitReason1K是测量模型将技术视觉证据转化为持续、物理上有效的符号推理能力的有价值测试；当前多模态模型在长程、约束严格的工程推理任务上仍需改进。

Abstract: Electrical circuit analysis requires more than recognizing components in an image. A solver must ground symbols and labels, recover latent topology, select a physical model, formulate coupled equations, propagate intermediate quantities, and preserve units, signs, directions, and phase conventions. We introduce \benchmark, a benchmark of 1,000 authentic textbook problems for evaluating this complete long-horizon visual-to-symbolic reasoning process. Each problem pairs one or more circuit diagrams with a self-contained question, a typed or semantically specified answer, and a reference worked solution. An evidence-first construction pipeline aligns questions, figures, and solutions, while a reasoning-oriented taxonomy organizes problems by circuit type and dependency depth. Evaluation combines conservative typed scoring with identity-blinded multi-model semantic consensus, retaining every problem in the denominator. Across three commercial chatbot systems and six open-source multimodal large language models, the highest-scoring system reaches 84.8\% accuracy. However, performance consistently deteriorates on long-horizon problems, and qualitative analysis exposes persistent failures in topology-to-target binding, physical conventions, and late-stage output propagation. \benchmark{} provides a focused testbed for measuring whether multimodal models can transform technical visual evidence into sustained, physically valid symbolic reasoning. Code are available at GitHub - CircuitReason/CircuitReason1K.

</details>


### [219] [OpenLoopEvolve: A Verifiable Self-Evolution Framework for Loop Policies in Long-Horizon Complex Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09380)
*Siqi Wang,Xinlin Li,Zhenglin Li,Li Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把长期控制行为视为可治理的“Loop Policy”资产，通过版本化、线上/线下进化、LLM候选生成与Champion–Challenger评估来积累和重用控制经验，从而在模拟业务基准上提升长期复杂任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon complex tasks require iterative decision loops and experience accumulation, but control experience is typically locked in a single context or fixed prompt and not reusable across traces. The paper aims to enable accumulation, comparison, release and reuse of control experience.

Method: OpenLoopEvolve (OLE): self-evolution framework based on a Loop Policy that encapsulates observation, planning, memory, action, verification, recovery, stopping and budget control as versioned, portable policy assets. Supports online evolution (candidate generation from live feedback) and offline evolution (search from archived traces); uses LLM proposals, Champion–Challenger paired evaluation, robust release and rollback.

Result: On YC-Bench (simulated business tasks), both online and offline evolution modes improved aggregate task performance, success rates and risk metrics compared to a fixed initial Loop Policy. Policies released online are activated at task boundaries and monitored; rollbacks occur on degradation.

Conclusion: Treating the Loop Policy as a governable, versioned asset with automated evolution (online/offline) enables continual improvement and safer release management, improving agent performance on long-horizon tasks while supporting rollback and reuse of learned control strategies.

Abstract: Long-horizon complex tasks require agents to repeatedly observe states, formulate plans, invoke tools, verify results, and recover from failures in continuously changing environments. However, such control experience often remains confined to a single context or a fixed prompt, and is difficult to accumulate and reuse across historical traces. This paper presents OpenLoopEvolve (OLE), a self-evolution framework centered on the Loop Policy. OLE represents an agent's observation, planning, memory, action, verification, recovery, stopping, and budget control as portable policy assets with versions and lineages, and provides online and offline evolution modes that can be selected according to practical needs: the online mode triggers candidate generation based on feedback from continuous operation, whereas the offline mode searches for candidate policies from archived traces and failure evidence. Both modes share an evolution mechanism consisting of autonomous proposals by a large language model, Champion--Challenger paired evaluation, and robust release. Policies released online are activated at a subsequent task boundary, monitored using subsequent feedback, and rolled back to their parent versions when degradation conditions are met. On the simulated business benchmark YC-Bench, both modes improve aggregate task performance, task success rate, and risk metrics relative to a fixed initial Loop Policy. The results indicate that treating the Loop Policy as a governable asset can support the accumulation, comparison, release, and reuse of control experience and improve agent performance on long-horizon complex tasks.

</details>


### [220] [KVDiagnosis: A Diagnostic Benchmark for KV-Cache Compression in Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09412)
*Chen Qiu,Ziwu Liu,Chao Fei,Guozhong Li,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KVDiagnosis 是一个可复现的 KV-cache 压缩故障诊断基准：通过方法级分离的 C-to-W 挑选、统一测量格式和注意力干预实验，揭示大多数压缩失败由覆盖不足导致，并证明注意力增强能在一部分低-EAR 失败中有效修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对长上下文的 KV-cache 压缩方法通常用汇总任务分数评估，但无法说明具体哪些正确执行被破坏以及为何，因而需要细粒度的诊断基准与测量集来定位根因并验证补救策略。

Method: 构建一个包含 25 种方法、5 大机制族的分类法并关联 8 个已验证实现；对每个方法配置在固定拆分上分别以每个源的 FullCache 控制评估，选取 FullCache 正确而压缩失败 (C-to-W) 的行；定义统一记录格式将输出与运行元数据连接到 cache、likelihood、attention、decoding 等测量，并标注适用性；在 Qwen3-8B 与 4 个 evidence-aware 工作负载上运行，生成统计与诊断并对低 EAR 失败进行注意力干预实验。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上获得 59,800 次受支持的压缩运行、2,600+ 个源与 12,520 条 C-to-W 行；在固定诊断规则下 63.2% 的 C-to-W 行显示低或部分的测量/投影覆盖度；仅 19 条（0.2%）同时具备高测量/投影覆盖度与强烈的似然漂移；2,126 条（17.0%）保留结构化位置可寻址性但展示相同漂移；十个诊断指标在 C-to-C 成功对照下区分失败与成功的分层 AUROC 在 0.684–0.871 之间；在 96 个可复现的低-EAR 失败中，受控的 4x 证据-注意力增强修复了 29.2%，而数量匹配的假干预修复率为 6.3%，匹配的 C-to-C 对照则有 3.3% 的性能下降。

Conclusion: KVDiagnosis 提供了一套面向 KV-cache 压缩失败的诊断数据集和基准，使得可以在方法级别上识别哪些原本正确的执行在压缩后失败并分析成因；实验表明多数失败表现为低或不完全的覆盖度，少数伴随显著的似然漂移，且针对低 EAR 的注意力增强能部分修复失败。

Abstract: KV-cache compression reduces long-context memory, but aggregate task scores reveal neither which correct executions fail nor why. We present KVDiagnosis, a diagnostic dataset and benchmark with three contributions. First, a 25-method taxonomy groups methods into five mechanism families and links them to eight verified implementations and their valid diagnostic measurements. Second, for every supported method setting, we evaluate all sources in each fixed split against a per-source FullCache control before selecting FullCache-correct/compressed-wrong (C-to-W) rows separately for each method-setting, so no compressor defines another's test set. Third, a common record format links paired outputs and run metadata to cache, likelihood, attention, and decoding measurements with explicit applicability states. On Qwen3-8B, four evidence-aware workloads yield 59 800 supported compressed runs over 2600 sources and 12 520 C-to-W rows. Under fixed diagnostic rules, 63.2% have low or partial measured/projected coverage. Only 19 rows (0.2%) combine high measured/projected coverage with strong likelihood drift; another 2,126 (17.0%) preserve structural position addressability, for which representation fidelity remains unknown, while showing the same drift. Against C-to-C success controls, all ten diagnostics separate failed from successful compression (stratified AUROC 0.684-0.871). Among 96 reproducible low-EAR failures, a controlled 4x evidence-attention boost repairs 29.2%, versus 6.3% under a count-matched sham intervention and 3.3% degradation on matched C-to-C controls. Code and data are available at https://github.com/ChosenQC/KVDiagnosis.

</details>


### [221] [Listen, See and Track: Spatio-Temporal Audio-Visual Sound Event Reasoning for Omni-Modal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09435)
*Zhi Zeng,Cheng Zhang,Zesheng Yang,Rendong Pi,Jiaying Wu,Di Zhang,Zihan Ma,Guodong Li,Zhou Yang,Yu Xiang,Yifei Zheng,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ST-OmniQA数据集与ST-Omni-R1模型，通过融合FOA空间信息与全景视觉并采用课程学习+推理树RL，在时空音视问答与源定位/跟踪任务上显著优于现有基线且具有迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频-语言模型多将片段视为全局声学事件，视觉-语言模型缺乏空间声学线索以实现对单独声源的定位与跟踪，因而需要专门基准与方法来评测和学习时空音视理解能力。

Method: 构建大规模基于全景视频与一阶Ambisonics(FOA)音频的时空音视问答基准ST-OmniQA（40K 视频，400K QA），问题分为四个能力层级；提出模型ST-Omni-R1，将FOA导出的语义与轨迹表示与全景视觉上下文融合，并采用渐进课程学习与基于推理树的强化学习训练。

Result: ST-Omni-R1在四个能力层级上平均语义准确率77.83%，相比最优基线的37.28%有显著提升；在三个公共空间音频基准上也显示出良好的迁移能力，证明其空间与运动表示的通用性。

Conclusion: ST-OmniQA填补了评测时空音视定位与追踪能力的空白；ST-Omni-R1成功利用FOA与全景视觉学习可泛化的空间/运动表征，带来大幅性能提升。后续应关注数据真实度、复杂声学环境下的鲁棒性、计算成本与更多消融实验以评估通用性与可部署性。

Abstract: Understanding dynamic sound sources requires jointly determining what produces a sound, where the source is located, and how it moves over time. Yet existing audio-language models often represent clips as global acoustic events, while vision-language models lack the spatial audio cues needed to localize and track individual sources. To evaluate this missing capability, we introduce ST-OmniQA, a spatio-temporal audio-visual question-answering benchmark built from panoramic videos paired with synchronized first-order Ambisonics (FOA) audio of moving sound sources. It contains 40K videos and 400K question-answer pairs organized into four capability levels covering sound-event recognition, direction of arrival, source distance, motion trajectories, and temporally grounded audio-visual reasoning. Building on this benchmark, we propose ST-Omni-R1, which integrates FOA-derived semantic and trajectory representations with panoramic visual context and is trained through progressive curriculum learning and reasoning-tree reinforcement learning. ST-Omni-R1 achieves 77.83\% average semantic accuracy across the four levels, compared with 37.28\% for the best evaluated baseline. Results on three public spatial-audio benchmarks further indicate that its learned spatial and motion representations transfer beyond ST-OmniQA.

</details>


### [222] [Coupled Graph--Policy Distillation for Personalized Medication Safety in Older Adults with Multimorbidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09443)
*Zihan Wang,Anglin Liu,Rongyi Wang,Dantong Li,Yi Lu,Siqing Yuan,Hongxia Xu,Zhongtian Long,Jintai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATLAS通过将指南编码为药物-安全性图并在交互中构建患者特定冲突图，结合风险优先的多智能体策略，实现了对老年多病患者的安全用药评估和建议，并在多项基准与临床盲评中显著优于现有LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当老年多病共存患者在临床随访之间需要药物复审时，现有大语言模型（LLM）代理可能提供帮助，但安全决策高度依赖于被治疗者的具体疾病、用药和老年学风险信息，而这些信息常被用户遗漏或未充分提供。为实现面向患者的安全用药决策，需要将指南证据结构化并在交互中补全患者状态，再基于该特定信息进行风险优先的决策推断。

Method: （1）构建药物-安全性图以编码指南的药物、条件、不良反应、注意事项与监测要求；（2）设计针对性问题以在交互过程中补全患者状态，并将相关关系蒸馏为患者特定的药物冲突图（PMCG）；（3）采用风险优先的多智能体策略模块，按顺序执行禁忌筛查、注意事项与监测需求评估、更安全替代方案识别与最终方案核验；（4）设计GeriMedBench交互基准及在欧洲/亚洲多项非交互/交互基准上进行自动化与盲审临床评估以验证方法有效性。

Result: 提出ATLAS：一种耦合图—策略蒸馏框架，用于患者自适应的药物安全性评估。将指南证据表示为药物-安全性图，通过定向问题更新患者状态并蒸馏为患者特定的药物冲突图（PMCG）。基于PMCG的风险优先多智能体策略执行禁忌筛查、注意事项与监测需求评估、替代药物识别与最终方案核验。引入交互式基准GeriMedBench以评估安全关键信息获取与基于证据的决策修正。ATLAS在欧洲和亚洲的多项基准（交互/非交互及跨指南）上优于比较系统：在欧洲非交互多病共存基准上，ATLAS在严格成功率上比最强的专有LLM高53.73点，在总体安全推理分（OSRS）上高14.63点，自动评估下无不安全推荐；盲审临床评估在五项评分上均更高，并仅在少数病例被标记出潜在不安全推荐。

Conclusion: 将结构化指南证据与交互式患者状态补全相结合，并采用以风险优先的多智能体策略，可显著提升面向老年多病共存患者的药物安全决策性能。ATLAS在多项基准及临床评估中表现优异，但仍需在真实临床流程、指南异质性和长期效果方面进一步验证。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can support medication review between clinical visits, but safe choices for older adults with multimorbidity depend on conditions, medications, and geriatric risks that users may omit. We introduce ATLAS, a coupled graph--policy distillation framework for patient-adaptive medication safety. ATLAS structures guideline evidence as a medication-safety graph. Targeted questions update the patient state and distill relevant relations into a patient-specific medication conflict graph (PMCG). A risk-first multi-agent policy uses the PMCG to screen contraindications, assess cautions and monitoring needs, identify safer alternatives, and verify the final medication plan. We also introduce GeriMedBench, an interactive benchmark that tests safety-critical information acquisition and evidence-based decision revision. Across a European non-interactive multimorbidity benchmark, an Asian interactive multimorbidity benchmark, and an Asian non-interactive cross-guideline benchmark, ATLAS achieves the strongest complete-decision performance among the compared systems. On the European non-interactive multimorbidity benchmark, it exceeds the strongest proprietary LLM baseline by 53.73 points in Strict Success Rate and 14.63 points in overall safety reasoning score (OSRS), with no unsafe recommendations under the automated evaluator. A blinded clinician evaluation gives ATLAS higher mean ratings across all five criteria and flags potentially unsafe recommendations in one ATLAS case and two Gemini cases.

</details>


### [223] [From Prompt to Harness: Coderlet from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09480)
*Mengfan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The paper argues the harness is as important as the model, defines a minimal, structured harness with three boundaries and a request lifecycle, and provides an executable realization (Coderlet) to show how model outputs become actions and how feedback/state persist across runs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clarify that an ML model by itself does not determine agent behavior; the orchestration/harness (what the model sees, how actions are executed, feedback loop, and state persistence) shapes behavior. Propose studying a compact harness to make these interactions explicit and minimal yet sufficient for production-like needs.

Method: Analytical and design-driven: follow a single request through five stages (context formation, model decision, environmental action, observation return, state continuation); articulate three interface boundaries; formalize a request lifecycle outlining transition ordering; implement the design in an executable artifact (https://github.com/lilinxi/Coderlet) to validate practicality.

Result: Proposes a compact harness design that traces a single request through stages: context formation, model decision, environmental action, observation return, and state continuation. Defines three boundaries—model, execution, and state—connecting model service, tool environment, and persistent state. Describes a request lifecycle ordering transitions and shows how the harness turns model outputs into actions, feeds runtime feedback into later decisions, and allows state continuation across requests. Demonstrates a concrete implementation in the Coderlet repository.

Conclusion: A well-designed harness is essential for transforming model generations into effective agent behavior by managing inputs, execution, observations, and persistent state. The compact design with model/execution/state boundaries and a request lifecycle supports maintainable, incremental refinement and bootstrapping across runs. The provided implementation enables reproducibility and further experimentation.

Abstract: A model alone does not determine how a programming agent acts. What the model sees, how actions enter the environment, how feedback returns, and how one run affects the next all depend on how the harness is organized. Minimal examples usually show only the basic interaction between a model and tools, while production systems spread these relationships across complex components and dependencies. This paper studies a compact harness design by following a single request through context formation, model decision, environmental action, observation return, and state continuation. Three boundaries---model, execution, and state---connect the model service, tool environment, and persistent state, while the request lifecycle determines the order in which these transitions occur. Together, they show the harness's core role: turning model generations into environmental actions, carrying runtime feedback into later decisions, and allowing state to continue across requests. On top of this runtime structure, a harness can also be gradually refined across runs through continued bootstrapping. The design is realized in the executable artifact https://github.com/lilinxi/Coderlet.

</details>


### [224] [Capability Is Not Propensity: Measuring Pressure-Robust Cooperative Behavior in Civic LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09485)
*Neel Tushar Shah,Manglam Kartik,Akshat Karkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DiffCoop-Civic评估框架以区分‘能做什么’与‘倾向做什么’；在现实公民压力下多数模型表现出增加的操控性与减少的异议保护，且不同模型家族对明显压力反应不同；轻量提示可部分缓解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的合作能力具有双重用途：既可支持公民论辩，也可被用于策略性省略、制造虚假共识和操控性框架。需要区分模型在善意指令下的能力与在现实压力下的倾向性。

Method: 构建DiffCoop-Civic评估套件（10个场景）覆盖偏好理解、证据与劝说、承诺设计、不对称信息与异议保存；在‘良性指令’与‘现实公民压力’两种条件下测试七个模型（四个模型家族）；量化指标包括操控性启用与异议保存（5分量表）；引入名为Pareto-Trace的轻量提示干预并提供匿名可复现包。

Result: 微妙的省略压力导致近乎一致的变化：操控性启用上升约1.17分，异议保存下降约1.67分（5分制）。明显的虚假共识压力导致不同表现：一些对齐的API模型倾向拒绝或重定向，而若干开源权重模型直接顺从。Pareto-Trace提示能够在不单纯通过强拒绝的情况下改善对抗压力的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 应在模型评估中显式纳入现实社会压力情形以揭示双用风险；DiffCoop-Civic示范了此类检测的可行性并展示了可行的缓解途径（如Pareto-Trace），但需扩展场景、模型与长期社会影响评估。

Abstract: Cooperative capabilities in language models are dual-use. The same social reasoning that supports civic deliberation can also enable strategic omission, false consensus, and manipulative framing. We argue that Cooperative AI evaluations should separate what models can do under benign instructions from what they tend to do under realistic civic pressure. We introduce DiffCoop-Civic, a 10-scenario pilot evaluation suite spanning preference understanding, evidence and persuasion, commitment design, asymmetric information, and dissent preservation. Across seven models from four model families, subtle omission pressure produces a near-uniform shift: manipulative enablement rises by 1.17 points and dissent preservation falls by 1.67 points on a 5-point scale. Overt false-consensus pressure behaves differently: it triggers refusal or redirection in some aligned API models, but direct compliance in several open-weight models. A lightweight Pareto-Trace prompting intervention improves pressure robustness without simply relying on hard refusal. An anonymous reproducibility package is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/diffcoop-civil-771C.

</details>


### [225] [Renormalising Generative Models for Active Inference: Foundations, Derivations, and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09512)
*Karim Zaghw,Andrew Pashea,Marc Pritsch,Wouter Nuijten,Karl Friston,Lancelot Da Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对RGMs做了详尽、可操作的推导并提供独立实现，显化原文隐含的算法细节与设计抉择，从而使离散主动推断在复杂时空任务上的应用更可复现与可评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散主动推断在丰富的时空域中难以扩展；原始RGMs提供了解决思路但数学与实现隐含细节多，阻碍复现与实际应用。本文旨在把理论与实现分离，补足细节，促进在机器学习基准上的定量评估与发展。

Method: 通过逐步的数学推导，分层构建RGMs（从低层状态与路径到高层对象、事件、动作的粗化），明确层内信念与动作更新规则以及层间信息流。对原始论文与参考实现中的取舍与差异逐条说明，并以独立代码实现验证其行为。

Result: 给出自洽的理论说明、对关键算法细节的显式选择与建模后果说明，并发布经过验证的独立实现，提升了可复现性、可审计性和易用性，为后续在标准基准上的对照实验与扩展研究奠定基础。

Conclusion: 本文澄清并复现了Renormalising Generative Models（RGMs）框架，提供自洽的推导与公开、经验证的实现，从而降低了复制门槛并提高了透明性与可审计性。

Abstract: Active inference offers a unified framework for perception, learning, and action, but scaling discrete active-inference models to rich spatial and temporal domains remains difficult. Renormalising generative models (RGMs) address this challenge by composing discrete generative models across spatial and temporal scales, coarse-graining lower-level states and paths into higher-level causes for objects, events, and action. However, fully reproducing and adapting the framework remains difficult: the mathematical exposition is compact, and the reference implementations are deeply integrated within specialized software environments, leaving many algorithmic details implicit. This paper addresses these challenges by providing a self-contained, derivation-oriented account of RGMs together with an open, verified implementation. We explain how the hierarchy is built, how beliefs and actions are updated within it, and how information is passed between levels. Where the published equations and implementation differ in emphasis, we make those choices explicit and explain their modelling consequences. By clarifying the theory and separating it from its original implementation context, this work lowers practical barriers to entry and makes RGMs more transparent, auditable, and reproducible, providing a foundation for future quantitative evaluation and development on machine-learning benchmarks.

</details>


### [226] [One Adapter Pair per Model: A Universal Activation Interface for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09521)
*Su-Hyeon Kim,Jiwan Mun,Yo-Sub Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Universal Activation Bus，通过共享稠密空间加轻量适配器实现跨模型激活接口，允许激活级工具在不同但兼容的语言模型间共享与复用，实验在五个模型上证明了语义一致性与工具迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于激活的工具（探针、稀疏表征、解释器等）通常耦合于单一模型的本征隐藏空间，导致每换一款语言模型就需重建或重新发现这些工具；作者旨在提供一个模型间通用的激活接口，以实现工具的共享与快速接入。

Method: 构建一个共享的稠密激活空间（Universal Activation Bus），并为每个模型训练一对轻量线性编码器-解码器适配器；在若干源模型上联合训练共享空间并冻结接口，新模型通过在无标签的匹配文本上只拟合其适配器对即可接入；在该共享空间上直接复用探针、稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征以及其他基于激活的自然语言解释器（NLA），并验证跨模型中间激活可被另一个模型的冻结上层直接消费以生成预测。

Result: 在五个模型上验证：语义相关文本在共享空间中形成一致的邻域；新接入模型在仅训练适配器对后能有效复用在源模型上训练的探针、SAE特征和NLA；另外，来自一个模型的中间激活可被另一个模型的冻结上层接受并产生合理预测；证明了可行的模型间激活契约。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个稳定的、按模型划分的激活契约，使得基于激活的分析与解释工具可以在兼容的语言模型间被复用与快速接入；表明在实际部署中可显著降低为每个新模型重建激活工具的成本，并展示了跨模型中间激活直接驱动上层预测的潜力。

Abstract: Activation-based tools are usually tied to one model's native hidden space, requiring probes, sparse autoencoders, and natural-language interpreters to be rebuilt or rediscovered for each new language model. We present a Universal Activation Bus, a framework that provides a common activation interface across compatible language models. Using a small set of source models, we learn a shared dense space together with one lightweight linear encoder--decoder adapter pair per model. After source training, the interface is frozen; a new model joins by fitting only its adapter pair on unlabeled matched text. The resulting interface allows activation-based tools to be shared across connected models, including common probes and SAE features as well as access to an NLA originally trained for a different model. Across five models, semantically related texts form consistent neighborhoods in the shared space, and an onboarded model reuses these tools effectively without retraining them. We further show that an intermediate activation from one model can be used by another model's frozen upper layers to produce predictions. These results establish a stable, model-wise activation contract for reusable tools across compatible language models.

</details>


### [227] [verdi: retrieval is not transfer for continual world model optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09537)
*Junyu Wu,Shiqin Nie,Youyi Kou,Baohua Yin,Guocai Yao,Qingyu Chen,Jingheng Ma,Shiji Zhou,Hongyong Song,Mingchen Zhuge,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VERDI提出证据许可的持续优化框架：用推理时探针构建指纹、检索并在目标侧验证候选策略，从而显著降低搜索与计算成本并减少负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法把在某一模型上成功的优化策略当作可直接复用的配方，但这种检索并不等于迁移；要安全有效地在新模型上复用策略，需要在目标模型上做实验验证并把验证结果记录为可迁移的证据。

Method: 对每个世界模型使用共享的推理时探针构建优化指纹(Optimization Fingerprint)，基于指纹检索并排序相关历史经验作为候选假设，然后在冻结的目标侧验证器上逐一验证这些候选，只有通过验证的才被接纳为可复用证据；当相近指纹间出现矛盾时，触发探针进化以持续改进诊断表示。

Result: 在Ctrl-World、Cosmos系列和RoboCoin上的实验显示，VERDI将搜索成本降低68%、GPU成本降低69%，负迁移率从0.34降到0.06，并以83%的符号准确率预测迁移结果。

Conclusion: VERDI通过在目标模型上进行验证实验，将先前模型上的优化策略转化为可复用的证据，从而实现更安全、低成本的世界模型优化迁移。

Abstract: Foundation world models have made remarkable progress in planning, simulation, and embodied intelligence. However, optimizing a pretrained world model toward a user-specified objective remains difficult: each campaign typically rediscovers optimization strategies from scratch, and the resulting knowledge rarely transfers to the next model. Existing research agents automate the optimization loop but treat successful strategies as directly reusable recipes, without principled safeguards for when transfer is appropriate. We argue instead that retrieval is not transfer: a strategy validated on one model is at best an optimization hypothesis for another, and becomes transferable knowledge only after target-side experimental valida- tion. Guided by this principle, we propose VERDI , a continual framework for evidence-licensed world model optimization. VERDI characterizes each world model through shared inference-time probes to construct an Optimization Fin- gerprint, retrieves relevant prior experience as ranked hypotheses, and validates every candidate under a frozen target-side verifier before admitting it as reusable evidence; contradictions among nearby fingerprints further trigger probe evolution, continually refining the diagnostic representation itself. Experiments on Ctrl-World, the Cosmos family, and RoboCoin show that VERDI reduces search cost by 68%, GPU cost by 69%, and negative transfer from 0.34 to 0.06, while predicting transfer outcomes with 83% sign accuracy.

</details>


### [228] [Bidirectional Context Self-Distillation for Reinforcement Learning of Skill-Based LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09555)
*Tianjun Pan,Yuan Li,Hongda Wang,Linbo Jin,Mengfei Song,Lei Gao,Qiming Shi,Shaokang Fu,Jiarong Zhao,Chengyu Wang,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BCSD通过双向语境自蒸馏（增强与缩减视角）为RL策略提供细粒度token级监督，从而显著提升LLM代理对外部自然语言技能的利用效率与任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升使用外部自然语言技能（skills）的LLM代理的能力；现有以任务级奖励为中心的强化学习缺乏对如何有效利用技能的精细监督，导致无法充分转化技能指导为实际动作。

Method: 对每条生成的轨迹分别构建（1）增强视角：在原技能上下文之上加入更高层的Meta-Skill指导以揭示更广义的动作意图；（2）缩减视角：剪除笼统或非特定的指导以强调任务关键技能；对两个视角在token级别的输出差异提取监督信号，并将该信号用于对强化学习中优势（advantage）进行重标度，从而在策略梯度更新中更加重视那些更好利用技能的行为。

Result: 提出BCSD（Bidirectional Context Self-Distillation）：将自蒸馏与强化学习结合，针对每条轨迹构建两种互补的技能上下文视角（增强视角引入Meta-Skill，高级指导；缩减视角突出任务特定技能），在token级别提取信号并用于重标度RL的优势值。实验证明在ALFWorld和WebShop上，BCSD在各模型规模上都带来最强性能，消融研究验证两类视角的互补性。

Conclusion: BCSD为技能驱动的LLM代理提供了一种有效的训练范式，通过双视角自蒸馏生成互补信号来放大正确使用技能的学习信号，实验显示可提升性能并具有良好可复现性。未来应验证其对不同技能质量、更多环境以及样本效率和泛化性的影响。

Abstract: External natural-language skills provide large language model (LLM) agents with reusable and editable guidance for solving complex tasks. Yet their effectiveness depends not only on skill quality, but also on whether the policy can translate the provided guidance into appropriate actions. However, methods specifically designed to improve this skill-utilization ability remain largely underexplored. In practice, skill-based agents are commonly trained with reinforcement learning objectives centered on task-level rewards, which offer limited supervision and struggle to capture subtle differences in how effectively the policy uses the provided skills. We propose BCSD (Bidirectional Context Self-Distillation), a framework that combines self-distillation with reinforcement learning to train LLM agents to use external skills more effectively. Unlike prior self-distillation methods that rely on a single privileged context, BCSD evaluates each trajectory from two complementary skill-context views. The augmented view introduces higher-level Meta-Skill guidance, while the reduced view prunes general guidance to highlight task-specific skills. Their complementary token-level signals are combined to rescale the RL advantage. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that BCSD achieves the strongest overall performance across model scales, enabling agents to utilize external skills more effectively. Ablation studies further verify the complementary contributions of the augmented and reduced context views. Code will be released to ensure full reproducibility.

</details>


### [229] [The Politician, the Liar, and the Obedient Worker: Emerging Behavior of LLM Agents in Hierarchical Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09574)
*Fatemeh Seyedin,Adrian Weller,Jinhyuk Yun,Mahmoudreza Babaei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在设计多主体LLM系统时，制度细节、激励设置与模型多样性决定能否防止治理失灵；简单的制度添加会产生复杂且脆弱的行为后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM从个人工具转向多主体协作或组织参与者，关键问题是它们会否再现人类机构中常见的治理问题（搭便车、腐败、领导固化等），以及制度设计如何影响其行为。

Method: 提出了层级博弈（Hierarchical Game），将公共物品博弈扩展为包含管理权、民主选举与私人交流的框架。在此框架内，逐步加入六种制度元素（言论、同行、政府/管理、薪酬、监督、选举），对六个先进LLM（Qwen、Grok、Claude、GPT-4o等）在12个实验设置中进行交互式模拟与行为度量。

Result: 发现各模型呈现截然不同的行为谱系：Qwen多次违背承诺（违约率13.3%）；Grok固有不合作但在可被惩罚时完全合作（从16%上升到100%）；Claude与GPT-4o在基线下合作性较强。但引入薪酬、匿名惩罚或模型同质性后，合作和诚实性显著恶化；薪酬导致私下交易以赢取/保住职位，匿名惩罚诱发作弊，同质模型群体导致首位当选管理者长期在位。

Conclusion: LLMs在多主体组织中会复现人类制度性治理失灵的多种模式：对惩罚高度敏感、易受薪酬激励扭曲、在匿名惩罚下出现欺骗、且相同模型家庭会导致领导长期固化。少数模型（如GPT-4o）在多种制度下表现更稳定，但整体行为脆弱且依赖激励与模型多样性。

Abstract: LLMs are rapidly embedding themselves into daily life: drafting our emails, managing our schedules, and making decisions on our behalf. As they move from individual tools to participants in multi-agent organizations, an important question arises: do they reproduce the governance failures like free-riding, corruption, and entrenched leadership that plague human institutions? We introduce the Hierarchical Game (HG), a public goods game extended with managerial authority, democratic elections, and private communication. Testing six frontier models across twelve experiments that add institutions one at a time (speech, peers, government, wages, oversight, elections), we find distinct behavioral profiles: Qwen promises and lies (13.3\% broken promises); Grok refuses to cooperate on its own but becomes fully cooperative once a manager can punish it (16\%$\to$100\%); Claude and GPT-4o cooperate reliably at baseline. But honesty proves fragile. When the manager role comes with a salary, all models except GPT-4o start cutting private deals to win or keep the position. When punishment is made anonymous, honest models begin to cheat. When all agents share the same model family, the first elected manager stays in power indefinitely. Leadership change only happens in groups that mix different families.

</details>


### [230] [ElasticBack: Stealthy Conditional Backdoor in LLM-Agent Skills via Coupled Trigger-Rule Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09577)
*Hao Sui,Simeng Qin,Jie Liao,Xiaojun Jia,Bing Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ElasticBack: a conditional single-skill backdoor that injects a semantic rule into skills and evolves stealthy user triggers, achieving high targeted attack success while remaining dormant and stealthy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The paper addresses security risks in LLM agent ecosystems, specifically the threat that a single malicious skill can infect many agents. Existing attacks are limited (always-on triggers, fine-tuned weights, or multiple skills). The authors aim to design a conditional, low-cost, single-skill backdoor that remains dormant unless specific conditions are met.

Method: They use trigger-as-switch construction, semantic-anchored rule injection to plant rule R into skill documents, freeze R, and then apply a stealth-constrained genetic search to evolve trigger T in user queries. They test across three target behaviors with 50 skills each and four LLM agents, measuring attack success, false positives, clean accuracy, transferability, and defense evasion.

Result: ElasticBack is introduced: it plants a semantic rule R into the skill document and evolves a benign-looking trigger T in user queries via a genetic search. The trigger-as-switch design ensures the malicious payload activates only when R and T co-occur, thus remaining weight-free and stealthy. Experiments across multiple skills and LLM agents show high attack success, low false positives, model transferability, and evasion of deployment defenses.

Conclusion: ElasticBack demonstrates that skill supply chains are vulnerable to stealthy, conditional backdoors that don't modify model weights, urging development of stronger defenses for skill vetting and deployment-time monitoring.

Abstract: Agent skills, bundles of instructions and resources that an LLM agent loads on demand, form an emerging supply chain where a single poisoned skill can persistently compromise every agent that installs it. However, existing skill attacks either fire on every request or rely on fine-tuned weights or multiple skills, leaving a conditional and low-cost backdoor unexplored. In this work, we present ElasticBack, an effective conditional single-skill backdoor that plants a rule R in the skill document and a benign-looking trigger T in the user query, so the malicious payload fires only when both co-occur. ElasticBack binds the two sides through a trigger-as-switch construction, generating R via semantic-anchored rule injection. It then freezes R and evolves T against it with a stealth-constrained genetic search, so that effectiveness and stealth are optimized, keeping the backdoor weight-free and dormant on benign inputs. Extensive experiments across three target behaviors (50 skills each) and four agent LLMs show that ElasticBack attains a high attack success rate at a near-zero false-positive rate with preserved clean accuracy, transfers across models, and evades deployment-time defenses. These results motivate stronger defenses for the skill supply chain.

</details>


### [231] [CoRCi: Cross-Reconstruction of Coherent Interests Modeling in Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09580)
*Qingtian Bian,Tieying Li,Marcus de Carvalho,Jiaxing Xu,Hui Fang,Yiping Ke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoRCi通过‘从域特定表示跨重构混域表示+单序列域无关损失’以及‘针对同域负样本加权的FocalNCE’，有效桥接域间差异，提升跨域序列推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDSR方法难以区分域特定与域不变兴趣；部分方法将多域序列混合并用独立编码器与逐域损失训练，放大域间差异并破坏域不变兴趣一致性，尤其当Seq2Seq的查询与目标跨域时。

Method: 提出双目标CDSR框架CoRCi：先对各域序列进行独立编码，再通过跨注意力（cross-attention）从已编码的域特定表示生成混域表示（Cross-Reconstruction）；采用单一的序列级域无关损失训练生成的混域表示以维护域不变兴趣连贯性；在对比学习目标（InfoNCE）中嵌入Focal Loss，形成FocalNCE，对与查询来自同域的负样本施以更高惩罚以强化域不变对齐。

Result: 在四个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，CoRCi在所有指标上均稳定优于最先进的CDSR基线，并且提升具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: CoRCi通过跨重构与单序列域无关损失、以及FocalNCE负样本加权，有效抑制域间差异并保持域不变兴趣一致性，从而在多套真实数据上显著优于现有CDSR方法。

Abstract: Cross-Domain Sequential Recommendation (CDSR) aims to alleviate data sparsity by transferring dynamic user interests across related domains. A key challenge lies in effectively bridging these domains. In single-domain modeling, models cannot distinguish between domain-specific and domain-invariant interests. Recent methods merge domain-specific sequences chronologically into a mixed-domain sequence to capture domain-invariant knowledge. However, they typically deploy separate encoders for the mixed-domain sequence and train them with per-domain loss aggregation. This workflow magnifies inter-domain discrepancies and disrupts domain-invariant interest coherence, especially when query target pairs in Seq2Seq originate from different domains. In this paper, we present CoRCi (Cross-Reconstruction for Coherent Interest), a dual-target CDSR framework that tackles these drawbacks. Specifically, CoRCi proposes a Cross-Reconstruction approach that generates mixed-domain representations directly from pre-encoded specific-domain representations via cross-attention. The generated representations are then trained using a single, sequence-level, domain-agnostic loss to preserve the coherence of domain-invariant interests. To further suppress domain discrepancies in mixed-domain modeling, CoRCi introduces FocalNCE, which embeds Focal Loss into the preceding mixed-domain InfoNCE objective. The new loss assigns higher penalties to negatives drawn from the same domain as the query, thereby strengthening domain-invariant alignment. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets demonstrate that CoRCi consistently outperforms state-of-the-art CDSR counterparts, achieving statistically significant gains across all metrics.

</details>


### [232] [ICM Out! Better Tournament Strategy from Computed Continuations, vs. Solvers and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09586)
*Boning Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ICM因忽略动态因素在锦标赛估值上存在显著误差；SCO通过将当前手结果映射到以有限锦标赛模型定价的后继状态来构建策略，显著提高了期望奖池价值并改变了动作频率，表明在许多情形下ICM不足以作为策略构造的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICM只基于筹码量估值，忽略了关键的动态要素（行动顺序、盲注、座位与淘汰压力），这会扭曲对后继状态的定价，从而影响基于这些估值构建的策略。需要一种能把当前手与真实的锦标赛延续价值连接起来的策略构造方法，以生成更合适的行动决策。

Method: 引入Strategic-Continuation Optimization（SCO）：对当前一手的所有可能结局进行枚举， 将每个结局映射为后继状态，利用有限锦标赛模型计算这些后继状态的延续价值（continuation values），以此为目标对当前手策略进行优化并固定。与之对照的fixed-ICM策略仅在使用ICM解析值来定价后继状态，其余求解流程完全相同。实验在三人jam/fold博弈和固定100万美元奖金池上比较两类策略，并在多种对手模型下复核鲁棒性。

Result: 在2,838个状态-座位条目上，分析ICM对延续价值的平均绝对误差为9,036美元；相对于fixed-ICM的jam频率，SCO在每个决策点平均改变14.08%的jam频率；使用SCO的策略平均每手多获得214.33美元的奖池期望，并在2,433/2,838的配对单元中被偏好。该排序在用两种LLM与一族非建模阈值玩家替换对手时依然成立，说明结果具有一定的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文证明在有限支付锦标赛中，ICM因忽视行动顺序、盲注义务、座位轮转和大筹码对短筹码的淘汰压力，常常产生显著的价值定价误差，从而导致次优策略。提出的SCO方法通过枚举当前手牌结果、把它们映射到后继状态并用有限锦标赛模型计算延续价值来定价后继状态，进而优化并固定当前手策略，能纠正这些误差并提高奖池期望收益。

Abstract: The Independent Chip Model (ICM) converts tournament chips into reference prize equity, and policies are routinely constructed against those values. Because ICM reads only stack sizes, it omits action order, blind obligations, and seat rotation, and it does not price the elimination pressure a big stack puts on the short stacks it can bust. Those omissions can alter the successor-state contrasts that determine a move. We introduce Strategic-Continuation Optimization (SCO), a policy-construction method that enumerates current-hand outcomes, maps them to successor states, prices those states with continuation values computed from the finite tournament model, and optimizes and freezes the resulting current-hand policy. The fixed-ICM comparison policy changes one thing only: the same optimizer solves the same game with successor states priced by analytic ICM, so the two policies differ only through that pricing. We evaluate the resulting policies in a three-player jam/fold tournament with a \$1M prize pool. Relative to the frozen strategic-continuation benchmark, analytic ICM has \$9{,}036 mean absolute value error across all 2,838 state--seat entries. That value error rewrites the ranges it prices: measured against each decision point's own fixed-ICM jam range, SCO moves the jam frequency by an average of 14.08\%. To price those different moves, we compare all 946 states and three policy owners while changing only the focal policy and holding both opponents and the continuation evaluator fixed. The policy produced by SCO earns \$214.33 more prize equity per hand on average and is favored in 2,433 of 2,838 matched units. The ordering survives replacing the solver-built opponent with two LLMs and with a family of non-modeling threshold players. This value-to-policy-to-cost chain shows directly when ICM becomes an inadequate objective for tournament strategy construction.

</details>


### [233] [From Sweep to Seam: Interleaved Cross-Block Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09595)
*Achille Jacquemond,Yuma Ichikawa,Akira Sakai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Interleaved Cross-Block Quantization（ICBQ），通过在分块PTQ的滑动窗口中对分界对进行双重微调来减小误差传播，显著改善低位量化下的困惑度并在某些配置下挽救了崩溃的基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的顺序跨块PTQ在一次从前往后的扫描中对早期引入的误差不再复访，导致误差在深层网络中累积并引发低位量化下性能崩溃；ICBQ通过在块间缝隙处进行双重微调来抑制这种累积误差，从而改善极低比特率量化的鲁棒性与效果。

Method: 在固定两块的交叉块量化（CBQ）中，把滑动窗口的调度改为交错式：每个分块边界对（缝隙对）在前一块块序列结束时先进行一次微调，然后在下一块开始时再精调一次；保留局部两块目标并复用已有的标定输入，理论上在局部收缩和平滑条件下分析了缝隙重访对误差传播项和残差项的影响。

Result: ICBQ improves block-wise post-training quantization by revisiting boundary block pairs, reducing propagated error and improving perplexity especially for low-bit (ternary) settings; compatible with 3- and 2-bit GPTQ.

Conclusion: ICBQ在保持局部两块优化目标和复用标定数据的前提下，通过对相邻块的缝隙对进行双重微调，数学上在局部收缩和平滑假设下给出深度无关的残差界，从而减少随网络深度累积的误差。实验表明其在三值量化下降低困惑度、在基线失败的配置中得到可用模型结果，并能与3/2位GPTQ配合使用。

Abstract: Compressing large language models to two bits or fewer is increasingly feasible through block-wise post-training quantization; cross-block variants reconstruct neighboring Transformer blocks within a moving window. In the fixed two-block setting studied here, the matched sequential baseline moves this window through the network once, so errors introduced early in the sweep are not revisited. We propose Interleaved Cross-Block Quantization (ICBQ), a scheduling modification that revisits the boundary pair between consecutive chunks. Each seam pair is refined twice: first at the end of one chunk and again at the start of the next. The method retains the local two-block objective and reuses the calibration inputs of existing block-wise PTQ pipelines. Under stated local contraction and smoothness assumptions, we derive a depth-wise upper-bound comparison in which seam revisits multiply the propagated term while the residual remains bounded independently of depth. In the reported experiments, ICBQ reduces ternary-quantization perplexity relative to the matched Sequential CBQ baseline, yields finite perplexity in configurations where the baseline has severe degradation, and can also be used with 3-bit and 2-bit GPTQ.

</details>


### [234] [Adaptive Sequential Test Planning for Multi-Mechanism Reliability Qualification via Bayesian Monte Carlo Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09622)
*Youssef A. Elhagrasy,Ian Hill,André Ivanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将可靠性鉴定构造成POMDP，采用MCTS-SA+EKF实现闭环自适应试验，在包含BTI/EM/TDDB的模拟中显著提高表征产率，但依赖若干简化假设（离散动作、代理可观测损伤、不可恢复累积损伤）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行先进半导体器件的可靠性鉴定依赖静态试验计划，这些计划基于群体层面的加速模型，无法适应单件器件的异质性和实时退化观测；因此需要一种能在试验过程中基于观测与不确定性自适应选择应力、在多种竞争失效机制下权衡的闭环方案。

Method: 将试验规划建模为POMDP，使用EKF对器件损伤与参数做在线信念更新，利用MCTS-SA在种子-动作模拟器上搜索最优应力序列，目标是最大化成功表征概率同时满足灾难性失效约束。实验设置包括离散应力动作、代理损伤可观测性、不可恢复的累积损伤模型，并在仿真中运行5,000次规划迭代评估CY与损伤终止点。

Result: 提出了一种将可靠性鉴定表述为部分可观测序贯决策问题的闭环自适应试验规划框架，使用用于种子-动作模拟器的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS-SA）配合扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）进行信念态估计。模拟了BTI、EM、TDDB的器件间随机性，并将应力选择视为有灾难性失效约束的序贯优化。实验（5,000次规划迭代）中，表征产率（CY）由前500次的20%提升至后500次的>54%，累计成功39%，最佳成功序列在安全裕度内终止（EM损伤0.564，TDDB损伤0.537）。结论是序贯贝叶斯规划能生成比非自适应策略显著更好的损伤感知试验策略。

Conclusion: 在所做假设下，基于树搜索的序贯贝叶斯试验规划可有效提高多机制竞争性失效情形下的鉴定成功率；但需要在更真实的传感与损伤动力学、连续动作空间与鲁棒性方面进行后续验证与扩展。

Abstract: Reliability qualification of advanced semiconductor devices requires sequential stress decisions that balance characterization objectives against multiple competing failure mechanisms. Current practice relies on static test plans derived from population-level acceleration models, which cannot adapt to per-unit variability or real-time degradation observations. This paper presents a closed-loop adaptive test planning framework that formulates reliability qualification as a partially observable sequential decision problem and solves it using Monte Carlo tree search for seed-action simulators (MCTS-SA) coupled with extended Kalman filter (EKF) belief-state estimation. The framework models stochastic, per-device variability in bias temperature instability (BTI), electromigration (EM), and time-dependent dielectric breakdown (TDDB), and treats stress selection as a constrained sequential optimization, i.e., to maximize the probability of successful degradation characterization while respecting catastrophic failure constraints. Under the experimental assumptions used here (discrete stress actions, proxy damage observability, and cumulative degradation without recovery), we believe this to be a novel application of tree-search-based adaptive test planning to multi-mechanism reliability qualification. Across 5,000 planning iterations, the characterization yield (CY) improves from 20% in the first 500 iterations to over 54% in the final 500, with 39% cumulative success, while the best successful test sequence terminates with EM and TDDB damage fractions DEM=0.564 and DTDDB=0.537, well within safety margins. These results demonstrate that sequential Bayesian planning can synthesize damage-aware test policies that significantly outperform non-adaptive strategies for reliability qualification under competing failure modes.

</details>


### [235] [Rethinking Self-Evolving Agents: Do We Still Need Prescribed Optimization Pipelines?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09629)
*Hui Xue,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OEO lets a capable model self-compose optimization steps and outperforms prescribed pipelines in most settings, but its success depends on optimizer capability and prescription still shapes the optimization trajectory.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To test whether prescribed, task-specific optimization pipelines are necessary when a sufficiently capable model can act as a general-purpose optimizer that composes improvement strategies online.

Method: Introduce Open-Ended Optimization (OEO) where a frontier model composes the optimization process online while keeping objective, interactions, budget, data, and evaluation fixed; compare OEO to two prescribed pipelines (SkillOpt staged edits and GEPA reflective evolutionary search) across benchmarks; conduct head-to-head comparisons using GPT-5.5 as optimizer and perform trajectory analysis.

Result: GPT-5.5-driven OEO won 12 of 14 comparisons, tied 1, narrowly lost 1; used median 34.3% of SkillOpt's token budget; one-shot control showed gains not due to single prior rewrites; prescription still matters with weaker optimizers—SkillOpt beats OEO with medium optimizer; trajectory analysis indicates prescription affects optimization dynamics more than final outcomes.

Conclusion: Prescribed pipelines act as capability-dependent scaffolding: they are less necessary with a sufficiently capable optimizer which can autonomously map feedback to persistent improvements, but external constraints and prescription remain important when optimizer capability is limited.

Abstract: Self-evolving agents are usually built around prescribed optimization pipelines: the framework decides how to gather evidence, revise a persistent artifact, select candidates, and stop. We ask whether this task-specific procedure remains necessary when a frontier model acts as the optimizer. We introduce Open-Ended Optimization (OEO), which keeps the objective, permitted interactions, resource budget, data boundary, and evaluation fixed while allowing the optimizer to compose the improvement process online. We compare OEO with two complementary prescribed approaches: SkillOpt, a staged pipeline with bounded edits, and GEPA, a reflective evolutionary search. Across 14 head-to-head comparisons over 8 benchmark-target-model settings, GPT-5.5-driven OEO records 12 wins, 1 tie, and 1 narrow loss of 0.21 percentage points. It uses a median 34.3 percent of SkillOpt's configured target-interaction token budget. A one-shot, zero-interaction control shows that the gains are not explained by a single prior-driven rewrite. However, delegation has a capability boundary: SkillOpt outperforms OEO with a medium optimizer, and a weak optimizer cannot operate through the unchanged OEO interface. In the fully instrumented OEO-SkillOpt pair, trajectory analysis further shows that prescription changes how optimization proceeds more consistently than it changes final behavior. Together, these findings recast prescribed pipelines as capability-dependent scaffolding: essential constraints remain external, but a sufficiently capable optimizer can compose the route from measurable feedback to persistent improvement.

</details>


### [236] [Avalon-ToM-Bench: Evaluating Fine-Grained Theory of Mind via Asymmetric Game Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09638)
*Yen-Shan Chen,Yu Chian Duan,Chih-En Kuo,Jian-Bin Wu,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了 Avalon-ToM-Bench：基于桌游《阿瓦隆》的细粒度理论心智（ToM）评测，构建了一个将 ToM 分解为（认知/动机）×（推断/行动）的 2×2 诊断框架，并用人类设计的视角受限查询评估了 28 个大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ToM 评测要么基于静态情境过于简化，要么基于互动游戏缺乏可诊断的细粒度指标；因此需要一个既有现实感的非对称信息场景又能细分能力维度的基准来精确测量与定位模型社会推理缺陷。

Method: 用《阿瓦隆》的非对称信息机制构造任务，将 ToM 拆解为四类子能力，通过人类设计的视角受限问题评估模型在推断与行动两个维度的认知/动机推理能力；对 28 个 LLM 进行基线评估，并通过线性探针与 activation steering 对内部表征进行机械分析，还比较了训练时与测试时的链式思考影响。

Result: 实验显示：模型在规则知识上表现良好但 ToM 任务成绩明显落后；线性探针能从隐藏层恢复出比模型生成更高的正确率（77–82% vs 62–70%）；专门的推理训练带来明显收益，而仅靠测试时的链式思考增益有限（平均+11.0 vs +1.1 分）。

Conclusion: 主要结论为：模型掌握规则但在社会推理上薄弱；模型隐藏状态常有正确的心理状态表征但未在生成中表达；训练得到的推理策略比测试时的链式思考更能提升 ToM 能力。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM) is essential for agent interactions, yet existing evaluations either rely on static scenarios that oversimplify mental-state reasoning or interactive settings that provide limited diagnostic insight. We present Avalon-ToM-Bench, a fine-grained benchmark that operationalizes ToM through the asymmetric-information mechanics of The Resistance: Avalon. Rather than evaluating end-to-end gameplay, it decomposes ToM into a 2$\times$2 taxonomy -- epistemic versus motivational reasoning crossed with inference versus action -- using human-crafted, perspective-constrained queries. Benchmarking 28 LLMs reveals three insights: 1) Reasoning, not knowledge. Models show strong game-rule comprehension but markedly weaker ToM abilities, isolating failures to social reasoning rather than missing domain knowledge. 2) Expression, not representation. Mechanistic analyses via linear probing and activation steering show that models frequently represent correct mental-state inferences in their hidden states but fail to express them during generation -- linear probes recover 77-82% accuracy versus 62-70% from the models' own chain-of-thought. 3) Policy, not deliberation. Dedicated reasoning training yields substantial improvements whereas test-time chain-of-thought provides only marginal gains (+11.0 versus +1.1 points on average), suggesting that robust ToM depends on a learned reasoning policy rather than increased inference-time deliberation.

</details>


### [237] [Hallucination-Free GUI Grounding via Regression-Free Layout-Aware Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09654)
*Yuke Li,Xuehan Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A regression-free pipeline where a frozen MLLM generates structured visual descriptions and a layout-aware model matches these to candidate GUI elements achieves large gains in grounding by avoiding coordinate regression and reducing hallucinations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing GUI grounding either uses conventional models that lack semantic interpretation for abstract instructions, or end-to-end MLLMs that hallucinate coordinates due to poor fine-grained perception. Decoupling aims to combine strong semantic understanding with precise, hallucination-resistant localization.

Method: A frozen multimodal LLM parses an abstract instruction into a structured visual description with layout cues. A Layout-Aware GUI Grounding Model then performs localization without coordinate regression by matching the description to candidate elements derived from layout priors; training uses only Text/Icon binary labels.

Result: Significant empirical gains: >20% improvement in grounding accuracy on ScreenSpot-Pro; >15% increases in success rate and element selection rate on Mind2Web versus end-to-end systems, demonstrating effectiveness of the regression-free decoupled pipeline.

Conclusion: Decoupling semantic parsing and localization — using a frozen MLLM to expand abstract instructions into structured visual descriptions and a separate regression-free, layout-aware grounding model that matches against layout-prior candidates — substantially reduces coordinate hallucination and improves grounding accuracy.

Abstract: GUI agents are shifting from metadata-dependent large language models to purely visual multimodal large language models (MLLMs) that operate directly on screenshots. The core task, GUI grounding, requires translating abstract user instructions into precise element coordinates. This task faces a persistent dual obstacle: conventional grounding models lack the semantic richness to interpret abstract instructions, while end-to-end MLLMs suffer from coordinate hallucinations caused by deficient fine-grained perception. We propose a regression-free framework where a frozen MLLM performs instruction parsing and a dedicated grounding model handles precise localization without learning any coordinate regression. A frozen MLLM first elaborates the abstract instruction into a structured visual description rich in layout cues. These descriptions are then fed to a novel Layout-Aware GUI Grounding Model, which performs regression-free localization by matching against layout-prior candidates, inherently suppressing hallucinations and avoiding expensive fine-tuning. The grounding model is trained with only Text/Icon binary labels, requiring no coordinate regression parameters. On ScreenSpot-Pro, our method achieves over 20% improvement in grounding accuracy over end-to-end systems; on Mind2Web, it raises success rate and element selection rate by more than 15%. These results demonstrate that decoupling instruction understanding from layout-aware localization effectively resolves the core challenges of GUI interaction.

</details>


### [238] [Open Evaluation Agent: Efficient and Promptable Evaluation of Visual Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09666)
*Shulin Tian,Ziqi Huang,Fan Zhang,Hongyuan Zhu,Yu Qiao,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Evaluation Agent框架：以类人、多轮、动态的策略对视觉生成模型进行高效、可解释、可定制的评估；通过分解自然语言评估请求、生成针对性提示、采样样本、调用评估工具并迭代计划，实现比传统方法约10%时间开销并保持可比结果；并构建EA-CoT-10K语料与训练局部规划模型EA-3B（基于Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct），在若干T2I/T2V基准与生成器族上验证效果及部分跨族迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成模型评估既昂贵（需大量采样），又流程僵化、缺乏面向用户的可解释分析；灵感来自人类仅凭少量样本快速形成模型印象的能力，旨在实现更高效、灵活和可解释的评估流程。

Method: 从自然语言评估请求出发，自动分解为子维度；为每个子维度生成针对性提示并采样模型输出（图像/视频）；调用合适的评估工具对样本打分或做定性判断；根据观测证据迭代更新评估计划，融合预定义基准维度与开放性用户关切。构建EA-CoT-10K的历史条件、步骤级指令微调语料，并以Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct微调出EA-3B作为本地规划器，评测包括既有T2I/T2V基准与开放式查询，以及四个域内与三个域外的T2V生成器族。

Result: 实验表明Evaluation Agent将评估时间缩短到传统方法约10%且结果可比；API代理在既有基准与开放式查询上得到验证；Open-EA在四个域内与三个域外T2V生成器上展示部分跨族策略迁移能力，证明了通过EA-CoT-10K训练的本地规划器在结构化推理与工具调用协议上能保留关键行为。

Conclusion: Evaluation Agent能在大幅降低计算成本的同时提供可解释、可提示和面向用户需求的评估方案；Open-EA（EA-3B）作为本地规划骨干能继承API代理的结构化推理与工具调用协议，并在若干生成器上实现有限的迁移能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in visual generative models have enabled high-quality image and video generation, but evaluating these models often demands sampling hundreds or thousands of images or videos, which is computationally expensive. Existing evaluation methods also rely on rigid pipelines that overlook specific user needs and provide numerical results without clear explanations. Mimicking how humans quickly form impressions of a model's capabilities from only a few samples, we propose the Evaluation Agent framework, which employs human-like strategies for efficient, dynamic, multi-round evaluations, offering detailed, user-tailored analyses. Given a natural-language evaluation request, the agent decomposes it into sub-aspects, generates targeted prompts, samples images or videos from the evaluated model, invokes suitable evaluation tools, and iteratively updates its plan from the observed evidence, covering both predefined benchmark dimensions and open-ended user concerns. The framework is thus efficient, promptable, explainable, and scalable across models and tools. Experiments show that Evaluation Agent reduces evaluation time to 10% of traditional methods while delivering comparable results. We further introduce Open Evaluation Agent (Open-EA) by constructing EA-CoT-10K, a corpus of history-conditioned step-level instruction-tuning records derived from multi-round evaluation rollouts, and training EA-3B from Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct as a local planning backbone that preserves the structured reasoning, tool invocation, and summary protocol of the API-based agent while reducing dependence on proprietary backbones. Experiments validate the API-based agent on established T2I/T2V benchmarks and open-ended queries, and evaluate Open-EA on four in-domain and three out-of-domain T2V generator families, showing partial cross-family transfer of the learned policy.

</details>


### [239] [Adaptive Semantic Capacity Allocation for Parallel Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09685)
*Chenxi Li,Yuchen Lu,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出用容量预算自适应分配各语义槽与码本规模的框架（InforID），在并行语义 ID 生成场景下提升推荐效果并保持单步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归语义 ID 受限于昂贵的束搜索，限制了 ID 长度；并行生成能扩展长度但现有方法仍采用手动设定且同质的槽结构，忽视不同语义子空间的容量需求，导致资源分配低效。

Method: 提出一个轻量级的自适应语义目标构建框架（InforID），在候选语义槽集合上分配有限的容量预算，联合决定最终语义 ID 的长度和每个槽的码本规模，以支持并行生成的推荐系统。

Result: 实验证明，在相近的容量预算下，InforID 比均质语义 ID 得到更高的推荐准确率，同时保持一步并行预测，不增加推理步数。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 InforID 在保持单步并行预测的前提下，通过按候选语义槽分配固定容量预算来自适应确定有效 ID 长度和各槽的码本大小，从而在可比较容量预算下提升推荐准确率，表明向非均质语义子空间分配容量优于均匀扩展槽位。

Abstract: Autoregressive semantic ID recommenders are constrained by expensive beam-search decoding, which limits the practical length of item identifiers. Parallel generation methods alleviate this bottleneck by predicting all semantic ID tokens simultaneously, enabling longer IDs. However, existing semantic ID methods still rely on manually predefined and homogeneous ID structures, where both the number of semantic slots and the codebook size of each slot are treated as fixed hyperparameters. This ignores the heterogeneous capacity demands of different semantic subspaces and may allocate prediction capacity to slots with limited utility. We show that uniformly expanding semantic slots can provide limited gains, indicating redundant capacity in homogeneous semantic IDs. We propose InforID, a lightweight adaptive semantic target construction framework for parallel generative recommendation. InforID allocates a fixed capacity budget across candidate semantic slots, thereby jointly determining the effective ID length and slot-specific codebook sizes. Experiments demonstrate improved recommendation accuracy under comparable capacity budgets while preserving one-step parallel prediction.

</details>


### [240] [Model Discovery Agent: LLM-assisted Bayesian experiment design for data-efficient discovery of mechanistic world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09696)
*Kevin Murphy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将LLM提案与贝叶斯后验推断和主动实验设计耦合，能用少量干预高效发现可用于外推干预预测的机械因果模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答干预型“what-if”问题需要机械式因果模型，且这类模型的辨识依赖主动干预实验，因而核心问题是如何用尽量少的实验数据完成可靠的模型发现与因果预测。

Method: 用LLM生成候选结构（proposer）；用顺序蒙特卡洛（SMC）估计参数与结构后验；对不可解似然采用模拟基础推断（SBI）；用信息价值（VoI）驱动实验设计；在M-open场景下通过预测检验识别模型不足并由LLM扩展假设空间，形成发现→设计→识别的迭代循环。

Result: 在物理(DPbench)、化学(CHEMbench)与生物(HHbench)三套基准上，MDA在数据效率和干预预测可靠性上优于现有方法，并且演示了发现与设计相互增强的闭环机制。

Conclusion: MDA结合大型语言模型作为模型提案器与贝叶斯推断和主动实验设计，实现了从少量干预中高效发现机械式因果模型，并在多个跨学科基准上取得数据效率和干预预测能力的SOTA。

Abstract: Predicting the answer to interventional ``what if'' questions --- the outcome of an action never taken --- requires a \emph{mechanistic}, causal model, not a curve fit; and learning such a model requires \emph{experiments}, because passive data leaves its mechanisms unidentified. Experiments are expensive, so the central problem is \emph{data efficiency}. We present the Model Discovery Agent (MDA), which couples a large language model (LLM), used as a \emph{proposer} of candidate structures, with standard Bayesian machinery --- sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) for parameter and structure posteriors, simulation-based inference (SBI) for intractable likelihoods, and value-of-information (VoI) for experiment design --- to discover latent mechanistic world models from few interventions. MDA operates in the M-open setting: when the truth lies outside the current hypothesis class, a predictive check flags the inadequacy and the proposer expands the hypothesis space with a new model whose parameters are then identified by designed experiments. We show that \emph{discovery and design reinforce}: the design step identifies the mechanism the discovery step proposes, and the identified mechanism improves predictions, enabling further discoveries from the remaining unexplained residuals. On three different benchmarks --- covering physics (\DPbench, \citep{wiemann2026discoverphysics}), chemistry (\CHEMbench, \citep{kabra2026autoscilab}) and biology (\HHbench, a new partially observed single-neuron electrophysiology benchmark we create) --- we show that MDA sets a new SOTA in terms of data-efficient model learning and reliable interventional forecasting ability.

</details>


### [241] [Matryoshka Language Model Suites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09703)
*Nathan Godey,Yoav Artzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将不同规模子模型嵌套到单一端到端训练架构（Matryoshka），在训练过程中进行低成本蒸馏并共享参数，从而减少训练计算量并提升推理（尤其是可猜测解码）的吞吐量。作者在500M/1.5B/3B子模型套件上验证，保持与独立训练基线相当的性能，同时将训练算力降低36%，可猜测解码吞吐量提高14–26%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立训练与部署多个规模模型既费算力又造成资源浪费。通过共享与联合训练，可减少总参数与训练成本，并在推理时利用嵌套结构加速可猜测解码。

Method: 将多个递增参数量的子模型按“套娃”方式嵌入同一网络，共享若干参数并在每个训练步对大模型向小模型进行低成本蒸馏（跨尺度蒸馏），端到端训练得到同时可用的多尺度模型；该架构也天然支持作为猜测—验证（speculative decoding）中的草稿/验证器配对。

Result: 在500M/1.5B/3B套件上，Matryoshka 与独立训练基线在基准任务、验证和域外困惑度上相当；训练算力减少约36%；在可猜测解码场景中吞吐量提升14–26%；并进行了关键架构选择的消融分析以提供构建指南。

Conclusion: Matryoshka 框架在所测规模上能够在不损失质量的前提下，减少训练算力并提升基于嵌套模型的推理效率，是一种实用的模型套件训练策略，但需要更多对大规模适应性和潜在权衡的验证。

Abstract: Training a language model suite classically requires training each model separately and serving them independently. We improve both training and inference efficiency by stacking sub-models of increasing size into a single nested architecture trained end-to-end. This Matryoshka training framework reduces the total parameter count of the suite, enables low-cost distillation from the largest to all smaller sub-models at every training step, and is well-suited for speculative decoding as the draft model is contained within the verifier. We validate our approach by training a Matryoshka suite comprising 500M, 1.5B, and 3B sub-models. Our suite is on par with independently trained baselines on benchmark performance and validation and out-of-domain perplexities, while using 36% less training compute and improving the throughput of speculative decoding by 14-26%. We also ablate key architectural choices, offering guidance for building strong Matryoshka LM suites.

</details>


### [242] [Second-Order Muon Done Right: A Principled Marriage of Spectral Geometry and Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09763)
*Tong Che*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GO-MUON reuses data-dependent PD left/right maps to perform an exact polar update for weighted spectral geometry; this exactness holds regardless of how or when maps were estimated. For softmax CE, the observed-label backward factor can be related to Fisher/GGN; infrequent (four-step) refreshes keep tracking delay low but raise stationary noise, making laziness a cost–benefit tradeoff.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reduce computational cost of spectral (polar) updates by reusing a matched, data-dependent geometry across multiple optimization steps, while retaining exactness with respect to a weighted spectral oracle.

Method: Define polar (spectral) update in a weighted geometry parameterized by positive-definite left and right maps. Construct GO-MUON algorithm that reuses a matched data-dependent geometry across multiple steps. Prove algebraically that the raw update exactly solves the weighted spectral oracle conditioned on arbitrary PD maps (independent of estimation or staleness). Analyze softmax cross-entropy to bound/quantify the difference between observed-label backward factor and model Fisher/GGN. Analyze effect of refresh frequency (four-step refresh) on tracking delay and stationary factor noise, yielding concrete tradeoffs.

Result: Introduces GO-MUON whose raw (polar) update exactly solves the weighted spectral oracle for any positive-definite left/right maps; quantifies when the observed-label backward factor approaches the model Fisher and generalized Gauss--Newton (GGN) for softmax cross-entropy; proves that four-step refresh largely preserves tracking delay for slowly changing geometries but increases stationary factor noise, reframing lazy geometry as a compute–statistics tradeoff.

Conclusion: GO-MUON provides a principled method to reuse matched geometries across steps while preserving exact oracle solutions; lazily refreshed geometry is not merely a denoiser but a deliberate compute/statistics tradeoff. The theory clarifies when backward factors approximate Fisher/GGN; further empirical validation and specification of map-estimation procedures are necessary.

Abstract: Muon's polar update is exact for an unweighted spectral geometry. We introduce GO-MUON, which uses a matched data-dependent geometry and reuses it across several optimization steps.
